## Exploration Report: AgentCenter Nuxt 4 Project

### 1. **Page/Route Structure**

**Locale-Prefixed Routing:** The project uses `@nuxtjs/i18n` with `strategy: 'prefix'`, creating routes like:
- `/en/extensions` and `/zh/extensions`
- `/en/publish` and `/zh/publish`
- `/en/profile` and `/zh/profile`

**Page files** (`/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/`):
```
index.vue                    # Landing page (trending)
sign-in.vue, sign-up.vue     # Auth pages
profile.vue                  # My workspace (requires-auth)
onboard.vue                  # Department selection
publish/
  index.vue                  # Publisher dashboard (list drafts)
  new.vue                    # New extension form
  [id]/edit.vue              # Resume draft
extensions/
  index.vue                  # Browse/filter all extensions
  [slug].vue                 # Detail page
cli/
  auth.vue                   # CLI auth code entry
```

**Example page pattern** (`app/pages/index.vue`):
```typescript
const { t } = useI18n()                              // i18n: t() for translations
const localePath = useLocalePath()                  // i18n: for locale-prefixed links
const { data } = await useFetch("/api/internal/extensions", { ... })
defineOgImageComponent("Frame", { title: t("home.featuredLabel") })
```

---

### 2. **Component Organization**

**Component directories** (`app/components/`):

| Folder | Contents | Key Components |
|--------|----------|-----------------|
| **ui/** (30 files) | shadcn-vue components | Button, Input, Dialog, Select, Tabs, Popover, Sheet, Checkbox, Label, Textarea, Skeleton |
| **extension/** (9 files) | Extension display | `ExtCard.vue`, `ExtGrid.vue` (grid layout), `ExtHero.vue`, `ExtTabs.vue`, `InstallButton.vue`, `SaveButton.vue` |
| **filters/** (8 files) | Browse filters | `FilterBar.vue`, `DeptPicker.vue` (hierarchical), `TagDrawer.vue`, `SortSelect.vue`, `ScopePills.vue`, `CreatorPicker.vue`, `PublisherPicker.vue` |
| **publish/** (12 files) | Publish wizard | `UploadWizard.vue`, `WizardBasics.vue`, `WizardBundle.vue`, `WizardListing.vue`, `WizardReview.vue`, `WizardSuccess.vue`, `DashboardSkeleton.vue` |
| **profile/** (9 files) | User workspace | `SectionInstalled.vue`, `SectionPublished.vue`, `SectionDrafts.vue`, `SectionActivity.vue`, `SectionSaved.vue`, `ProfileHero.vue`, `ProfileSettingsForm.vue` |
| **layout/** (5 files) | App chrome | `TopBar.vue`, `Sidebar.vue`, `UserButton.vue`, `ThemeSwitch.vue`, `LocaleSwitch.vue` |

---

### 3. **i18n Setup**

**Config** (`nuxt.config.ts`):
```typescript
i18n: {
  strategy: "prefix",                           // Always prefix URLs
  defaultLocale: "en",
  locales: [
    { code: "en", language: "en-US", file: "en.json" },
    { code: "zh", language: "zh-CN", file: "zh.json" },
  ],
  detectBrowserLanguage: { useCookie: true },
}
```

**Locale files** (`i18n/locales/`):
- `en.json` and `zh.json` (3-level nested JSON with 455+ keys)

**Page usage** (`app/pages/index.vue`):
```typescript
const { t } = useI18n()
const { locale } = useI18n()  // "en" | "zh"

// In template:
{{ t("home.featuredDescription") }}
{{ locale === "zh" && ext.nameZh ? ext.nameZh : ext.name }}
```

**DB columns**: Bilingual content stored as separate columns:
- `name` / `nameZh`
- `description` / `descriptionZh`
- `tagline` / `taglineZh`
- Tags table: `labelEn` / `labelZh`

---

**Tailwind config** (`app/assets/css/tailwind.css`):
- **Theme**: "Editorial Ivory" (default) + Dark mode
- **CSS variables** (oklch color space):
  ```css
  --color-bg: oklch(98.5% 0.008 80)
  --color-card: #ffffff

**Fonts** (`nuxt.config.ts`):
- **Inter** (UI, sans-serif)

**Badge classes** (`.badge-official`, `.badge-popular`, `.badge-new`):

### 7. **Extension Data Model (Drizzle Schema)**

**Key fields** (`shared/db/schema/extension.ts`):

```typescript
extensions: {
  id: text().primaryKey(),
  slug: text().unique(),
  category: extensionCategoryEnum("skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"),
  scope: extensionScopeEnum("personal", "org", "enterprise"),
  badge: extensionBadgeEnum("official", "popular", "new"),
  
  // Taxonomy (nullable — admin-curated)
  funcCat: funcCatEnum("workTask", "business", "tools"),
  subCat: text(),
  l2: text(),
  
  // Organization & department
  publisherUserId: text,
  ownerOrgId: text (FK → organizations),
  deptId: text (FK → departments),
  
  // Bilingual metadata
  name: text, nameZh: text,
  tagline: text, taglineZh: text,
  description: text, descriptionZh: text,
  readmeMd: text,  // Long-form, English only
  
  // Detail page
  homepageUrl, repoUrl, licenseSpdx, compatibilityJson, permissions,
  
  // Denormalized counters (updated by background jobs)
  downloadsCount: integer,
  starsAvg: numeric,
  ratingsCount: integer,
  
  // Timestamps
  publishedAt, createdAt, updatedAt,
  searchVector: tsvector (Postgres FTS, read-only)
}

extensionTags: {
  extensionId (FK → extensions),
  tagId (FK → tags)
}

tags: {
  id: text (primary key, e.g., "real-time"),
  labelEn: text,
  labelZh: text
}
```

**No MCP-specific type yet**, but `category: "mcp"` and `extensionCategoryEnum` provide the hook. Extensions store metadata in `compatibilityJson` and `manifestJson` (in versions table).

---

You have a solid foundation:

- ✓ Filter/facet infrastructure (DeptPicker, TagDrawer, etc.)

- **i18n** — click the 🌐 button top-right to switch between English and 中文 (all labels, descriptions, and categories translate)
- **3 visual themes via Tweaks** — open the Tweaks panel (bottom-right toolbar toggle) to switch between:

  - **Slate Dark** — full dark mode with teal accent
  - **Mono Clean** — black & white minimal
- **Density + card radius** also tweakable for layout exploration

6	
7	## User
8	
9	Since we are developing an internal extension marketplace for the enterprise, we need a Kanban-style dashboard to visualize the MCP (Model Context Protocol) adoption status of our internal tools. This will help drive the transition to MCP and enable employees to quickly locate the MCP versions of traditional tools. Internal tool services are categorized into two layers: Industry Services and Public Services. Industry Services include over a dozen sectors such as Wireless, Datacom, Cloud, and Terminals. Each sector contains multiple tool services, ranging from a few to several dozen. Public Services are divided into several domains: AI R&D, Product & Software, Hardware, Product Digitization, and Infrastructure. Each domain consists of several PDTs (Product Development Teams). The AI R&D Domain features six distinct PDTs: System Design, Development Services, Testing Services, R&D Maintenance, AI Production Line, and Knowledge Services. The Product & Software Domain comprises eight PDTs, including Research & Innovation, Product Management, and Build Services. Within each PDT, there are various tool services. For example, the Development Services PDT hosts tools such as IDE, CodeCheck, and DT.

14	  *, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

33	<body>
34	<div id="root"></div>
35	
36	<script type="text/babel" src="tweaks-panel.jsx"></script>
37	<script type="text/babel" src="mcp-kanban-data.jsx"></script>
38	<script type="text/babel" src="mcp-kanban-ui.jsx"></script>
39	<script type="text/babel" src="mcp-kanban-views.jsx"></script>
40	<script type="text/babel" src="mcp-kanban-panorama.jsx"></script>
41	
42	<script type="text/babel">
43	const { useState, useMemo, useEffect } = React;
44	
45	function App() {
46	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
47	
48	  const TWEAK_DEFAULTS = /*EDITMODE-BEGIN*/{
49	    "viewMode": "panorama",
50	    "panSort": "default",
51	    "panDensity": "normal",
52	    "accentLayer": true
53	  }/*EDITMODE-END*/;
54	  const [tweaks, setTweak] = useTweaks(TWEAK_DEFAULTS);
55	
56	  const [layer, setLayer] = useState("public");          // "industry" | "public"
57	  const [activePrimary, setActivePrimary] = useState(null);   // sector key | domain key
58	  const [activeSecondary, setActiveSecondary] = useState(null);// pdt key (public only)
59	  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("all"); // "all" | status key
60	  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
61	  const [activeTool, setActiveTool] = useState(null);
62	  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState({});            // domain key → bool
63	
64	  // When switching layers, reset drill-down
65	  useEffect(() => {
66	    setActivePrimary(null);
67	    setActiveSecondary(null);
68	  }, [layer]);
69	
70	  // Filtered tool list
71	  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
72	    let items = window.TOOLS.filter(x => {
73	      const p = window.ownerToPath(x.owner);
74	      return p.layer === layer;
75	    });
76	    if (activePrimary) items = items.filter(x => window.ownerToPath(x.owner).primary === activePrimary);
77	    if (activeSecondary) items = items.filter(x => window.ownerToPath(x.owner).secondary === activeSecondary);
78	    if (statusFilter !== "all") items = items.filter(x => x.status === statusFilter);
79	    if (search.trim()) {
80	      const q = search.toLowerCase();
81	      items = items.filter(x =>
82	        x.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
83	        x.blurb.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
84	        (x.tags || []).some(tg => tg.includes(q))
85	      );
86	    }
87	    return items;
88	  }, [layer, activePrimary, activeSecondary, statusFilter, search]);
89	
90	  // Aggregate counts for header
91	  const totals = useMemo(() => {
92	    const all = window.TOOLS.filter(x => window.ownerToPath(x.owner).layer === layer);
93	    const counts = {};
94	    window.STATUSES.forEach(s => counts[s.key] = all.filter(x => x.status === s.key).length);
95	    counts.total = all.length;
96	    return counts;
97	  }, [layer]);
98	
99	  // Visible counts (for KPI strip when filters applied)
100	  const visibleCounts = useMemo(() => {
101	    const counts = {};
102	    window.STATUSES.forEach(s => counts[s.key] = filtered.filter(x => x.status === s.key).length);
103	    counts.total = filtered.length;
104	    return counts;
105	  }, [filtered]);
106	
107	  const layerMeta = window.LAYERS.find(l => l.key === layer);
108	  const layerAccent = tweaks.accentLayer
109	    ? (layer === "industry" ? t.industry : t.public)
110	    : t.accent;
111	
112	  return (
113	    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100vh", overflow: "hidden" }}>
114	      {/* ─── Top bar ─── */}
115	      <TopBar
116	        search={search} setSearch={setSearch}
117	        totals={totals} layerAccent={layerAccent}
118	      />
119	
120	      <div style={{ display: "flex", flex: 1, overflow: "hidden", position: "relative" }}>
121	        {/* ─── Sidebar ─── */}
122	        <Sidebar
123	          layer={layer} setLayer={setLayer}
124	          activePrimary={activePrimary} setActivePrimary={setActivePrimary}
125	          activeSecondary={activeSecondary} setActiveSecondary={setActiveSecondary}
126	          expanded={expanded} setExpanded={setExpanded}
127	          totals={totals} layerAccent={layerAccent}
128	        />
129	
130	        {/* ─── Main content ─── */}
131	        <main style={{ flex: 1, overflow: "auto", background: t.bg }}>
132	          {/* Header strip */}
133	          <SectionHeader
134	            layer={layer} activePrimary={activePrimary} activeSecondary={activeSecondary}
135	            visibleCounts={visibleCounts} totals={totals}
136	            statusFilter={statusFilter} setStatusFilter={setStatusFilter}
137	            viewMode={tweaks.viewMode} setViewMode={(v) => setTweak("viewMode", v)}
138	            search={search} setSearch={setSearch}
139	            onClearDrill={() => { setActivePrimary(null); setActiveSecondary(null); }}
140	            layerAccent={layerAccent}
141	          />
142	
143	          {/* View */}
144	          {tweaks.viewMode === "panorama" && (
145	            <window.PanoramaView
146	              tools={filtered} onPick={setActiveTool} activeId={activeTool?.id}
147	              layer={layer} sort={tweaks.panSort} density={tweaks.panDensity}
148	            />
149	          )}
150	          {tweaks.viewMode === "kanban" && (
151	            <window.KanbanView tools={filtered} onPick={setActiveTool} activeId={activeTool?.id}/>
152	          )}
153	          {tweaks.viewMode === "list" && (
154	            <window.GroupedListView tools={filtered} onPick={setActiveTool} activeId={activeTool?.id} layer={layer}/>
155	          )}
156	          {tweaks.viewMode === "heatmap" && (
157	            <window.HeatmapView tools={filtered} onPick={setActiveTool} activeId={activeTool?.id} layer={layer}/>
158	          )}
159	        </main>
160	
161	        {/* ─── Detail side panel ─── */}
162	        <window.ToolDetailPanel tool={activeTool} onClose={() => setActiveTool(null)}/>
163	      </div>
164	
165	      {/* ─── Tweaks panel ─── */}
166	      <TweaksPanel title="Tweaks">
167	        <TweakSection title="View">
168	          <TweakSelect
169	            label="Layout"
170	            value={tweaks.viewMode}
171	            onChange={(v) => setTweak("viewMode", v)}
172	            options={[
173	              { value: "panorama", label: "Panorama (landscape map)" },
174	              { value: "kanban",   label: "Kanban (3 columns)" },
175	              { value: "list",     label: "Grouped list" },
176	              { value: "heatmap",  label: "Heatmap grid" },
177	            ]}
178	          />
179	          <TweakToggle
180	            label="Layer-tinted accent"
181	            value={tweaks.accentLayer}
182	            onChange={(v) => setTweak("accentLayer", v)}
183	          />
184	        </TweakSection>
185	        {tweaks.viewMode === "panorama" && (
186	          <TweakSection title="Panorama">
187	            <TweakSelect
188	              label="Sort cards"
189	              value={tweaks.panSort}
190	              onChange={(v) => setTweak("panSort", v)}
191	              options={[
192	                { value: "default",  label: "Default order" },
193	                { value: "coverage", label: "Coverage (best first)" },
194	                { value: "lagging",  label: "Lagging first" },
195	                { value: "size",     label: "Tool count" },
196	              ]}
197	            />
198	            <TweakRadio
199	              label="Tile density"
200	              value={tweaks.panDensity}
201	              onChange={(v) => setTweak("panDensity", v)}
202	              options={[
203	                { value: "normal", label: "Normal" },
204	                { value: "dense",  label: "Dense" },
205	              ]}
206	            />
207	          </TweakSection>
208	        )}
209	      </TweaksPanel>
210	    </div>
211	  );
212	}

214	// ─── Top bar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
215	function TopBar({ search, setSearch, totals, layerAccent }) {

218	    <header
219	      data-screen-label="Top bar"
220	      style={{
221	        display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 14,
222	        padding: "0 20px", height: 56, flexShrink: 0,
223	        background: t.topBg, borderBottom: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
224	        backdropFilter: "blur(20px) saturate(180%)",
225	        WebkitBackdropFilter: "blur(20px) saturate(180%)",
226	        zIndex: 20, position: "relative",
227	      }}

229	      {/* Logo - clickable, links back to AgentCenter */}

231	        href="AgentCenter.html"
232	        title="Back to AgentCenter"

236	          width: 28, height: 28, borderRadius: "50%", background: t.accent,
237	          display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", flexShrink: 0,
238	        }}>
239	          <span className="serif" style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 500, color: t.accentFg, fontStyle: "italic" }}>A</span>
240	        </div>
241	        <span className="serif" style={{ fontWeight: 500, fontSize: 19, color: t.text, letterSpacing: "-0.02em" }}>
242	          Agent<span style={{ fontStyle: "italic", fontWeight: 400, color: t.accent }}>Center</span>
243	        </span>
244	      </a>

248	        fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",

251	        display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
252	      }}>

263	          value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}

268	            borderRadius: 8, color: t.text, fontSize: 13,

273	            e.target.style.boxShadow = `0 0 0 3px ${t.accent}18`;
274	          }}
275	          onBlur={e => {

277	            e.target.style.boxShadow = "none";
278	          }}
279	        />
280	      </div>
281	
282	      {/* Main nav (matches AgentCenter) */}
283	      <nav style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 2, marginLeft: "auto" }}>

286	          { label: "MCP Adoption", href: "#",                     active: true  },
287	          { label: "Publish",    href: "AgentCenter.html#publish",active: false },
288	          { label: "Docs",       href: "#",                       active: false },
289	        ].map(item => (
290	          <a key={item.label} href={item.href} style={{
291	            padding: "7px 12px", borderRadius: 7,
292	            fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500,
293	            textDecoration: "none",
294	            background: item.active ? `${t.accent}14` : "transparent",

296	            transition: "background 0.14s",
297	          }}
298	          onMouseEnter={e => { if (!item.active) e.currentTarget.style.background = t.surfaceAlt; }}
299	          onMouseLeave={e => { if (!item.active) e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"; }}>
300	            {item.label}
301	          </a>
302	        ))}

308	          cursor: "pointer", padding: 0,

317	// ─── Sidebar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
318	function Sidebar({
319	  layer, setLayer, activePrimary, setActivePrimary,
320	  activeSecondary, setActiveSecondary,
321	  expanded, setExpanded, totals, layerAccent,
322	}) {
323	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
324	
325	  const toggleDomain = (k) => setExpanded(prev => ({ ...prev, [k]: !prev[k] }));
326	
327	  return (
328	    <nav
329	      data-screen-label="Sidebar"
330	      style={{
331	        width: 268, flexShrink: 0, background: t.surface,
332	        borderRight: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
333	        display: "flex", flexDirection: "column",
334	        overflow: "hidden",
335	      }}
336	    >
337	      {/* Layer toggle */}
338	      <div style={{ padding: "16px 16px 12px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
339	        <div style={{
340	          fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted,
341	          letterSpacing: "0.08em", textTransform: "uppercase", marginBottom: 8,
342	        }}>
343	          Service Layer
344	        </div>
345	        <div style={{
346	          display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 6,
347	          background: t.surfaceAlt, padding: 4, borderRadius: 8,
348	          border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
349	        }}>
350	          {window.LAYERS.map(l => {
351	            const active = l.key === layer;
352	            return (
353	              <button
354	                key={l.key}
355	                onClick={() => setLayer(l.key)}
356	                style={{
357	                  padding: "8px 10px", border: "none", borderRadius: 6,
358	                  cursor: "pointer",
359	                  background: active ? t.surface : "transparent",
360	                  color: active ? t.text : t.textMuted,
361	                  fontSize: 12, fontWeight: active ? 600 : 500,
362	                  boxShadow: active ? "0 1px 3px rgba(60,40,20,0.08)" : "none",
363	                  display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 2,
364	                  transition: "all 0.14s",
365	                }}
366	              >
367	                <span style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6 }}>
368	                  <window.MCPIcon name={l.key === "industry" ? "industry" : "public"} size={12} color={active ? (l.key === "industry" ? t.industry : t.public) : t.textMuted}/>
369	                  {l.label.split(" ")[0]}
370	                </span>
371	                <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: t.textFaint, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}>
372	                  {l.label.split(" ").slice(1).join(" ") || "Services"}
373	                </span>
374	              </button>
375	            );
376	          })}
377	        </div>
378	      </div>
379	
380	      {/* Tree */}
381	      <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: "auto", padding: "12px 8px" }}>
382	        <div style={{
383	          fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted,
384	          letterSpacing: "0.08em", textTransform: "uppercase", padding: "4px 10px 8px",
385	        }}>
386	          {layer === "industry" ? "Sectors" : "Domains / PDTs"}
387	        </div>
388	
389	        {/* All */}
390	        <SidebarRow
391	          label="All tools"
392	          count={totals.total}
393	          active={!activePrimary && !activeSecondary}
394	          accent={layerAccent}
395	          onClick={() => { setActivePrimary(null); setActiveSecondary(null); }}
396	        />
397	
398	        {layer === "industry" ? (
399	          window.INDUSTRY_SECTORS.map(sec => {
400	            const count = window.countToolsForOwner("industry", sec.key).length;
401	            if (count === 0) return null;
402	            const active = activePrimary === sec.key;
403	            return (
404	              <SidebarRow
405	                key={sec.key}
406	                label={sec.label}
407	                count={count}
408	                active={active}
409	                accent={layerAccent}
410	                onClick={() => {
411	                  setActivePrimary(active ? null : sec.key);
412	                  setActiveSecondary(null);
413	                }}
414	              />
415	            );
416	          })
417	        ) : (
418	          window.PUBLIC_DOMAINS.map(dom => {
419	            const totalC = window.countToolsForOwner("public", dom.key).length;
420	            const open = expanded[dom.key] ?? (activePrimary === dom.key);
421	            const domActive = activePrimary === dom.key && !activeSecondary;
422	            return (
423	              <div key={dom.key}>
424	                <SidebarRow
425	                  label={dom.label}
426	                  count={totalC}
427	                  active={domActive}
428	                  accent={layerAccent}
429	                  expandable
430	                  expanded={open}
431	                  onToggle={() => toggleDomain(dom.key)}
432	                  onClick={() => {
433	                    setActivePrimary(domActive ? null : dom.key);
434	                    setActiveSecondary(null);
435	                    if (!open) toggleDomain(dom.key);
436	                  }}
437	                />
438	                {open && dom.pdts.map(p => {
439	                  const c = window.countToolsForOwner("public", dom.key, p.key).length;
440	                  const isActive = activePrimary === dom.key && activeSecondary === p.key;
441	                  return (
442	                    <SidebarRow
443	                      key={p.key}
444	                      label={p.label}
445	                      count={c}
446	                      active={isActive}
447	                      accent={layerAccent}
448	                      indent
449	                      onClick={() => {
450	                        setActivePrimary(dom.key);
451	                        setActiveSecondary(isActive ? null : p.key);
452	                      }}
453	                    />
454	                  );
455	                })}
456	              </div>
457	            );
458	          })
459	        )}
460	      </div>
461	
462	      {/* Footer hint */}
463	      <div style={{
464	        padding: "14px 16px", borderTop: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
465	        fontSize: 11, color: t.textMuted, lineHeight: 1.5,
466	        background: t.surfaceAlt,
467	      }}>
468	        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, marginBottom: 4 }}>
469	          <window.MCPIcon name="sparkle" size={12} color={layerAccent}/>
470	          <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: t.text }}>Push to MCP</span>
471	        </div>
472	        Click any card to inspect dependencies & request a build.
473	      </div>
474	    </nav>
475	  );
476	}
477	
478	function SidebarRow({ label, count, active, onClick, accent, indent, expandable, expanded, onToggle }) {
479	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
480	  return (
481	    <div
482	      onClick={onClick}
483	      style={{
484	        display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
485	        padding: indent ? "5px 10px 5px 28px" : "6px 10px",
486	        margin: "1px 0",
487	        borderRadius: 6, cursor: "pointer",
488	        background: active ? `${accent}14` : "transparent",
489	        color: active ? accent : t.text,
490	        fontSize: 13, fontWeight: active ? 600 : 500,
491	        letterSpacing: "-0.01em",
492	        position: "relative",
493	        transition: "background 0.12s",
494	      }}
495	      onMouseEnter={e => { if (!active) e.currentTarget.style.background = t.surfaceAlt; }}
496	      onMouseLeave={e => { if (!active) e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"; }}
497	    >
498	      {expandable && (
499	        <button
500	          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggle && onToggle(); }}
501	          style={{
502	            background: "none", border: "none", padding: 0, margin: 0,
503	            display: "flex", alignItems: "center", cursor: "pointer",
504	            color: t.textMuted,
505	          }}
506	        >
507	          <window.MCPIcon name={expanded ? "chevD" : "chevR"} size={12} color={t.textMuted}/>
508	        </button>
509	      )}
510	      {!expandable && <span style={{ width: 12 }}/>}
511	      <span style={{
512	        flex: 1, overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap",
513	      }}>{label}</span>
514	      <span style={{
515	        fontSize: 10, color: active ? accent : t.textFaint,
516	        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
517	        background: active ? "transparent" : "transparent",
518	      }}>
519	        {count}
520	      </span>
521	      {active && (
522	        <span style={{
523	          position: "absolute", left: 0, top: 6, bottom: 6, width: 2,
524	          background: accent, borderRadius: 2,
525	        }}/>
526	      )}
527	    </div>
528	  );
529	}
530	
531	// ─── Section header (above board) ─────────────────────────────────────────
532	function SectionHeader({
533	  layer, activePrimary, activeSecondary, visibleCounts, totals,
534	  statusFilter, setStatusFilter, viewMode, setViewMode,
535	  search, setSearch, onClearDrill, layerAccent,
536	}) {
537	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
538	  const layerLabel = window.LAYERS.find(l => l.key === layer)?.label;
539	  let title = "All " + layerLabel;
540	  let subtitle = `${visibleCounts.total} of ${totals.total} tools in this layer`;
541	  let crumb = null;
542	  if (activePrimary) {
543	    if (layer === "industry") {
544	      const sec = window.INDUSTRY_SECTORS.find(x => x.key === activePrimary);
545	      title = sec?.label || activePrimary;
546	      crumb = layerLabel;
547	    } else {
548	      const dom = window.PUBLIC_DOMAINS.find(x => x.key === activePrimary);
549	      const pdt = activeSecondary ? dom?.pdts.find(p => p.key === activeSecondary) : null;
550	      title = pdt?.label || dom?.label || activePrimary;
551	      crumb = pdt ? `${layerLabel} / ${dom?.label}` : layerLabel;
552	    }
553	    subtitle = `${visibleCounts.total} tool${visibleCounts.total === 1 ? "" : "s"} · ${visibleCounts.released} released · ${visibleCounts.dev} in dev · ${visibleCounts.none} no MCP needed`;
554	  }
555	
556	  return (
557	    <div data-screen-label="Section header" style={{ padding: "24px 28px 18px" }}>
558	      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "flex-start", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 20, marginBottom: 18 }}>
559	        <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
560	          {crumb && (
561	            <div style={{
562	              display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, marginBottom: 6,
563	              fontSize: 11, color: t.textMuted, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
564	              letterSpacing: "0.04em", textTransform: "uppercase",
565	            }}>
566	              <span>{crumb}</span>
567	              <button onClick={onClearDrill} style={{
568	                background: "none", border: "none", padding: "1px 6px",
569	                color: layerAccent, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 10,
570	                fontFamily: "inherit", letterSpacing: "0.04em",
571	              }}>
572	                ← clear
573	              </button>
574	            </div>
575	          )}
576	          <h1 className="serif" style={{
577	            fontSize: 36, fontWeight: 500, letterSpacing: "-0.025em", margin: 0,
578	            color: t.text, lineHeight: 1.05,
579	          }}>
580	            {title}
581	          </h1>
582	          <p style={{ marginTop: 6, fontSize: 13, color: t.textMuted }}>
583	            {subtitle}
584	          </p>
585	        </div>
586	
587	        {/* View switcher */}
588	        <div style={{
589	          display: "flex", padding: 3, borderRadius: 8,
590	          background: t.surfaceAlt, border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
591	          flexShrink: 0,
592	        }}>
593	          {[
594	            { key: "panorama", icon: "panorama", label: "Panorama" },
595	            { key: "kanban",   icon: "kanban",   label: "Kanban" },
596	            { key: "list",     icon: "list",     label: "List" },
597	            { key: "heatmap",  icon: "heatmap",  label: "Heatmap" },
598	          ].map(v => {
599	            const active = viewMode === v.key;
600	            return (
601	              <button
602	                key={v.key}
603	                onClick={() => setViewMode(v.key)}
604	                style={{
605	                  display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
606	                  padding: "6px 12px", border: "none", borderRadius: 6,
607	                  background: active ? t.surface : "transparent",
608	                  color: active ? t.text : t.textMuted,
609	                  fontSize: 12, fontWeight: active ? 600 : 500,
610	                  cursor: "pointer",
611	                  boxShadow: active ? "0 1px 2px rgba(60,40,20,0.06)" : "none",
612	                }}
613	              >
614	                <window.MCPIcon name={v.icon} size={13} color={active ? t.text : t.textMuted}/>
615	                {v.label}
616	              </button>
617	            );
618	          })}
619	        </div>
620	      </div>
621	
622	      {/* Status filter chips + KPI inline */}
623	      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
624	        <StatusChip
625	          label="All"
626	          count={visibleCounts.total}
627	          active={statusFilter === "all"}
628	          onClick={() => setStatusFilter("all")}
629	          accent={layerAccent}
630	        />
631	        {window.STATUSES.map(s => (
632	          <StatusChip
633	            key={s.key}
634	            label={s.label}
635	            dot={s.dot}
636	            count={visibleCounts[s.key]}
637	            active={statusFilter === s.key}
638	            onClick={() => setStatusFilter(statusFilter === s.key ? "all" : s.key)}
639	            accent={s.dot}
640	          />
641	        ))}
642	      </div>
643	    </div>
644	  );
645	}
646	
647	function StatusChip({ label, count, active, onClick, accent, dot }) {
648	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
649	  return (
650	    <button
651	      onClick={onClick}
652	      style={{
653	        display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8,
654	        padding: "6px 12px", border: `1px solid ${active ? accent : t.border}`,
655	        background: active ? `${accent}14` : t.surface,
656	        color: active ? accent : t.text,
657	        borderRadius: 999, cursor: "pointer",
658	        fontSize: 12, fontWeight: active ? 600 : 500,
659	        transition: "all 0.14s",
660	      }}
661	    >
662	      {dot && <span style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: "50%", background: dot }}/>}
663	      <span>{label}</span>
664	      <span style={{
665	        fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
666	        color: active ? accent : t.textFaint,
667	        padding: "1px 6px", borderRadius: 999,
668	        background: active ? "transparent" : t.surfaceAlt,
669	      }}>
670	        {count}
671	      </span>
672	    </button>
673	  );
674	}
675	
676	ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(<App/>);
677	</script>
678	
679	</body>
680	</html>

294	// ─── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
295	window.ownerToPath = function(owner) {
296	  // returns { layer, primary, primaryLabel, secondary?, secondaryLabel? }
297	  if (owner.includes(".")) {
298	    const [dKey, pKey] = owner.split(".");
299	    const d = window.PUBLIC_DOMAINS.find(x => x.key === dKey);
300	    const p = d?.pdts.find(x => x.key === pKey);
301	    return {
302	      layer: "public", primary: dKey, primaryLabel: d?.label || dKey,
303	      secondary: pKey, secondaryLabel: p?.label || pKey,
304	    };
305	  }
306	  const s = window.INDUSTRY_SECTORS.find(x => x.key === owner);
307	  return { layer: "industry", primary: owner, primaryLabel: s?.label || owner };
308	};
309	
310	window.countToolsForOwner = function(layer, primary, secondary) {
311	  return window.TOOLS.filter(t => {
312	    const p = window.ownerToPath(t.owner);
313	    if (p.layer !== layer) return false;
314	    if (primary && p.primary !== primary) return false;
315	    if (secondary && p.secondary !== secondary) return false;
316	    return true;
317	  });
318	};

1	// ─── MCP Panorama View ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2	// Landscape map: every tool = a colored tile; grouped into sector / domain /
3	// PDT cards. Each card has an aggregate header so you can spot leaders &
4	// laggards at a glance.
5	
6	window.PanoramaView = function PanoramaView({ tools, onPick, activeId, layer, sort = "default", density = "normal" }) {
7	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
8	
9	  // Build groups, each with stats
10	  const groups = React.useMemo(() => {
11	    const out = [];
12	    if (layer === "industry") {
13	      window.INDUSTRY_SECTORS.forEach(sec => {
14	        const items = tools.filter(x => x.owner === sec.key);
15	        if (items.length) {
16	          out.push({
17	            kind: "sector",
18	            key: sec.key,
19	            title: sec.label,
20	            short: sec.short,
21	            items,
22	            stats: computeStats(items),
23	          });
24	        }
25	      });
26	    } else {
27	      window.PUBLIC_DOMAINS.forEach(dom => {
28	        const allItems = tools.filter(x => window.ownerToPath(x.owner).primary === dom.key);
29	        if (!allItems.length) return;
30	        const pdts = dom.pdts
31	          .map(p => ({
32	            key: p.key, title: p.label,
33	            items: tools.filter(x => x.owner === `${dom.key}.${p.key}`),
34	          }))
35	          .filter(p => p.items.length);
36	        out.push({
37	          kind: "domain",
38	          key: dom.key,
39	          title: dom.label,
40	          short: dom.short,
41	          items: allItems,
42	          pdts,
43	          stats: computeStats(allItems),
44	        });
45	      });
46	    }
47	    // Sort
48	    if (sort === "coverage") {
49	      out.sort((a, b) => b.stats.coveragePct - a.stats.coveragePct);
50	    } else if (sort === "lagging") {
51	      out.sort((a, b) => b.stats.lagPct - a.stats.lagPct);
52	    } else if (sort === "size") {
53	      out.sort((a, b) => b.items.length - a.items.length);
54	    }
55	    return out;
56	  }, [tools, layer, sort]);
57	
58	  // Layer-wide aggregate
59	  const layerStats = useLayerStats(layer, tools);
60	
61	  if (groups.length === 0) {
62	    return <window.EmptyState/>;
63	  }
64	
65	  return (
66	    <div style={{ padding: "0 28px 28px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 22 }}>
67	      {/* Layer dashboard strip */}
68	      <LayerSummary layer={layer} stats={layerStats} groups={groups}/>
69	
70	      {/* Card grid */}
71	      <div style={{
72	        display: "grid",
73	        gridTemplateColumns: layer === "industry"
74	          ? "repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))"
75	          : "repeat(auto-fit, minmax(520px, 1fr))",
76	        gap: 14,
77	        alignItems: "start",
78	      }}>
79	        {groups.map(g => g.kind === "sector"
80	          ? <SectorCard key={g.key} group={g} onPick={onPick} activeId={activeId} density={density}/>
81	          : <DomainCard key={g.key} group={g} onPick={onPick} activeId={activeId} density={density}/>
82	        )}
83	      </div>
84	    </div>
85	  );
86	};
87	
88	// ─── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
89	function computeStats(items) {
90	  const counts = { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0 };
91	  items.forEach(x => counts[x.status]++);
92	  const total = items.length;
93	  // Coverage = live OR in-dev share of "MCP-eligible" tools (excluding none).
94	  // But for leaderboard purposes we also expose release-only and lag rates.
95	  const eligible = total - counts.none;
96	  const coveragePct = eligible === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((counts.released / eligible) * 100);
97	  const activePct = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round(((counts.released + counts.dev) / total) * 100);
98	  const lagPct = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((counts.none / total) * 100);
99	  const releasedPct = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((counts.released / total) * 100);
100	  return { counts, total, coveragePct, activePct, lagPct, releasedPct };
101	}
102	
103	function useLayerStats(layer, tools) {
104	  return React.useMemo(() => computeStats(tools), [tools, layer]);
105	}
106	
107	function rankTag(stats) {
108	  // returns { label, fg, bg } | null
109	  if (stats.total < 3) return null;
110	  if (stats.releasedPct >= 75) return { label: "Leading",   fg: "oklch(38% 0.10 150)", bg: "oklch(95% 0.04 155)" };
111	  if (stats.releasedPct >= 50) return { label: "On track",  fg: "oklch(38% 0.07 110)", bg: "oklch(95% 0.03 110)" };
112	  if (stats.lagPct >= 50)      return { label: "Lagging",   fg: "oklch(40% 0.10 30)",  bg: "oklch(95% 0.04 50)"  };
113	  if (stats.releasedPct < 25)  return { label: "Early",     fg: "oklch(45% 0.06 60)",  bg: "oklch(96% 0.02 70)"  };
114	  return null;
115	}
116	
117	// ─── Layer-wide dashboard summary ────────────────────────────────────────────
118	function LayerSummary({ layer, stats, groups }) {
119	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
120	  const meta = window.LAYERS.find(l => l.key === layer);
121	  const accent = layer === "industry" ? t.industry : t.public;
122	  const tintBg = layer === "industry" ? t.industrySoft : t.publicSoft;
123	
124	  // best & worst by released %
125	  const ranked = [...groups].sort((a, b) => b.stats.releasedPct - a.stats.releasedPct);
126	  const top = ranked[0];
127	  const bot = ranked[ranked.length - 1];
128	
129	  return (
130	    <section data-screen-label={`Layer summary · ${meta.label}`} style={{
131	      display: "grid",
132	      gridTemplateColumns: "minmax(260px, 1.3fr) repeat(3, minmax(120px, 1fr)) minmax(220px, 1.4fr)",
133	      gap: 0,
134	      background: t.surface,
135	      border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
136	      borderRadius: 12,
137	      overflow: "hidden",
138	    }}>
139	      {/* Cell: layer label + big stacked bar */}
140	      <div style={{ padding: "16px 20px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
141	        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
142	          <span style={{
143	            padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 4,
144	            background: tintBg, color: accent,
145	            fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
146	            letterSpacing: "0.06em", textTransform: "uppercase", fontWeight: 600,
147	          }}>
148	            {layer === "industry" ? "Industry" : "Public"}
149	          </span>
150	          <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: t.textMuted }}>
151	            {groups.length} {layer === "industry" ? "sectors" : "domains"} · {stats.total} tools
152	          </span>
153	        </div>
154	        <div className="serif" style={{
155	          fontSize: 36, fontWeight: 500, color: t.text,
156	          letterSpacing: "-0.025em", lineHeight: 1,
157	        }}>
158	          {stats.activePct}%
159	          <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: t.textMuted, fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif", fontWeight: 400, marginLeft: 8 }}>
160	            MCP-active
161	          </span>
162	        </div>
163	        {/* Big stacked bar */}
164	        <div style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>
165	          <div style={{
166	            display: "flex", height: 10, borderRadius: 5, overflow: "hidden",
167	            background: t.surfaceAlt,
168	          }}>
169	            {window.STATUSES.map((s) => {
170	              const c = stats.counts[s.key];
171	              if (c === 0) return null;
172	              return (
173	                <div key={s.key} title={`${s.label}: ${c}`} style={{
174	                  flex: c, background: s.dot, opacity: s.key === "none" ? 0.45 : 1,
175	                }}/>
176	              );
177	            })}
178	          </div>
179	          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 14, marginTop: 6, fontSize: 11, color: t.textMuted, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}>
180	            {window.STATUSES.map(s => (
181	              <span key={s.key} style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 4 }}>
182	                <span style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: "50%", background: s.dot }}/>
183	                {stats.counts[s.key]} {s.short}
184	              </span>
185	            ))}
186	          </div>
187	        </div>
188	      </div>
189	
190	      <StatCell label="Released" value={stats.counts.released} dot={window.STATUSES[2].dot}/>
191	      <StatCell label="In development" value={stats.counts.dev} dot={window.STATUSES[1].dot}/>
192	      <StatCell label="No MCP needed" value={stats.counts.none} dot={window.STATUSES[0].dot}/>
193	
194	      {/* Leader / Laggard cell */}
195	      <div style={{
196	        padding: "16px 20px", borderLeft: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
197	        display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10,
198	        background: t.surfaceAlt,
199	      }}>
200	        {top && (
201	          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
202	            <span style={{ fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted, letterSpacing: "0.06em", textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
203	              Leading
204	            </span>
205	            <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: t.text, fontWeight: 500 }}>
206	              {top.title} <span style={{ color: window.STATUSES[2].dot, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12 }}>{top.stats.releasedPct}%</span>
207	            </span>
208	          </div>
209	        )}
210	        {bot && bot.key !== top?.key && (
211	          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
212	            <span style={{ fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted, letterSpacing: "0.06em", textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
213	              Lagging
214	            </span>

216	              {bot.title} <span style={{ color: window.STATUSES[0].dot, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: 12 }}>{bot.stats.releasedPct}%</span>
217	            </span>
218	          </div>
219	        )}
220	      </div>
221	    </section>
222	  );
223	}

226	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
227	  return (
228	    <div style={{
229	      padding: "16px 18px", borderLeft: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
230	      display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6, justifyContent: "center",
231	    }}>
232	      <span style={{
233	        fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted,
234	        letterSpacing: "0.06em", textTransform: "uppercase",
235	        display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
236	      }}>
237	        <span style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: "50%", background: dot }}/>
238	        {label}
239	      </span>
240	      <span style={{
241	        fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 500, color: t.text,
242	        fontFamily: "'Fraunces', Georgia, serif", letterSpacing: "-0.02em",
243	        lineHeight: 1,
244	      }}>
245	        {value}
246	      </span>
247	    </div>
248	  );
249	}

251	// ─── Sector card (Industry) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
252	function SectorCard({ group, onPick, activeId, density }) {
253	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
254	  const tag = rankTag(group.stats);
255	
256	  return (
257	    <article data-screen-label={`Sector · ${group.title}`} style={{
258	      background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}`, borderRadius: 12,
259	      padding: 14, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12,
260	    }}>
261	      <CardHeader group={group} tag={tag}/>
262	      <TileWrap items={group.items} onPick={onPick} activeId={activeId} density={density}/>
263	    </article>

265	}
266	
267	// ─── Domain card (Public) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
268	function DomainCard({ group, onPick, activeId, density }) {
269	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
270	  const tag = rankTag(group.stats);
271	
272	  return (
273	    <article data-screen-label={`Domain · ${group.title}`} style={{
274	      background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}`, borderRadius: 12,
275	      padding: 14, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12,
276	    }}>
277	      <CardHeader group={group} tag={tag}/>
278	      <div style={{
279	        display: "grid",
280	        gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr))",
281	        gap: 10,
282	      }}>
283	        {group.pdts.map(p => (
284	          <PdtBlock key={p.key} pdt={p} onPick={onPick} activeId={activeId} density={density}/>
285	        ))}
286	      </div>

288	  );
289	}
290	
291	function PdtBlock({ pdt, onPick, activeId, density }) {
292	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
293	  const stats = computeStats(pdt.items);
294	  return (
295	    <div style={{
296	      background: t.surfaceAlt, borderRadius: 8, padding: "10px 11px",
297	      border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
298	      display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8,
299	    }}>
300	      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8 }}>
301	        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: t.text, letterSpacing: "-0.01em" }}>
302	          {pdt.title}
303	        </span>
304	        <span style={{
305	          fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted,
306	        }}>
307	          {pdt.items.length}
308	        </span>
309	      </div>
310	      <TileWrap items={pdt.items} onPick={onPick} activeId={activeId} density={density} compact/>
311	    </div>
312	  );
313	}
314	
315	// ─── Header used by both card types ─────────────────────────────────────────
316	function CardHeader({ group, tag }) {
317	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
318	  const stats = group.stats;
319	  return (
320	    <header style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
321	      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "baseline", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 10 }}>

323	          <h3 className="serif" style={{
324	            fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 500, color: t.text,
325	            letterSpacing: "-0.02em", margin: 0,
326	            overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap",
327	          }}>
328	            {group.title}
329	          </h3>
330	          <span style={{
331	            fontSize: 11, color: t.textMuted, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
332	          }}>
333	            {group.items.length}
334	          </span>
335	        </div>
336	        {tag && (
337	          <span style={{
338	            padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 4,
339	            background: tag.bg, color: tag.fg,
340	            fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
341	            letterSpacing: "0.06em", textTransform: "uppercase", fontWeight: 600,
342	            flexShrink: 0,
343	          }}>
344	            {tag.label}
345	          </span>
346	        )}
347	      </div>
348	
349	      {/* Coverage bar */}
350	      <div>
351	        <div style={{ display: "flex", height: 5, borderRadius: 3, overflow: "hidden", background: t.surfaceAlt }}>
352	          {window.STATUSES.map(s => {
353	            const c = stats.counts[s.key];
354	            if (c === 0) return null;
355	            return (
356	              <div key={s.key} title={`${s.label}: ${c}`} style={{
357	                flex: c, background: s.dot, opacity: s.key === "none" ? 0.5 : 1,
358	              }}/>
359	            );
360	          })}
361	        </div>
362	        <div style={{
363	          marginTop: 6, display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between",
364	          fontSize: 10, color: t.textMuted, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
365	          letterSpacing: "-0.005em",
366	        }}>
367	          <span style={{ display: "inline-flex", gap: 10 }}>
368	            <Stat dot={window.STATUSES[2].dot} v={stats.counts.released} l="live"/>
369	            <Stat dot={window.STATUSES[1].dot} v={stats.counts.dev} l="dev"/>
370	            <Stat dot={window.STATUSES[0].dot} v={stats.counts.none} l="—"/>
371	          </span>
372	          <span style={{ color: t.text, fontWeight: 600 }}>
373	            {stats.releasedPct}% live
374	          </span>
375	        </div>
376	      </div>
377	    </header>
378	  );

380	
381	function Stat({ dot, v, l }) {
382	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
383	  return (
384	    <span style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 4 }}>
385	      <span style={{ width: 5, height: 5, borderRadius: "50%", background: dot, opacity: l === "—" ? 0.55 : 1 }}/>
386	      <span style={{ color: t.text, fontWeight: 600 }}>{v}</span>
387	      <span style={{ opacity: 0.7 }}>{l !== "—" ? l : ""}</span>
388	    </span>
389	  );
390	}
391	
392	// ─── Tile wrap (the panorama itself) ────────────────────────────────────────
393	function TileWrap({ items, onPick, activeId, density, compact }) {
394	  return (
395	    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: compact ? 4 : 6 }}>
396	      {items.map(tool => (
397	        <ToolTile
398	          key={tool.id} tool={tool}
399	          onClick={() => onPick(tool)}
400	          active={activeId === tool.id}
401	          density={density} compact={compact}
402	        />
403	      ))}
404	    </div>
405	  );
406	}
407	
408	function ToolTile({ tool, onClick, active, density, compact }) {
409	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
410	  const meta = window.STATUSES.find(s => s.key === tool.status);
411	  const isDense = density === "dense" || compact;
412	  const padY = isDense ? 4 : 6;
413	  const padX = isDense ? 7 : 9;
414	  const fontSize = isDense ? 11 : 12;
415	  const clickable = tool.status === "released";
416	
417	  const baseStyle = {
418	    display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
419	    flexShrink: 0,
420	    padding: `${padY}px ${padX}px`,
421	    background: meta.bg,
422	    color: meta.text,
423	    border: `1px solid ${active && clickable ? meta.dot : meta.dot + "33"}`,
424	    borderLeft: `3px solid ${meta.dot}`,
425	    borderRadius: 5,
426	    fontSize, fontWeight: 500,
427	    letterSpacing: "-0.005em",
428	    fontFamily: "inherit",
429	    transition: "all 0.14s",
430	    position: "relative",
431	    textDecoration: "none",
432	  };
433	
434	  const inner = (
435	    <>
436	      <span style={{ whiteSpace: "nowrap", flexShrink: 0 }}>
437	        {tool.name}
438	      </span>
439	      {tool.deps >= 10 && (
440	        <span style={{
441	          fontSize: 9, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
442	          padding: "0 4px", borderRadius: 4,
443	          background: meta.dot, color: "#fff",
444	          fontWeight: 600,
445	        }}>
446	          {tool.deps}
447	        </span>
448	      )}
449	      {clickable && (
450	        <svg width="9" height="9" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" style={{ marginLeft: 1, opacity: 0.7 }}>
451	          <path d="M5 3l5 5-5 5" stroke={meta.dot} strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"/>
452	        </svg>
453	      )}
454	    </>
455	  );
456	
457	  if (!clickable) {
458	    // Static tile: no pointer, no hover transform, no click target.
459	    return (
460	      <span
461	        data-comment-anchor={`tile-${tool.id}`}
462	        title={`${tool.name} · ${meta.label}  (MCP not yet available)`}
463	        aria-disabled="true"
464	        style={{ ...baseStyle, cursor: "default", userSelect: "none" }}
465	      >
466	        {inner}
467	      </span>
468	    );
469	  }
470	
471	  // Clickable tile (released) — navigates to AgentCenter MCP detail.
472	  const slug = tool.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
473	  return (
474	    <a
475	      href={`AgentCenter.html#mcp=${slug}`}
476	      data-comment-anchor={`tile-${tool.id}`}
477	      title={`${tool.name} · Open MCP detail →`}
478	      style={{
479	        ...baseStyle,
480	        cursor: "pointer",
481	        boxShadow: active ? `0 0 0 3px ${meta.dot}33` : "none",
482	      }}
483	      onClick={(e) => {
484	        // Hold cmd/ctrl/shift/middle to let native behavior (new tab) happen.
485	        if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.button === 1) return;
486	      }}
487	      onMouseEnter={e => {
488	        e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = meta.dot;
489	        e.currentTarget.style.transform = "translateY(-1px)";
490	        e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = `0 4px 12px -4px ${meta.dot}55`;
491	      }}
492	      onMouseLeave={e => {
493	        e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = active ? meta.dot : meta.dot + "33";
494	        e.currentTarget.style.borderLeftColor = meta.dot;
495	        e.currentTarget.style.transform = "translateY(0)";
496	        e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = active ? `0 0 0 3px ${meta.dot}33` : "none";
497	      }}
498	    >
499	      {inner}
500	    </a>
501	  );
502	}
503

49	// Status pill (dot + label)
50	window.StatusPill = function StatusPill({ status, size = "sm" }) {
51	  const meta = window.STATUSES.find(s => s.key === status);
52	  if (!meta) return null;
53	  const small = size === "sm";
54	  return (
55	    <span style={{
56	      display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: small ? 5 : 6,
57	      padding: small ? "2px 8px" : "3px 10px",
58	      borderRadius: 999,
59	      background: meta.bg,
60	      color: meta.text,
61	      fontSize: small ? 11 : 12,
62	      fontWeight: 500,
63	      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
64	      letterSpacing: "-0.01em",
65	      whiteSpace: "nowrap",
66	      lineHeight: 1.4,
67	    }}>
68	      <span style={{ width: small ? 5 : 6, height: small ? 5 : 6, borderRadius: "50%", background: meta.dot }}/>
69	      {meta.short}
70	    </span>
71	  );
72	};
73	
74	// Owner crumb path
75	window.OwnerCrumbs = function OwnerCrumbs({ owner, layer }) {
76	  const p = window.ownerToPath(owner);
77	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
78	  return (
79	    <div style={{
80	      fontSize: 11, color: t.textMuted, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
81	      letterSpacing: "-0.01em", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 4,
82	    }}>
83	      <span>{p.primaryLabel}</span>
84	      {p.secondaryLabel && (
85	        <>
86	          <span style={{ opacity: 0.5 }}>/</span>
87	          <span>{p.secondaryLabel}</span>
88	        </>
89	      )}
90	    </div>
91	  );
92	};
93	
94	// Compact tool card (Kanban + list view)
95	window.ToolCard = function ToolCard({ tool, onClick, dense = true, selected = false }) {
96	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
97	  return (
98	    <button
99	      onClick={onClick}
100	      data-comment-anchor={`tool-${tool.id}`}
101	      style={{
102	        width: "100%", textAlign: "left", background: t.surface,
103	        border: `1px solid ${selected ? t.accent : t.border}`,
104	        borderRadius: 8,
105	        padding: dense ? "8px 10px" : "12px 14px",
106	        cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: "inherit", color: t.text,
107	        transition: "all 0.16s",
108	        boxShadow: selected ? `0 0 0 3px ${t.accent}18` : "0 1px 0 rgba(60,40,20,0.02)",
109	        display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: dense ? 4 : 6,
110	      }}
111	      onMouseEnter={e => {
112	        e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = t.borderStrong;
113	        e.currentTarget.style.transform = "translateY(-1px)";
114	      }}
115	      onMouseLeave={e => {
116	        e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = selected ? t.accent : t.border;
117	        e.currentTarget.style.transform = "translateY(0)";
118	      }}
119	    >
120	      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8 }}>
121	        <span style={{
122	          fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: "-0.01em",
123	          overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap",
124	        }}>
125	          {tool.name}
126	        </span>
127	        {tool.deps > 0 && (
128	          <span style={{
129	            display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 3,
130	            fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
131	            color: t.textMuted, flexShrink: 0,
132	          }}>
133	            <window.MCPIcon name="deps" size={10} color={t.textFaint}/>
134	            {tool.deps}
135	          </span>
136	        )}
137	      </div>
138	      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 6 }}>
139	        <window.OwnerCrumbs owner={tool.owner} />
140	        {tool.status === "released" && tool.adoptionPct > 0 && (
141	          <span style={{ fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted }}>
142	            {tool.adoptionPct}%
143	          </span>
144	        )}
145	      </div>
146	    </button>
147	  );
148	};
149	
150	// Detail side panel
151	window.ToolDetailPanel = function ToolDetailPanel({ tool, onClose }) {
152	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
153	  const meta = tool ? window.STATUSES.find(s => s.key === tool.status) : null;
154	  const path = tool ? window.ownerToPath(tool.owner) : null;
155	
156	  // Simulated downstream dependents — derived deterministically per tool id
157	  const downstreams = React.useMemo(() => {
158	    if (!tool) return [];
159	    const others = window.TOOLS.filter(x => x.id !== tool.id);
160	    const seed = tool.id * 7;
161	    const n = Math.min(tool.deps, 5);
162	    const picks = [];
163	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
164	      picks.push(others[(seed + i * 13) % others.length]);
165	    }
166	    return picks;
167	  }, [tool?.id]);
168	
169	  return (
170	    <aside
171	      data-screen-label={tool ? `Detail · ${tool.name}` : "Detail panel"}
172	      style={{
173	        position: "fixed", top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0,
174	        width: tool ? 440 : 0,
175	        background: t.surface,
176	        borderLeft: tool ? `1px solid ${t.border}` : "none",
177	        boxShadow: tool ? "-20px 0 40px -20px rgba(40,28,15,0.18)" : "none",
178	        transition: "width 0.28s cubic-bezier(.32,.72,.32,1)",
179	        overflow: "hidden", zIndex: 30, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column",
180	      }}
181	    >
182	      {tool && (
183	        <>
184	          {/* Header */}
185	          <div style={{
186	            padding: "20px 24px 16px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
187	            display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12,
188	          }}>
189	            <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 12 }}>
190	              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6, minWidth: 0 }}>
191	                <div style={{
192	                  display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
193	                  padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 4,
194	                  background: path.layer === "industry" ? t.industrySoft : t.publicSoft,
195	                  color: path.layer === "industry" ? t.industry : t.public,
196	                  fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
197	                  letterSpacing: "0.04em", textTransform: "uppercase",
198	                  alignSelf: "flex-start", fontWeight: 600,
199	                }}>
200	                  <window.MCPIcon name={path.layer === "industry" ? "industry" : "public"} size={10}/>
201	                  {path.layer === "industry" ? "Industry" : "Public"}
202	                </div>
203	                <h2 className="serif" style={{
204	                  fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 500, color: t.text, letterSpacing: "-0.02em",
205	                  lineHeight: 1.1, margin: 0,
206	                }}>{tool.name}</h2>
207	                <div style={{
208	                  fontSize: 12, color: t.textMuted, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
209	                }}>
210	                  {path.primaryLabel}{path.secondaryLabel ? ` · ${path.secondaryLabel}` : ""}
211	                </div>
212	              </div>
213	              <button onClick={onClose} style={{
214	                background: "none", border: "none", padding: 6, borderRadius: 6,
215	                cursor: "pointer", color: t.textMuted, flexShrink: 0,
216	              }} aria-label="Close">
217	                <window.MCPIcon name="close" size={16}/>
218	              </button>
219	            </div>
220	            <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: t.textMuted, lineHeight: 1.5, margin: 0 }}>
221	              {tool.blurb}
222	            </p>
223	          </div>
224	
225	          {/* Status block */}
226	          <div style={{ padding: "16px 24px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
227	            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 10 }}>
228	              <span style={{ fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted, letterSpacing: "0.04em", textTransform: "uppercase" }}>
229	                MCP Status
230	              </span>
231	              <window.StatusPill status={tool.status} size="md"/>
232	            </div>
233	            <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: t.text, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>{meta.desc}</div>
234	            {tool.status === "released" && tool.adoptionPct > 0 && (
235	              <div style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
236	                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", fontSize: 11, color: t.textMuted, marginBottom: 5, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", whiteSpace: "nowrap", gap: 8 }}>
237	                  <span>Adoption (estimate)</span>
238	                  <span>{tool.adoptionPct}%</span>
239	                </div>
240	                <div style={{ height: 6, background: t.surfaceAlt, borderRadius: 3, overflow: "hidden" }}>
241	                  <div style={{
242	                    width: `${tool.adoptionPct}%`, height: "100%",
243	                    background: meta.dot, borderRadius: 3,
244	                  }}/>
245	                </div>
246	              </div>
247	            )}
248	          </div>
249	
250	          {/* Meta grid */}
251	          <div style={{
252	            padding: "16px 24px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
253	            display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "14px 18px",
254	          }}>
255	            <DetailField label="Dependents" value={`${tool.deps} tools`}/>
256	            <DetailField label="Last update" value={tool.updated === "—" ? "—" : tool.updated + " ago"}/>
257	            <DetailField label="Owner" value={path.secondaryLabel || path.primaryLabel}/>
258	            <DetailField label="Endpoint" value={tool.status === "released" ? `mcp://${tool.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,"-")}` : "—"} mono/>
259	          </div>
260	
261	          {/* Dependents */}
262	          <div style={{ padding: "16px 24px", flex: 1, overflow: "auto" }}>
263	            <div style={{
264	              fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: t.textMuted,
265	              letterSpacing: "0.04em", textTransform: "uppercase", marginBottom: 10,
266	            }}>
267	              Downstream tools ({tool.deps})
268	            </div>
269	            {downstreams.length === 0 ? (
270	              <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: t.textFaint, fontStyle: "italic" }}>No tracked dependents.</div>
271	            ) : (
272	              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6 }}>
273	                {downstreams.map(d => {
274	                  const dp = window.ownerToPath(d.owner);
275	                  return (
276	                    <div key={d.id} style={{
277	                      display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between",
278	                      padding: "8px 10px", border: `1px solid ${t.border}`, borderRadius: 6, gap: 8,
279	                    }}>
280	                      <div style={{ minWidth: 0, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
281	                        <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500 }}>{d.name}</span>
282	                        <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: t.textMuted, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}>
283	                          {dp.primaryLabel}{dp.secondaryLabel ? ` · ${dp.secondaryLabel}` : ""}
284	                        </span>
285	                      </div>
286	                      <window.StatusPill status={d.status} size="sm"/>
287	                    </div>
288	                  );
289	                })}
290	              </div>
291	            )}
292	          </div>
293	
294	          {/* Action footer */}
295	          <div style={{
296	            padding: "14px 24px", borderTop: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
297	            display: "flex", gap: 8, background: t.surfaceAlt,
298	          }}>
299	            {tool.status === "released" ? (
300	              <button style={{
301	                flex: 1, padding: "9px 14px", borderRadius: 7,
302	                background: t.accent, color: t.accentFg, border: "none",
303	                fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: "inherit",
304	                display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: 6,
305	              }}>
306	                <window.MCPIcon name="arrow" size={12} color={t.accentFg}/>
307	                Open in marketplace
308	              </button>
309	            ) : (
310	              <button style={{
311	                flex: 1, padding: "9px 14px", borderRadius: 7,
312	                background: t.surface, color: t.text, border: `1px solid ${t.borderStrong}`,
313	                fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: "inherit",
314	              }}>
315	                {tool.status === "dev" ? "Track progress" : "Request MCP build"}
316	              </button>
317	            )}
318	            <button style={{
319	              padding: "9px 14px", borderRadius: 7,
320	              background: t.surface, color: t.textMuted, border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
321	              fontSize: 13, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: "inherit",
322	              display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6,
323	            }}>
324	              <window.MCPIcon name="link" size={12} color={t.textMuted}/>
325	              Docs
326	            </button>
327	          </div>
328	        </>
329	      )}
330	    </aside>
331	  );
332	};

335	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
336	  return (

340	      </span>

342	        fontSize: 13, color: t.text,

1	// ─── MCP Kanban — View modes ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

4	window.KanbanView = function KanbanView({ tools, onPick, activeId }) {
5	  const t = window.MCPTheme;
6	  const grouped = window.STATUSES.map(s => ({
7	    ...s,
8	    items: tools.filter(x => x.status === s.key),
9	  }));
10	  const max = Math.max(1, ...grouped.map(g => g.items.length));
11	
12	  return (
13	    <div style={{
14	      display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr))",
15	      gap: 16, alignItems: "start", padding: "0 28px 28px",
16	    }}>
17	      {grouped.map(col => (
18	        <section key={col.key} data-screen-label={`Kanban · ${col.short}`} style={{
19	          display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12,
20	          background: t.surfaceAlt, borderRadius: 12, padding: 14,
21	          border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
22	          minHeight: 200,
23	        }}>
24	          {/* Column header */}
25	          <header style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8, padding: "4px 4px 0" }}>
26	            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8 }}>
27	              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 9 }}>
28	                <span style={{ width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: "50%", background: col.dot }}/>
29	                <h3 style={{
30	                  fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: t.text,
31	                  letterSpacing: "-0.01em", margin: 0,
32	                }}>
33	                  {col.label}
34	                </h3>
35	              </div>
36	              <span style={{
37	                fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
38	                color: t.textMuted, padding: "2px 8px",
39	                background: t.surface, borderRadius: 999, border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
40	              }}>
41	                {col.items.length}
42	              </span>
43	            </div>
44	            {/* mini progress bar relative to biggest column */}
45	            <div style={{ height: 3, background: t.border, borderRadius: 2, overflow: "hidden" }}>
46	              <div style={{
47	                width: `${(col.items.length / max) * 100}%`, height: "100%",
48	                background: col.dot, opacity: 0.7,
49	              }}/>
50	            </div>
51	          </header>
52	
53	          {/* Cards */}
54	          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
55	            {col.items.length === 0 ? (
56	              <div style={{
57	                fontSize: 12, color: t.textFaint, fontStyle: "italic",
58	                padding: "20px 4px", textAlign: "center",
59	              }}>
60	                No tools in this column.
61	              </div>
62	            ) : (
63	              col.items.map(tool => (
64	                <window.ToolCard
65	                  key={tool.id} tool={tool}
66	                  selected={activeId === tool.id}
67	                  onClick={() => onPick(tool)}
68	                />
69	              ))
70	            )}
71	          </div>
72	        </section>
73	      ))}
74	    </div>
75	  );
76	};

18	const { t } = useI18n()

20	const localePath = useLocalePath()
21	
22	type Category = "all" | "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
23	
24	const BROWSE_ITEMS: { key: Category; labelKey: string; Icon: Component }[] = [
25	  { key: "all", labelKey: "sidebar.allExtensions", Icon: Boxes },
26	  { key: "skills", labelKey: "sidebar.skills", Icon: Zap },
27	  { key: "mcp", labelKey: "sidebar.mcpServers", Icon: Globe2 },
28	  { key: "slash", labelKey: "sidebar.slashCommands", Icon: Command },
29	  { key: "plugins", labelKey: "sidebar.plugins", Icon: Plug },
30	]

32	const COLLECTION_PLACEHOLDERS = [
33	  { id: "p1", name: "My Tools" },
34	  { id: "p2", name: "Reviewed" },
35	]
36	
37	const activeCategory = computed<Category>(() => {
38	  const raw = route.query.category
39	  if (typeof raw === "string" && ["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"].includes(raw)) {
40	    return raw as Category
41	  }
42	  return "all"
43	})
44	
45	const activeFuncCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.funcCat === "string" ? route.query.funcCat : null))
46	const activeSubCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.subCat === "string" ? route.query.subCat : null))
47	const activeL2 = computed(() => (typeof route.query.l2 === "string" ? route.query.l2 : null))
48	
49	const expansion = reactive({
50	  sections: { browse: true, categories: true, collections: false },
51	  funcCats: {} as Record<string, boolean>,
52	  l1: {} as Record<string, boolean>,
53	})
54	
55	if (activeFuncCat.value) expansion.funcCats[activeFuncCat.value] = true
56	else expansion.funcCats.workTask = true
57	if (activeSubCat.value) expansion.l1[activeSubCat.value] = true
58	
59	watch(activeFuncCat, (next) => {
60	  if (next) expansion.funcCats[next] = true
61	})
62	watch(activeSubCat, (next) => {
63	  if (next) expansion.l1[next] = true
64	})
65	
66	function buildHref(updates: Record<string, string | null>): string {
67	  const params = new URLSearchParams()
68	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
69	    if (typeof v === "string" && k !== "page") params.set(k, v)
70	  }
71	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(updates)) {
72	    if (v === null) params.delete(k)
73	    else params.set(k, v)
74	  }
75	  const qs = params.toString()
76	  const path = localePath("/extensions")
77	  return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path
78	}
79	
80	function toggleSection(key: "browse" | "categories" | "collections") {
81	  expansion.sections[key] = !expansion.sections[key]
82	}
83	function toggleFuncCat(key: string) {
84	  expansion.funcCats[key] = !expansion.funcCats[key]
85	}
86	function toggleL1(key: string) {
87	  expansion.l1[key] = !expansion.l1[key]
88	}
89	</script>

4	defineProps<{ collapsed: boolean }>()
5	const emit = defineEmits<{ "toggle-sidebar": [] }>()
6	
7	const { t } = useI18n()

9	const router = useRouter()

11

14	watch(
15	  () => route.query.q,
16	  (next) => {
17	    q.value = typeof next === "string" ? next : ""

21	function onSubmit() {
22	  const query: Record<string, string> = {}

25	  }
26	  const trimmed = q.value.trim()
27	  if (trimmed) query.q = trimmed
28	  router.push({ path: localePath("/extensions"), query })
29	}

function toggleSidebar() {
  sidebarCollapsed.value = !sidebarCollapsed.value
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="h-screen flex flex-col bg-(--color-bg) text-(--color-ink)">

      href="#main-content"

      <main id="main-content" tabindex="-1" class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto focus:outline-none">

      </main>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  bigint,
  customType,
  index,
  integer,
  jsonb,
  numeric,
  pgEnum,
  pgTable,
  primaryKey,
  text,
  timestamp,
  unique,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

import { users } from "./auth";
import { departments, organizations } from "./org";

// tsvector is a Postgres-native type; Drizzle doesn't ship a built-in helper.
// We declare it as a custom type so the ORM can reference it in WHERE clauses
// via sql``. It is GENERATED ALWAYS (read-only); never written by app code.
const tsvector = customType<{ data: string }>({
  dataType() { return "tsvector"; },
});

export const extensionCategoryEnum = pgEnum("extension_category", [
  "skills",
  "mcp",
  "slash",
  "plugins",
]);

export const extensionScopeEnum = pgEnum("extension_scope", [
  "personal",
  "org",
  "enterprise",
]);

export const extensionBadgeEnum = pgEnum("extension_badge", [
  "official",
  "popular",
  "new",
]);
