{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/since-we-are-developing-joyful-kahan.md",
  "content": "# MCP Adoption Panorama\n\n## Context\n\nThe user has been working with Claude Design and produced a **panorama / landscape map** of MCP adoption across internal tool services. The bundled design lives at `/tmp/design-bundle/agentcenter/project/MCP Adoption Kanban.html` (+ four `.jsx` partials). Key facts from the design + chat transcripts:\n\n- **Goal**: An internal-facing dashboard (lives inside AgentCenter) so leadership and engineers can see, at a glance, which traditional internal tools have shipped an MCP version, which are in development, and which don't need one — and spot leading vs. lagging organisations.\n- **Two layers**:\n  - **Industry Services** → 12 sectors (Wireless, Datacom, Cloud, Terminals, Optical, Carrier BG, Enterprise BG, Consumer BG, Digital Energy, Intelligent Auto, Smart City, Industrial). No PDT sub-layer.\n  - **Public Services** → 5 domains (AI R&D, Product & Software, Hardware, Product Digitization, Infrastructure), each with several PDTs. AI R&D has 6 named PDTs incl. Development Services with `IDE`, `CodeCheck`, `DT`. Product & Software has 8 PDTs.\n- **Three statuses** colour-coded: `released` (green) · `dev` (amber) · `none` (grey).\n- **Default view**: **Panorama** (every tool = one tile, grouped into sector / domain-PDT cards, each card has a coverage bar + leader/laggard tag). User explicitly **rejected** the original Kanban-as-default in favour of this landscape.\n- **User decisions for this iteration**: ship **Panorama + Grouped List** (skip Kanban + Heatmap), back the data with a **Drizzle table + seed** (not a static fixture), wire a sidebar entry from AgentCenter, and make **only `released` (green) tiles clickable** — they navigate to the marketplace MCP detail page.\n\nThe visual language is a 1:1 match with AgentCenter's existing Editorial Ivory theme (warm cream bg, Fraunces serif headers, Inter body, JetBrains Mono micro-text, accent `oklch(40% 0.09 35)`) — every CSS token in `app/assets/css/tailwind.css` already lines up with the design tokens in `mcp-kanban-ui.jsx`. The new screen needs **two extra tokens** for the layer-tinted accent: industry blue `oklch(48% 0.09 240)` and softened variants.\n\n---\n\n## Approach\n\n### 1 · Data model (Drizzle)\n\nAdd **one new schema file** `shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts` with three tables and one enum:\n\n```ts\nexport const mcpStatusEnum = pgEnum(\"mcp_status\", [\"none\", \"dev\", \"released\"]);\nexport const mcpLayerEnum  = pgEnum(\"mcp_layer\",  [\"industry\", \"public\"]);\n\n// Owner taxonomy lives in DB so ops can edit labels (zh) without code changes.\nmcpSectors        // industry sectors:  key (PK), label, labelZh, short, sortOrder\nmcpDomains        // public  domains:   key (PK), label, labelZh, short, sortOrder\nmcpPdts           // public  PDTs:      key (PK), domainKey (FK), label, labelZh, sortOrder\nmcpLandscapeTools // tools:             id (PK serial), name, slug (unique),\n                  //                    layer (mcpLayerEnum), ownerSector (FK→mcpSectors, null for public),\n                  //                    ownerDomain (FK→mcpDomains, null for industry),\n                  //                    ownerPdt (FK→mcpPdts, null for industry),\n                  //                    status (mcpStatusEnum), depsCount, adoptionPct,\n                  //                    blurb, blurbZh, tags (text[]),\n                  //                    extensionSlug (nullable — if a published MCP extension exists),\n                  //                    updatedAt\n```\n\nWhy three sub-tables vs. one polymorphic `owner_path` text column: cleaner FK integrity, lets us seed `sortOrder` per design ordering, and makes the join inside the layer-stats query trivial.\n\n**Index** by `(layer, ownerSector)` and `(layer, ownerDomain, ownerPdt)` for the panorama group queries.\n\nWire it into `shared/db/schema/index.ts`. Generate migration with `bun drizzle-kit generate`.\n\n### 2 · Seed data\n\nAdd `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` exporting the static taxonomy + tools (port from `mcp-kanban-data.jsx`, including all 12 sectors, 5 domains × ~30 PDTs, and the ~100 sample tools).\n\nAdd a one-shot seed script `scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts` (mirrors any existing seed pattern under `scripts/` if present; else stand-alone) that:\n1. Upserts sectors, domains, PDTs from the static taxonomy (idempotent).\n2. Truncates + inserts the tool rows.\n\nBilingual rule: sectors/domains/PDTs have `label` + `labelZh`; tool `name` is treated as a proper noun (single value, like `IDE`, `CodeCheck`); tool `blurb` gets `blurb` + `blurbZh`. The design also wants a landscape-scoped search. **Recommendation: keep the topbar search routing to `/extensions` as today, and put the landscape-scoped search inside the page's `SectionHeader` (next to the view switcher). One input per scope, no behavioural ambiguity.**\n\n3. **`adoptionPct` is fake.** The seed numbers (e.g. `IDE → 94%`) are illustrative. **Recommendation: render them as-is for v1 with a small \"estimate\" caption already present in the design's adoption bar; do not block on building a real install-events join.** Promote to real data once `installs` is producing a meaningful signal.\n\n4. **Dark theme.** The design only specifies light-mode colours. (Pick the right phase scope for the conventional commit prefix per `docs/plan.md` §13 — I'll match whatever phase tag you're currently on.)\n\n6. **Seed translation quality.** I'll write the Chinese labels for ~50 sectors/domains/PDTs and ~100 tool blurbs directly. They will be reasonable but not professionally translated. **Recommendation: ship them as the seed and add a `// TODO(translate): refine before GA` comment at the top of `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` so a human translator can do a pass later.** The alternative is leaving zh blank and falling back to English at runtime, which violates the \"EN/ZH from day one\" rule in CLAUDE.md.\n\n---\n\n## Out of scope (deferred)"

  "new_string": "Why three sub-tables vs. one polymorphic `owner_path` text column: cleaner FK integrity, lets us seed `sortOrder` per design ordering, and makes the join inside the layer-stats query trivial.\n\n**Status is derived, not stored.** Per your direction, the marketplace listing IS the release status:\n- `extensionId IS NOT NULL` → status = `released` (and the green tile links to that extension)\n- else `inDev = true` → status = `dev`\n- else → status = `none`\n\nThis lives in a tiny helper `deriveStatus(tool)` in `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` used by both the API response and any client consumer. When a publisher later ships an MCP-category extension and links it to a landscape tool (separate future PR — wires into the publish flow), the tool flips to `released` automatically with zero data drift.\n\n**Drop `adoptionPct`.** See concern #3 — the design's \"adoption %\" was a fabricated metric with no source. Removing it from the schema and the side-panel UI; the released status block keeps just the `StatusPill` + description. If/when real install data exists, we can add it back as a derived field over `installs`.\n\n**Index** by `(layer, ownerSector)` and `(layer, ownerDomain, ownerPdt)` for the panorama group queries; partial index on `extensionId` for joins."
}

> AGENT

Plan updated with all six resolutions. Quick summary of what changed in response to your feedback:

1. **Status ↔ marketplace listing.** Yes — much cleaner. Status is now **derived** from `extensionId` (FK → `extensions.id`) + an `inDev` boolean. No `status` enum stored on the tool. Seed creates ~30 thin marketplace MCP extension stubs in the existing `extensions` table so every "released" tile lands on a real listing. Future PR can wire the publish flow to auto-flip a tool to released when its linked extension goes live.

2. **One search**, in the page's `SectionHeader` — topbar search keeps its current `/extensions` behaviour everywhere.

3. **What `adoptionPct` meant**: in the design it's an *estimate* of internal penetration ("of teams that could use this MCP, what % do") shown as a progress bar in the side panel — but it has no real source and the seed numbers are made up. **I'm dropping it entirely** for v1: schema, side panel bar, and i18n key all gone. We can add it back later as a derived metric over the `installs` table once that produces real signal. (If you'd rather keep the visual element with a clearer label like "Internal usage" or just hide it behind a TODO, say so and I'll restore.)

4. **Dark theme** — every status / layer colour token now has a `:root` light value AND a `.dark` value in `tailwind.css`. Components reference variables only; no per-component branching.

5. **Multiple commits** — natural splits: schema+migration → seed taxonomy+tools → extension stubs → page+components+i18n → AgentCenter chrome wiring.

6. **Seed translation** — I'll write the Chinese myself with `// TODO(translate): refine before GA` at the top of the file.

Ready to exit plan mode unless #3 (dropping `adoptionPct`) needs more discussion.

> DEVELOPER

ok

  "plan": "# MCP Panorama\n\n## Context\n\nThe user has been working with Claude Design and produced a **panorama / landscape map** (renamed from \"MCP Adoption Panorama\" → just **\"MCP Panorama\"** per user direction) of MCP adoption across internal tool services. The bundled design lives at `/tmp/design-bundle/agentcenter/project/MCP Adoption Kanban.html` (+ four `.jsx` partials). Key facts from the design + chat transcripts:\n\n- **Goal**: An internal-facing dashboard (lives inside AgentCenter) so leadership and engineers can see, at a glance, which traditional internal tools have shipped an MCP version, which are in development, and which don't need one — and spot leading vs. lagging organisations.\n- **Two layers**:\n  - **Industry Services** → 12 sectors (Wireless, Datacom, Cloud, Terminals, Optical, Carrier BG, Enterprise BG, Consumer BG, Digital Energy, Intelligent Auto, Smart City, Industrial). No PDT sub-layer.\n  - **Public Services** → 5 domains (AI R&D, Product & Software, Hardware, Product Digitization, Infrastructure), each with several PDTs. AI R&D has 6 named PDTs incl. Development Services with `IDE`, `CodeCheck`, `DT`. Product & Software has 8 PDTs.\n- **Three statuses** colour-coded: `released` (green) · `dev` (amber) · `none` (grey).\n- **Default view**: **Panorama** (every tool = one tile, grouped into sector / domain-PDT cards, each card has a coverage bar + leader/laggard tag). User explicitly **rejected** the original Kanban-as-default in favour of this landscape.\n- **User decisions for this iteration**: ship **Panorama + Grouped List** (skip Kanban + Heatmap), back the data with a **Drizzle table + seed** (not a static fixture), wire a sidebar entry from AgentCenter, and make **only `released` (green) tiles clickable** — they navigate to the marketplace MCP detail page.\n\nThe visual language is a 1:1 match with AgentCenter's existing Editorial Ivory theme (warm cream bg, Fraunces serif headers, Inter body, JetBrains Mono micro-text, accent `oklch(40% 0.09 35)`) — every CSS token in `app/assets/css/tailwind.css` already lines up with the design tokens in `mcp-kanban-ui.jsx`. The new screen needs **two extra tokens** for the layer-tinted accent: industry blue `oklch(48% 0.09 240)` and softened variants.\n\n---\n\n## Approach\n\n### 1 · Data model (Drizzle)\n\nAdd **one new schema file** `shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts` with three tables and one enum:\n\n```ts\nexport const mcpStatusEnum = pgEnum(\"mcp_status\", [\"none\", \"dev\", \"released\"]);\nexport const mcpLayerEnum  = pgEnum(\"mcp_layer\",  [\"industry\", \"public\"]);\n\n// Owner taxonomy lives in DB so ops can edit labels (zh) without code changes.\nmcpSectors        // industry sectors:  key (PK), label, labelZh, short, sortOrder\nmcpDomains        // public  domains:   key (PK), label, labelZh, short, sortOrder\nmcpPdts           // public  PDTs:      key (PK), domainKey (FK), label, labelZh, sortOrder\nmcpLandscapeTools // tools:             id (PK serial), name, nameZh (nullable), slug (unique),\n                  //                    layer (mcpLayerEnum), ownerSector (FK→mcpSectors, null for public),\n                  //                    ownerDomain (FK→mcpDomains, null for industry),\n                  //                    ownerPdt (FK→mcpPdts, null for industry),\n                  //                    extensionId (text FK → extensions.id, nullable — single source of truth for \"released\"),\n                  //                    inDev boolean (true → \"dev\" status when no extension is linked),\n                  //                    depsCount,\n                  //                    blurb, blurbZh, tags (text[]),\n                  //                    updatedAt\n                  // CHECK (NOT (extensionId IS NOT NULL AND inDev = true))  -- can't be both\n```\n\nWhy three sub-tables vs. one polymorphic `owner_path` text column: cleaner FK integrity, lets us seed `sortOrder` per design ordering, and makes the join inside the layer-stats query trivial.\n\n**Status is derived, not stored.** Per your direction, the marketplace listing IS the release status:\n- `extensionId IS NOT NULL` → status = `released` (and the green tile links to that extension)\n- else `inDev = true` → status = `dev`\n- else → status = `none`\n\nThis lives in a tiny helper `deriveStatus(tool)` in `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` used by both the API response and any client consumer. When a publisher later ships an MCP-category extension and links it to a landscape tool (separate future PR — wires into the publish flow), the tool flips to `released` automatically with zero data drift.\n\n**Drop `adoptionPct`.** See concern #3 — the design's \"adoption %\" was a fabricated metric with no source. Removing it from the schema and the side-panel UI; the released status block keeps just the `StatusPill` + description. If/when real install data exists, we can add it back as a derived field over `installs`.\n\n**Index** by `(layer, ownerSector)` and `(layer, ownerDomain, ownerPdt)` for the panorama group queries; partial index on `extensionId` for joins.\n\nWire it into `shared/db/schema/index.ts`. Generate migration with `bun drizzle-kit generate`.\n\n### 2 · Seed data\n\nAdd `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` exporting the static taxonomy + tools (port from `mcp-kanban-data.jsx`, including all 12 sectors, 5 domains × ~30 PDTs, and the ~100 sample tools).\n\nAdd a one-shot seed script `scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts` (mirrors any existing seed pattern under `scripts/` if present; else stand-alone) that:\n1. Upserts sectors, domains, PDTs from the static taxonomy (idempotent).\n2. Upserts a small batch of **marketplace MCP extension stubs** (~30 records in the existing `extensions` table) — one per tool that should appear as `released`. Each stub has minimum required fields: `category='mcp'`, `scope='enterprise'`, `slug` matching the tool slug, bilingual `name`/`tagline`/`description`, ownerOrg = the seeded enterprise org. These are real, browsable marketplace listings; the green-tile click-through goes somewhere real.\n3. Truncates + inserts tool rows, setting `extensionId` for the released ones (via FK to the stubs from step 2) and `inDev=true` for the rest of the design's \"dev\" tools.\n\nSplitting this naturally falls into commits: (a) schema + migration, (b) seed taxonomy + tool data, (c) extension stubs + linkage.\n\n**Bilingual data rule** (DB-resident strings — every one must be seeded in both languages):\n- Sectors / domains / PDTs → `label` + `labelZh` (required).\n- Tool `name` → `name` (required, proper noun like `IDE`) + `nameZh` (optional — falls back to `name` when null; populated only where a Chinese name exists, e.g. `5G-Sim` → `5G 链路仿真器`).\n- Tool `blurb` → `blurb` + `blurbZh` (both required so the side panel + grouped list always render in the active locale).\n- Tool `tags` stay in English (used as keys, not display).\n\n**UI strings** (i18n keys — full coverage, no hardcoded English in templates):\n- `mcpPanorama.page.title` — \"MCP Panorama\" / \"MCP 全景图\"\n- `mcpPanorama.page.subtitleAll` / `.subtitleScoped` — \"{visible} of {total} tools in this layer\" with ICU plurals.\n- `mcpPanorama.layer.industry` / `.public` + `.industryDesc` / `.publicDesc`\n- `mcpPanorama.sidebar.serviceLayer` / `.sectors` / `.domainsPdts` / `.allTools` / `.pushToMcp` / `.pushToMcpHint`\n- `mcpPanorama.status.{none|dev|released}.{label,short,desc}` (e.g. released.label = \"MCP Released\" / \"MCP 已发布\")\n- `mcpPanorama.rank.{leading|onTrack|lagging|early}`\n- `mcpPanorama.summary.mcpActive` / `.released` / `.inDev` / `.noMcp` / `.leading` / `.lagging`\n- `mcpPanorama.card.{live,dev,none,liveSuffix}` for the inline counts\n- `mcpPanorama.view.{panorama,list}` — view switcher labels\n- `mcpPanorama.filter.all` / `.searchPlaceholder` / `.clear`\n- `mcpPanorama.detail.{mcpStatus,dependents,lastUpdate,owner,endpoint,downstream,empty,openInMarketplace,trackProgress,requestBuild,docs,close,notAvailable}`\n- `mcpPanorama.crumb.back` — \"← clear\" / \"← 清除\"\n- `sidebar.mcpPanorama.title` (\"MCP Panorama\") / `.subtitle` (\"Service landscape\" / \"服务全景\")\n- `nav.mcpPanorama` — top-bar link\n- `time.{today,daysAgo,weeksAgo,never}` for the \"Last update\" field with ICU plurals (so `\"2d\"` becomes \"2 days ago\" / \"2 天前\").\n\nThe static taxonomy in `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` includes Chinese for all 12 sectors, 5 domains, ~30 PDTs, and ~100 tool blurbs — I will translate seed values directly (a real translator can refine later, but no English will leak into the Chinese UI on day one).\n\n### 3 · Server API\n\n**Single endpoint** at `server/api/internal/mcp-landscape.get.ts` returns the entire layer's tree+tools in one round-trip (this is a dashboard, not a paginated browse):\n\n```ts\nGET ## Approved Plan:
# MCP Panorama

## Context

The user has been working with Claude Design and produced a **panorama / landscape map** (renamed from "MCP Adoption Panorama" → just **"MCP Panorama"** per user direction) of MCP adoption across internal tool services. The bundled design lives at `/tmp/design-bundle/agentcenter/project/MCP Adoption Kanban.html` (+ four `.jsx` partials). Key facts from the design + chat transcripts:

- **Goal**: An internal-facing dashboard (lives inside AgentCenter) so leadership and engineers can see, at a glance, which traditional internal tools have shipped an MCP version, which are in development, and which don't need one — and spot leading vs. lagging organisations.
- **Two layers**:
  - **Industry Services** → 12 sectors (Wireless, Datacom, Cloud, Terminals, Optical, Carrier BG, Enterprise BG, Consumer BG, Digital Energy, Intelligent Auto, Smart City, Industrial). No PDT sub-layer.
  - **Public Services** → 5 domains (AI R&D, Product & Software, Hardware, Product Digitization, Infrastructure), each with several PDTs. AI R&D has 6 named PDTs incl. Development Services with `IDE`, `CodeCheck`, `DT`. Product & Software has 8 PDTs.
- **Three statuses** colour-coded: `released` (green) · `dev` (amber) · `none` (grey).
- **Default view**: **Panorama** (every tool = one tile, grouped into sector / domain-PDT cards, each card has a coverage bar + leader/laggard tag). User explicitly **rejected** the original Kanban-as-default in favour of this landscape.
- **User decisions for this iteration**: ship **Panorama + Grouped List** (skip Kanban + Heatmap), back the data with a **Drizzle table + seed** (not a static fixture), wire a sidebar entry from AgentCenter, and make **only `released` (green) tiles clickable** — they navigate to the marketplace MCP detail page.

The visual language is a 1:1 match with AgentCenter's existing Editorial Ivory theme (warm cream bg, Fraunces serif headers, Inter body, JetBrains Mono micro-text, accent `oklch(40% 0.09 35)`) — every CSS token in `app/assets/css/tailwind.css` already lines up with the design tokens in `mcp-kanban-ui.jsx`. The new screen needs **two extra tokens** for the layer-tinted accent: industry blue `oklch(48% 0.09 240)` and softened variants.

---

## Approach

### 1 · Data model (Drizzle)

Add **one new schema file** `shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts` with three tables and one enum:

```ts
export const mcpStatusEnum = pgEnum("mcp_status", ["none", "dev", "released"]);
export const mcpLayerEnum  = pgEnum("mcp_layer",  ["industry", "public"]);

// Owner taxonomy lives in DB so ops can edit labels (zh) without code changes.
mcpSectors        // industry sectors:  key (PK), label, labelZh, short, sortOrder
mcpDomains        // public  domains:   key (PK), label, labelZh, short, sortOrder
mcpPdts           // public  PDTs:      key (PK), domainKey (FK), label, labelZh, sortOrder
mcpLandscapeTools // tools:             id (PK serial), name, nameZh (nullable), slug (unique),
                  //                    layer (mcpLayerEnum), ownerSector (FK→mcpSectors, null for public),
                  //                    ownerDomain (FK→mcpDomains, null for industry),
                  //                    ownerPdt (FK→mcpPdts, null for industry),
                  //                    extensionId (text FK → extensions.id, nullable — single source of truth for "released"),
                  //                    inDev boolean (true → "dev" status when no extension is linked),
                  //                    depsCount,
                  //                    blurb, blurbZh, tags (text[]),
                  //                    updatedAt
                  // CHECK (NOT (extensionId IS NOT NULL AND inDev = true))  -- can't be both
```

Why three sub-tables vs. one polymorphic `owner_path` text column: cleaner FK integrity, lets us seed `sortOrder` per design ordering, and makes the join inside the layer-stats query trivial.

**Status is derived, not stored.** Per your direction, the marketplace listing IS the release status:
- `extensionId IS NOT NULL` → status = `released` (and the green tile links to that extension)
- else `inDev = true` → status = `dev`
- else → status = `none`

This lives in a tiny helper `deriveStatus(tool)` in `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` used by both the API response and any client consumer. When a publisher later ships an MCP-category extension and links it to a landscape tool (separate future PR — wires into the publish flow), the tool flips to `released` automatically with zero data drift.

**Drop `adoptionPct`.** See concern #3 — the design's "adoption %" was a fabricated metric with no source. Removing it from the schema and the side-panel UI; the released status block keeps just the `StatusPill` + description. If/when real install data exists, we can add it back as a derived field over `installs`.

**Index** by `(layer, ownerSector)` and `(layer, ownerDomain, ownerPdt)` for the panorama group queries; partial index on `extensionId` for joins.

Wire it into `shared/db/schema/index.ts`. Generate migration with `bun drizzle-kit generate`.

### 2 · Seed data

Add `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` exporting the static taxonomy + tools (port from `mcp-kanban-data.jsx`, including all 12 sectors, 5 domains × ~30 PDTs, and the ~100 sample tools).

Add a one-shot seed script `scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts` (mirrors any existing seed pattern under `scripts/` if present; else stand-alone) that:
1. Upserts sectors, domains, PDTs from the static taxonomy (idempotent).
2. Upserts a small batch of **marketplace MCP extension stubs** (~30 records in the existing `extensions` table) — one per tool that should appear as `released`. Each stub has minimum required fields: `category='mcp'`, `scope='enterprise'`, `slug` matching the tool slug, bilingual `name`/`tagline`/`description`, ownerOrg = the seeded enterprise org. These are real, browsable marketplace listings; the green-tile click-through goes somewhere real.
3. Truncates + inserts tool rows, setting `extensionId` for the released ones (via FK to the stubs from step 2) and `inDev=true` for the rest of the design's "dev" tools.

Splitting this naturally falls into commits: (a) schema + migration, (b) seed taxonomy + tool data, (c) extension stubs + linkage.

**Bilingual data rule** (DB-resident strings — every one must be seeded in both languages):
- Sectors / domains / PDTs → `label` + `labelZh` (required).
- Tool `name` → `name` (required, proper noun like `IDE`) + `nameZh` (optional — falls back to `name` when null; populated only where a Chinese name exists, e.g. `5G-Sim` → `5G 链路仿真器`).
- Tool `blurb` → `blurb` + `blurbZh` (both required so the side panel + grouped list always render in the active locale).
- Tool `tags` stay in English (used as keys, not display).

**UI strings** (i18n keys — full coverage, no hardcoded English in templates):
- `mcpPanorama.page.title` — "MCP Panorama" / "MCP 全景图"
- `mcpPanorama.page.subtitleAll` / `.subtitleScoped` — "{visible} of {total} tools in this layer" with ICU plurals.
- `mcpPanorama.layer.industry` / `.public` + `.industryDesc` / `.publicDesc`
- `mcpPanorama.sidebar.serviceLayer` / `.sectors` / `.domainsPdts` / `.allTools` / `.pushToMcp` / `.pushToMcpHint`
- `mcpPanorama.status.{none|dev|released}.{label,short,desc}` (e.g. released.label = "MCP Released" / "MCP 已发布")
- `mcpPanorama.rank.{leading|onTrack|lagging|early}`
- `mcpPanorama.summary.mcpActive` / `.released` / `.inDev` / `.noMcp` / `.leading` / `.lagging`
- `mcpPanorama.card.{live,dev,none,liveSuffix}` for the inline counts
- `mcpPanorama.view.{panorama,list}` — view switcher labels
- `mcpPanorama.filter.all` / `.searchPlaceholder` / `.clear`
- `mcpPanorama.detail.{mcpStatus,dependents,lastUpdate,owner,endpoint,downstream,empty,openInMarketplace,trackProgress,requestBuild,docs,close,notAvailable}`
- `mcpPanorama.crumb.back` — "← clear" / "← 清除"
- `sidebar.mcpPanorama.title` ("MCP Panorama") / `.subtitle` ("Service landscape" / "服务全景")
- `nav.mcpPanorama` — top-bar link
- `time.{today,daysAgo,weeksAgo,never}` for the "Last update" field with ICU plurals (so `"2d"` becomes "2 days ago" / "2 天前").

The static taxonomy in `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` includes Chinese for all 12 sectors, 5 domains, ~30 PDTs, and ~100 tool blurbs — I will translate seed values directly (a real translator can refine later, but no English will leak into the Chinese UI on day one).

### 3 · Server API

**Single endpoint** at `server/api/internal/mcp-landscape.get.ts` returns the entire layer's tree+tools in one round-trip (this is a dashboard, not a paginated browse):

```ts
GET /api/internal/mcp-landscape?layer=industry|public
→ {
    layer,
    layerStats: { total, counts: { released, dev, none }, activePct, releasedPct },
    groups: [
      // industry: { kind: "sector",  key, label, labelZh, short, items, stats }
      // public:   { kind: "domain",  key, label, labelZh, short, items, pdts: [...], stats }
    ]
  }
```

Computes `coveragePct / activePct / lagPct / releasedPct` server-side using the same helper logic from `mcp-kanban-panorama.jsx#computeStats`. Locale-aware label resolution stays on the client (we always send both `label` and `labelZh`).

No mutation endpoints in v1 — landscape rows are seed-managed.

### 4 · Page + routing

Create `app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue` (locale-prefixed via existing `@nuxtjs/i18n` `prefix` strategy → `/en/mcp-panorama`, `/zh/mcp-panorama`).

The page **does not** use the default layout's chrome — the design has its own layer-toggle sidebar and section header. Define a **dedicated layout** `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue` (mirrors `default.vue` but swaps `Sidebar.vue` → `McpLayerSidebar.vue` and uses the existing `TopBar.vue` with one extra nav link for "MCP Panorama").

Page state (single Vue 3 `<script setup>`):
- `layer` (`'industry' | 'public'`, default `'public'` per design)
- `activePrimary`, `activeSecondary` (drill-down)
- `statusFilter` (`'all' | 'none' | 'dev' | 'released'`)
- `search` (debounced)
- `viewMode` (`'panorama' | 'list'`, persisted to `useState` only — no Tweaks panel in v1)
- `activeTool` (drives the side panel)

Data via `useFetch('/api/internal/mcp-landscape', { query: { layer } })`, re-fetched when `layer` changes. All filtering (search / status / drill-down) happens client-side over the in-memory tree.

--color-status-released:   oklch(58% 0.14 155);
--color-status-released-bg:oklch(95% 0.035 155);
--color-status-dev:        oklch(68% 0.16 65);
--color-status-dev-bg:     oklch(96% 0.04 80);
--color-status-none:       oklch(62% 0.012 60);
--color-status-none-bg:    oklch(96.5% 0.008 70);
--color-layer-industry:    oklch(48% 0.09 240);
--color-layer-industry-bg: oklch(94% 0.025 240);
--color-layer-public:      oklch(40% 0.09 35);
--color-layer-public-bg:   oklch(94% 0.025 70);

/* dark — same hues, surface-tinted backgrounds, lifted foregrounds for contrast */
--color-status-released:   oklch(72% 0.14 155);
--color-status-released-bg:oklch(22% 0.04 155);
--color-status-dev:        oklch(78% 0.14 65);
--color-status-dev-bg:     oklch(22% 0.04 65);
--color-status-none:       oklch(70% 0.012 240);
--color-status-none-bg:    oklch(20% 0.008 240);
--color-layer-industry:    oklch(75% 0.12 240);
--color-layer-industry-bg: oklch(22% 0.04 240);
--color-layer-public:      oklch(72% 0.18 200);  /* matches dark accent */
--color-layer-public-bg:   oklch(22% 0.04 70);
```

All tile / chip / pill / summary components reference the variables — no per-component theme branching, dark mode "just works" via the existing `.dark` selector toggle (`ThemeSwitch.vue`).

### 6 · Integration

1. **AgentCenter sidebar** (`app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue`): add a prominent callout block at the very top (above "Browse"), `bg-(--color-accent)/8`, with title `t('sidebar.mcpPanorama.title')` and subtitle `t('sidebar.mcpPanorama.subtitle')`, linking to `localePath('/mcp-panorama')`. Mirrors what the design did to AgentCenter itself.
2. **TopBar** (`app/components/layout/TopBar.vue`): add `MCP Panorama` link to the nav between `Explore` and `Publish`, highlighted on active route.
3. **i18n** (`i18n/locales/en.json` + `zh.json`): add the `mcpPanorama.*` section enumerated in §2 above, plus `sidebar.mcpPanorama.{title,subtitle}` and `nav.mcpPanorama`. Both files updated in lock-step (no untranslated keys in zh.json).

### 7 · Testing

- **Unit** (Vitest, matches `*.nuxt.test.ts` convention next to components): `LayerSummary.nuxt.test.ts` — verifies `activePct` / `releasedPct` math, leader/laggard pick. `ToolTile.nuxt.test.ts` — released renders `<a>`, dev/none render static `<span>` with `aria-disabled`. `LayerSidebar.nuxt.test.ts` — layer toggle resets drill-down; PDT row only visible when domain expanded.
- **API** (server test next to handler): one happy-path test per layer asserting the response shape, counts, and stats math against a small fixed seed.
- **Manual verification**: `bun dev`, then visit `/en/mcp-panorama`. Confirm:
  - Default layer is **Public**, default view is **Panorama**.
  - 12 sectors visible after toggling to Industry; AI R&D shows all 6 PDTs incl. Development Services with IDE / CodeCheck / DT tiles.
  - Green tiles navigate to `/en/extensions/<slug>` of the linked seeded extension stub (verify `IDE` opens a real listing); amber + grey tiles do not navigate and show no hover transform.
  - Status filter chips in the section header reduce the visible tile count without changing card structure.
  - Search "code" surfaces CodeCheck across the right group.
  - View switcher to "List" renders the 3-column-per-group grouped layout.
  - Side panel opens on tile click for any status; "Open in marketplace" appears only for released, "Track progress" for dev, "Request MCP build" for none. **No adoption bar visible** in any side panel.
  - Locale switch to Chinese renders sector / domain / PDT / status labels in Chinese; tool names with `nameZh` set show Chinese, others fall back to English.
  - Theme switch to Dark: status pills + tiles + layer-summary bars stay legible (no white-on-white, no oversaturated chips).
  - AgentCenter sidebar shows the "MCP Panorama · Service landscape" callout; clicking it from `/en/extensions` lands on the panorama.

---

## Critical files

**New**:
- `shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts`
- `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts`
- `scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts`
- `server/api/internal/mcp-landscape.get.ts`
- `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue`
- `app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue`
- `app/components/mcp-landscape/{LayerSidebar,SectionHeader,LayerSummary,PanoramaView,SectorCard,DomainCard,PdtBlock,CardHeader,ToolTile,GroupedListView,ToolCard,StatusPill,StatusChip,ToolDetailPanel}.vue`
- `drizzle/0005_mcp_landscape.sql` (generated)
- Test files alongside each non-trivial component + the API handler.

**Modified**:
- `shared/db/schema/index.ts` — re-export new schema.
- `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` — top callout block.
- `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — `MCP Panorama` nav link.
- `app/assets/css/tailwind.css` — status + layer colour tokens.
- `i18n/locales/en.json` + `zh.json` — `mcpPanorama.*`, `sidebar.mcpPanorama.*`, `nav.mcpPanorama`, and `time.*` (relative-time helpers) sections.

**Reused (no change)**:
- `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` chrome (search input, theme/locale switches, user button)
- `useLocalePath`, `useI18n`, `useFetch`, `useState`
- `lucide-vue-next` icons (`Factory`, `Globe2`, `LayoutGrid`, `List`, `ChevronDown`, `ChevronRight`, `X`, `Search`, `Sparkles`, `User`, `ArrowRight`, `Link2`)

---

---

## Resolved decisions (from review)

1. **Status ↔ marketplace listing.** Status is no longer a stored enum — it is **derived** from `extensionId` (FK to `extensions`) + an `inDev` boolean. Releasing a tool means linking it to a real marketplace MCP extension. Seed creates ~30 thin extension stubs in the existing `extensions` table so every green tile lands on a real listing.
2. **One search**, in the page's `SectionHeader` next to the view switcher. Topbar search keeps its current `/extensions` behaviour everywhere.
3. **`adoptionPct` removed** — see §4 of the schema. The number was a fabricated metric in the design; the side-panel "Adoption (estimate)" bar is dropped. The released-status block keeps the `StatusPill` + description text only. Add back later as a derived field over `installs` once that data exists.
4. **Dark theme supported** — every status / layer colour token has both a `:root` and `.dark` value (see §5 token list). All components reference the CSS variable, no per-component theme branching.
5. **Multiple commits expected.** Natural splits: (a) `feat(p?-db): mcp-landscape schema + migration`, (b) `feat(p?-mcp-panorama): seed taxonomy + tools + extension stubs`, (c) `feat(p?-mcp-panorama): page + components + i18n`, (d) `feat(p?-mcp-panorama): wire AgentCenter sidebar + topbar entry`. I'll match the current phase tag from `docs/plan.md`.
6. **Seed translation: my-pass + TODO marker.** I'll write Chinese for all sectors / domains / PDTs / tool blurbs directly and add `// TODO(translate): refine before GA` at the top of `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts`.

---

## Out of scope (deferred)

- Kanban + Heatmap views, Tweaks panel, layer-tinted accent toggle (panorama uses a static accent — the layer toggle itself already conveys context).
- Editing tools from the UI — landscape is seed-managed in v1.
- A real adoption % metric (was removed entirely from this PR — re-add later as a derived field over the `installs` table).
- Auto-flipping a tool's status to `released` from inside the publish flow (the schema supports it; the wiring is a future PR).
- Live "Push to MCP" / "Request MCP build" workflow (footer button is a no-op stub for v1).

```

409:**Total: ~17 days** to v1. Clickable demo URL by end of Phase 3 (~5 days in).
411:Each phase ends with a commit on its branch and a PR back to `main`. CI mirrors `bun run validate` (see §11). Tag the merge commit (`p0`, `p1`, …) to make per-phase rollback trivial.
448:`bun run validate` is the primary command — the fast inner loop that mirrors the first four steps of CI. It chains four steps in order, failing fast. The fifth gate, `bun run test:integration`, runs PGlite-backed repository tests; CI runs it on every PR but it's not part of `validate` (kept separate so the local typecheck/test loop stays under a few seconds). Phase 0 landed a working `bun run validate` against the bare scaffold; every subsequent phase keeps it green.
588:These cost ~1 extra hour of TS friction per phase and pay it back the first time a `(items[0] as Item).foo` would have crashed in production.
659:### Per-phase coverage discipline
661:Each phase PR ships:
684:The original Next.js project locked "Conventional Commits, one coherent unit per commit, phase scope." That carries over verbatim. This section spells out the parts that were left informal — granularity, breaking-change handling for `/api/v1`, merge strategy, the per-commit vs. per-PR validate split, and the hook tooling.
705:- **Phase scope** during the v1 build: `p<N>-<short>` matching §10. Example: `feat(p3-browse): wire filter chips to query string`. Use this for any commit that lands a phase deliverable.
712:A phase is one or several commits. Default to **one coherent unit per commit** — a single focused change that compiles and stands on its own. The exact number of commits per phase depends on the work: a mechanical port may be one commit per phase; a feature build may be several. Optimise for "the diff tells a clear story", not for a target commit count.
767:PRs merge as **merge commits**, not squash, not rebase. Each phase's commit history is preserved on `main`.
771:- The phase-internal commit split exists for a reason — squashing a phase into one commit destroys that.
778:### Hooks (Phase 0 deliverable)
780:Phase 0 installs:
801:2. Pause for an explicit human checkpoint at **phase boundaries**, not at every commit. Within a phase, commit at coherent-unit boundaries without pausing for each one — pause when surprised, when a decision is required, or at the end of the phase.
804:5. Each phase ends with an explicit human checkpoint; the agent does not auto-start the next phase.
809:A PR == one phase or one coherent post-v1 feature. PR title is a Conventional Commit summary (the same line that will appear in the merge commit). PR body:

405	| **12. Search FTS** | 0.5d | Apply `0002_fts_search_vector.sql` to dev DB, trgm indexes, top-bar search wired through `useFilters`. |

407	| **14. Deploy** | 1d | Node preset (`nitro: { preset: 'node-server' }`), env template, deploy runbook in `docs/deploy.md`, smoke test the CLI against the new URL (with `cli/src/config-store.ts` pointed at it). |
408	
409	**Total: ~17 days** to v1. Clickable demo URL by end of Phase 3 (~5 days in).
410	
411	Each phase ends with a commit on its branch and a PR back to `main`. CI mirrors `bun run validate` (see §11). Tag the merge commit (`p0`, `p1`, …) to make per-phase rollback trivial.
412	
413	### Status (2026-05-13)

413	### Status (2026-05-13)
414	
415	- **P0 – P13: shipped** in the original rewrite track (#1 – #9).
416	- **P14 (Deploy): pending.** The Node preset is already configured in `nuxt.config.ts`; the runbook in `docs/deploy.md` is up to date; what's left is wiring the production environment and flipping the CLI's default registry to point at it.
417	- **P14a – P20: rewrite-completion track, all merged.** A second pass closed gaps the audit found at the end of the initial rewrite:
418	  - **P14a** — trim `CLAUDE.md` to short-form, point to this plan for details (#10).
419	  - **P14** — import `cli/` as a standalone Bun project; ship a cross-platform Node JS bundle (#10).
420	  - **P15** — port shadcn-vue primitives backed by reka-ui (#10).
421	  - **P16** — filter drawer pickers: DeptPicker, TagDrawer, CreatorPicker, PublisherPicker + facets endpoint (#11).
422	  - **P17** — 4-step publish wizard with live preview + `publish/[id]/edit` resume flow (#11).
423	  - **P18** — `/profile` My Workspace page with 9 components + 3 internal endpoints (#12).
424	  - **P19** — split the detail page into hero / about / tabs / related + InstallCommand / ShareButton / SaveButton (#12).
425	  - **P20** — `nuxt-og-image` for social previews + Playwright browse / detail / navigation specs (#12).
426	
427	The "P14" label is reused intentionally — the original P14 (Deploy) never executed before the rewrite-completion track began, so P14a was named to avoid clashing with the unfinished P14 deploy work.
428	
429	### P1 (post-v1)
430	
431	- Publisher dashboard improvements (install metrics, version diffs)
432	- Collection sharing
433	- Ratings & reviews UI on detail page
434	- View events + trending calculation
435	- CLI installers for MCP / slash commands / plugins (in the carried-over CLI repo)
436	
437	### P2
438	
439	- MDX docs site at `/docs`
440	- SSO / SAML for enterprise sign-up
441	- Multi-tenant UI (org switcher, per-org branding)
442	- Meilisearch swap-in (only if Postgres FTS is hitting limits)
443	
444	---
445	
446	## 11. Validate pipeline
447	
448	`bun run validate` is the primary command — the fast inner loop that mirrors the first four steps of CI. It chains four steps in order, failing fast. The fifth gate, `bun run test:integration`, runs PGlite-backed repository tests; CI runs it on every PR but it's not part of `validate` (kept separate so the local typecheck/test loop stays under a few seconds). Phase 0 landed a working `bun run validate` against the bare scaffold; every subsequent phase keeps it green.
449	
450	```bash
451	bun run prepare           # nuxi prepare — generates .nuxt/types/* (needed for typecheck)
452	bun run lint              # @nuxt/eslint over the whole tree
453	bun run typecheck         # nuxi typecheck (vue-tsc) — handles .ts, .vue, auto-imports
454	bun run test              # vitest run — unit + contract suites
455	bun run test:integration  # vitest run -c vitest.config.integration.ts — PGlite repo suite (separate, slower)
456	```
457	
458	### Tools
459	
460	| Step | Tool | Why |
461	|---|---|---|
462	| Prepare | `nuxi prepare` | Generates type shims for auto-imports (`useFetch`, `useAsyncData`, `definePageMeta`, components, composables). Without it, `vue-tsc` errors on every auto-imported symbol. CI must run this before `typecheck`. |
```

1	import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
2	import {
3	  bigint,
4	  customType,
5	  index,
6	  integer,
7	  jsonb,
8	  numeric,
9	  pgEnum,
10	  pgTable,
11	  primaryKey,
12	  text,
13	  timestamp,
14	  unique,
15	} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
16	
17	import { users } from "./auth";
18	import { departments, organizations } from "./org";
19	
20	// tsvector is a Postgres-native type; Drizzle doesn't ship a built-in helper.
21	// We declare it as a custom type so the ORM can reference it in WHERE clauses
22	// via sql``. It is GENERATED ALWAYS (read-only); never written by app code.
23	const tsvector = customType<{ data: string }>({
24	  dataType() { return "tsvector"; },
25	});
26	
27	export const extensionCategoryEnum = pgEnum("extension_category", [
28	  "skills",
29	  "mcp",
30	  "slash",
31	  "plugins",
32	]);
33	
34	export const extensionScopeEnum = pgEnum("extension_scope", [
35	  "personal",
36	  "org",
37	  "enterprise",
38	]);
39	
40	export const extensionBadgeEnum = pgEnum("extension_badge", [
41	  "official",
42	  "popular",
43	  "new",
44	]);
45	
46	export const funcCatEnum = pgEnum("func_cat", [
47	  "workTask",
48	  "business",
49	  "tools",
50	]);
51	
52	export const visibilityEnum = pgEnum("extension_visibility", [
53	  "draft",
54	  "published",
55	  "archived",
56	]);
57	
58	export const versionStatusEnum = pgEnum("version_status", [
59	  "pending",
60	  "scanning",
61	  "ready",
62	  "rejected",
63	]);
64	
65	export const fileScanStatusEnum = pgEnum("file_scan_status", [
66	  "pending",
67	  "clean",
68	  "flagged",
69	]);
70	
71	export const extensions = pgTable(
72	  "extensions",
73	  {
74	    id: text().primaryKey(),
75	    slug: text().notNull().unique(),
76	    category: extensionCategoryEnum().notNull(),
77	    badge: extensionBadgeEnum(),
78	    scope: extensionScopeEnum().notNull(),
79	    // funcCat/subCat are nullable — the redesigned publish wizard does not
80	    // collect them; admin curation can backfill or system defaults apply.
81	    funcCat: funcCatEnum(),
82	    subCat: text(),
83	    l2: text(),
84	    publisherUserId: text().references(() => users.id, {
85	      onDelete: "set null",
86	    }),
87	    ownerOrgId: text()
88	      .notNull()
89	      .references(() => organizations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
90	    deptId: text().references(() => departments.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
91	    iconEmoji: text(),
92	    iconColor: text(),
93	    visibility: visibilityEnum().notNull().default("draft"),
94	    // i18n columns
95	    name: text().notNull(),
96	    nameZh: text(),
97	    tagline: text(),
98	    taglineZh: text(),
99	    description: text(),
100	    descriptionZh: text(),
101	    // Long-form markdown body for the detail page. Not duplicated to ZH yet —
102	    // most extensions will have an English README, with translations layered
103	    // on later if needed.
104	    readmeMd: text(),
105	    // metadata for detail page sidebar
106	    homepageUrl: text(),
107	    repoUrl: text(),
108	    licenseSpdx: text(),
109	    compatibilityJson: jsonb(),
110	    // Permissions captured during publish (network/files/runtime/data, etc.).
111	    // Surfaced on the detail page so users see what the extension wants.
112	    permissions: jsonb().notNull().default({}),
113	    // denormalized counters (updated by jobs)
114	    downloadsCount: integer().notNull().default(0),
115	    starsAvg: numeric({ precision: 2, scale: 1 }).notNull().default("0.0"),
116	    ratingsCount: integer().notNull().default(0),
117	    publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
118	    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
119	    updatedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
120	    searchVector: tsvector("search_vector"),
121	  },
122	  (t) => [
123	    index("idx_ext_category").on(t.category),
124	    index("idx_ext_scope").on(t.scope),
125	    index("idx_ext_func_sub_l2").on(t.funcCat, t.subCat, t.l2),
126	    index("idx_ext_dept_path").using(
127	      "btree",
128	      sql`${t.deptId} text_pattern_ops`,
129	    ),
130	    index("idx_ext_visibility").on(t.visibility),
131	    index("idx_ext_downloads").on(sql`${t.downloadsCount} DESC`),
132	    index("idx_ext_stars").on(sql`${t.starsAvg} DESC`),
133	  ],
134	);
135	
136	export const extensionVersions = pgTable(
137	  "extension_versions",
138	  {
139	    id: text().primaryKey(),
140	    extensionId: text()
141	      .notNull()
142	      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
143	    version: text().notNull(), // semver
144	    changelog: text(),
145	    changelogZh: text(),
146	    manifestJson: jsonb(),
147	    // Logical reference to files.id; no FK constraint to avoid a circular FK
148	    // with files.extensionVersionId. App code keeps these consistent.
149	    bundleFileId: text(),
150	    status: versionStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),
151	    // Source method chosen during publish: zip (only one wired today), git, cli.
152	    // Stored as text rather than enum so adding methods doesn't require a migration.
153	    sourceMethod: text().notNull().default("zip"),
154	    sourceMeta: jsonb().notNull().default({}),
155	    publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
156	    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
157	  },
158	  (t) => [unique("ext_version_unique").on(t.extensionId, t.version)],
159	);
160	
161	export const files = pgTable("files", {
162	  id: text().primaryKey(),
163	  extensionVersionId: text().references(() => extensionVersions.id, {
164	    onDelete: "set null",
165	  }),
166	  r2Key: text().notNull(),
167	  size: bigint({ mode: "bigint" }).notNull(),
168	  checksumSha256: text().notNull(),
169	  mimeType: text(),
170	  scanStatus: fileScanStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),
171	  scanReport: jsonb(),
172	  createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
173	});
174	
175	export const tags = pgTable("tags", {
176	  id: text().primaryKey(), // tag key, e.g. "real-time"
177	  labelEn: text().notNull(),
178	  labelZh: text().notNull(),
179	});
180	
181	export const extensionTags = pgTable(
182	  "extension_tags",
183	  {
184	    extensionId: text()
185	      .notNull()
186	      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
187	    tagId: text()
188	      .notNull()
189	      .references(() => tags.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
190	  },
191	  (t) => [
192	    primaryKey({ columns: [t.extensionId, t.tagId] }),
193	    index("idx_extension_tags_tag_ext").on(t.tagId, t.extensionId),
194	  ],
195	);

14	export const membershipRoleEnum = pgEnum("membership_role", [
15	  "viewer",

18	]);

20	export const organizations = pgTable("organizations", {

22	  slug: text().notNull().unique(),
23	  name: text().notNull(),
24	  nameZh: text(),
25	  logoUrl: text(),
26	  createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
27	});

29	export const departments = pgTable(
30	  "departments",
31	  {
32	    // Dotted-path identifier (e.g. "eng.cloud.infra"). Load-bearing: the
33	    // descendant lookup uses LIKE 'parent.%' against this column.
34	    id: text().primaryKey(),
35	    orgId: text()
36	      .notNull()
37	      .references(() => organizations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
38	    // Logical parent id; not a FK constraint to keep self-references simple.
39	    // The dotted-path id encodes the same structure.
40	    parentId: text(),
41	    name: text().notNull(),
42	    nameZh: text().notNull(),
43	    pathDepth: integer().notNull(),
44	    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
45	  },
46	  (t) => [
47	    index("idx_dept_path").using(
48	      "btree",
49	      sql`${t.id} text_pattern_ops`,
50	    ),
51	    index("idx_dept_org_parent").on(t.orgId, t.parentId),
52	  ],
53	);
54	
55	export const memberships = pgTable(
56	  "memberships",
57	  {
58	    id: text().primaryKey(),
59	    userId: text()
60	      .notNull()
61	      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
62	    orgId: text()
63	      .notNull()
64	      .references(() => organizations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
65	    deptId: text().references(() => departments.id, {
66	      onDelete: "set null",
67	    }),
68	    role: membershipRoleEnum().notNull().default("viewer"),
69	    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
70	  },
71	  (t) => [unique("memberships_user_org_unique").on(t.userId, t.orgId)],
72	);

1	import { sql } from "drizzle-orm"
2	import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js"
3	import postgres from "postgres"
4	
5	import { COLLECTIONS } from "../shared/data/collections"
6	import { DEPARTMENTS } from "../shared/data/departments"
7	import { EXTENSIONS } from "../shared/data/extensions"
8	import * as schema from "../shared/db/schema"
9	import {
10	  departments,
11	  extensions,
12	  extensionTags,
13	  memberships,
14	  organizations,
15	  tags,
16	  users,
17	} from "../shared/db/schema"
18	import { TAG_LABELS } from "../shared/tags"
19	import type { Department } from "../shared/types"
20	
21	const ORG_ID = "default"
22	
23	const CREATORS = [
24	  { id: "user-amy", email: "amy@agentcenter.dev", name: "Amy Chen" },
25	  { id: "user-ben", email: "ben@agentcenter.dev", name: "Ben Park" },
26	  { id: "user-cory", email: "cory@agentcenter.dev", name: "Cory Liu" },
27	  { id: "user-dao", email: "dao@agentcenter.dev", name: "Dao Tran" },
28	  { id: "user-eli", email: "eli@agentcenter.dev", name: "Eli Smith" },
29	  { id: "user-fei", email: "fei@agentcenter.dev", name: "Fei Wang" },
30	]
31	
32	interface FlatDept {
33	  id: string
34	  orgId: string
35	  parentId: string | null
36	  name: string
37	  nameZh: string
38	  pathDepth: number
39	}
40	
41	function flattenDepts(
42	  list: Department[],
43	  depth = 0,
44	  parentId: string | null = null,
45	  out: FlatDept[] = [],
46	): FlatDept[] {
47	  for (const d of list) {
48	    out.push({
49	      id: d.id,
50	      orgId: ORG_ID,
51	      parentId,
52	      name: d.name,
53	      nameZh: d.nameZh,
54	      pathDepth: depth,
55	    })
56	    if (d.children) flattenDepts(d.children, depth + 1, d.id, out)
57	  }
58	  return out
59	}
60	
61	function slugify(name: string): string {
62	  return name
63	    .toLowerCase()
64	    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
65	    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
66	}
67	
68	const CATEGORY_LABELS = {
69	  skills: "skill",
70	  mcp: "MCP server",
71	  slash: "slash command",
72	  plugins: "plugin",
73	} as const
74	
75	function generateReadme(ext: (typeof EXTENSIONS)[number]): string {
76	  const kind = CATEGORY_LABELS[ext.category]
77	  const installCmd = `agentcenter install ${slugify(ext.name)}`
78	  return `# ${ext.name}
79	
80	${ext.desc}
81	
82	## Overview
83	
84	${ext.name} is a ${kind} published by **${ext.author}**. It plugs into your
85	agent runtime so you can ${ext.desc.toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/, "")} without
86	hand-rolling integrations.
87	
88	## Install
89	
90	\`\`\`bash
91	${installCmd}
92	\`\`\`
93	
94	After installing, restart your agent to pick up the new ${kind}.
95	
96	## Compatibility
97	
98	- **Claude** — supported on the latest agent runtimes
99	- **Other agents** — install paths can be customized via \`agentcenter config\`
100	
101	## Tags
102	
103	${ext.tags.map((t) => `\`${t}\``).join(" · ")}
104	`
105	}
106	
107	async function main() {
108	  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL
109	  if (!url) {
110	    console.error("seed: DATABASE_URL is not set")
111	    process.exit(1)
112	  }
113	  const client = postgres(url)
114	  const db = drizzle(client, { schema, casing: "snake_case" })
115	
116	  console.log("seed: starting")
117	  console.log("seed: truncating catalog tables")
118	  await db.execute(
119	    sql`TRUNCATE TABLE ${organizations}, ${tags} RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE`,
120	  )

199	    userId: string
200	    orgId: string
201	    role: "publisher"
202	  }[] = []
203	  for (const row of extRows) {
204	    const key = `${row.publisherUserId}|${row.ownerOrgId}`
205	    if (seen.has(key)) continue
206	    seen.add(key)
207	    membershipRows.push({
208	      id: `mem-${row.publisherUserId}-${row.ownerOrgId}`,
209	      userId: row.publisherUserId,
210	      orgId: row.ownerOrgId,
211	      role: "publisher",
212	    })
213	  }
214	  console.log(`seed: inserting ${membershipRows.length} memberships`)
215	  await db.insert(memberships).values(membershipRows)
216	
217	  const extTagRows = EXTENSIONS.flatMap((e) =>
218	    e.tags.map((tagKey) => ({
219	      extensionId: `ext-${e.id}`,
220	      tagId: tagKey,
221	    })),
222	  )
223	  console.log(`seed: inserting ${extTagRows.length} extension-tag links`)
224	  await db.insert(extensionTags).values(extTagRows)
225	
226	  console.log(
227	    `seed: skipping ${COLLECTIONS.length} mock collections (user-owned, seeded post-signup in P9)`,
228	  )
229	
230	  console.log("seed: done")
231	  await client.end()
232	}
233	
234	main()
235	  .then(() => process.exit(0))
236	  .catch((err) => {
237	    console.error("seed: failed")
238	    console.error(err)
239	    process.exit(1)
```

// @vitest-environment nuxt
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest"
import { mountSuspended } from "@nuxt/test-utils/runtime"
import ThemeSwitch from "./ThemeSwitch.vue"

describe("ThemeSwitch", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    document.cookie = "theme=ivory; path=/"
  })

  it("renders Moon when theme is ivory", async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ThemeSwitch)
    expect(wrapper.find("svg.lucide-moon").exists()).toBe(true)
    expect(wrapper.find("svg.lucide-sun").exists()).toBe(false)
  })

  it("clicking the button writes theme=dark to the cookie", async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ThemeSwitch)
    await wrapper.find("button").trigger("click")
    expect(document.cookie).toContain("theme=dark")
  })

  it("aria-label reflects the next theme", async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(ThemeSwitch)
    expect(wrapper.find("button").attributes("aria-label")).toContain("dark")
  })
})
```

import * as extensionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/extensions"
import {
  parseFilters,
  searchParamsToInput,
} from "~~/shared/validators/filters"

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
  const url = getRequestURL(event)
  const filters = parseFilters(searchParamsToInput(url.searchParams))

  try {
    const db = useDb()
    const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
      extensionsRepo.findManyForList(db, filters),
      extensionsRepo.countFiltered(db, filters),
    ])
    return { items, total, filters }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[api/internal/extensions] db error:", err)
    throw createError({
      statusCode: 500,
      statusMessage: "Failed to load extensions",
    })
  }
})

import { drizzle, type PostgresJsDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js"
import postgres from "postgres"
import * as schema from "~~/shared/db/schema"

type Db = PostgresJsDatabase<typeof schema>

let _client: ReturnType<typeof postgres> | undefined
let _db: Db | undefined

export function useDb(): Db {
  if (_db) return _db
  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL
  if (!url) throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not set")
  _client = postgres(url, {
    max: 10,
    idle_timeout: 20,
    connect_timeout: 10,
    ...(process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" ? { ssl: "require" as const } : {}),
  })
  _db = drizzle(_client, { schema, casing: "snake_case" })
  return _db
}

export async function closeDb(): Promise<void> {
  if (_client) {
    await _client.end()
    _client = undefined
    _db = undefined
  }
}
```

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts",
  "content": "import { sql } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport {\n  boolean,\n  check,\n  index,\n  integer,\n  pgEnum,\n  pgTable,\n  serial,\n  text,\n  timestamp,\n} from \"drizzle-orm/pg-core\";\n\nimport { extensions } from \"./extension\";\n\nexport const mcpLayerEnum = pgEnum(\"mcp_layer\", [\"industry\", \"public\"]);\n\n// Industry sectors. PK is the design-time slug (\"wireless\", \"cloud\", …).\nexport const mcpSectors = pgTable(\"mcp_sectors\", {\n  key: text().primaryKey(),\n  label: text().notNull(),\n  labelZh: text().notNull(),\n  short: text().notNull(),\n  sortOrder: integer().notNull(),\n});\n\n// Public service domains (AI R&D, Hardware, …).\nexport const mcpDomains = pgTable(\"mcp_domains\", {\n  key: text().primaryKey(),\n  label: text().notNull(),\n  labelZh: text().notNull(),\n  short: text().notNull(),\n  sortOrder: integer().notNull(),\n});\n\n// PDTs (Product Development Teams) within a domain.\nexport const mcpPdts = pgTable(\n  \"mcp_pdts\",\n  {\n    key: text().primaryKey(),\n    domainKey: text()\n      .notNull()\n      .references(() => mcpDomains.key, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n    label: text().notNull(),\n    labelZh: text().notNull(),\n    sortOrder: integer().notNull(),\n  },\n  (t) => [index(\"idx_mcp_pdts_domain_sort\").on(t.domainKey, t.sortOrder)],\n);\n\n// One row per traditional internal tool service. Status is *derived* —\n// extensionId set ⇒ released; else inDev ⇒ dev; else none.\nexport const mcpLandscapeTools = pgTable(\n  \"mcp_landscape_tools\",\n  {\n    id: serial().primaryKey(),\n    slug: text().notNull().unique(),\n    name: text().notNull(),\n    nameZh: text(),\n    layer: mcpLayerEnum().notNull(),\n    ownerSector: text().references(() => mcpSectors.key, {\n      onDelete: \"restrict\",\n    }),\n    ownerDomain: text().references(() => mcpDomains.key, {\n      onDelete: \"restrict\",\n    }),\n    ownerPdt: text().references(() => mcpPdts.key, { onDelete: \"restrict\" }),\n    // Marketplace MCP listing. Non-null ⇒ tool is \"released\".\n    extensionId: text().references(() => extensions.id, {\n      onDelete: \"set null\",\n    }),\n    inDev: boolean().notNull().default(false),\n    depsCount: integer().notNull().default(0),\n    blurb: text().notNull(),\n    blurbZh: text().notNull(),\n    tags: text().array().notNull().default(sql`ARRAY[]::text[]`),\n    updatedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),\n  },\n  (t) => [\n    index(\"idx_mcp_tools_layer_sector\").on(t.layer, t.ownerSector),\n    index(\"idx_mcp_tools_layer_domain_pdt\").on(\n      t.layer,\n      t.ownerDomain,\n      t.ownerPdt,\n    ),\n    index(\"idx_mcp_tools_extension\").on(t.extensionId),\n    // A tool can be released or in-dev or neither — not both.\n    check(\n      \"mcp_tools_status_xor\",\n      sql`NOT (\"extension_id\" IS NOT NULL AND \"in_dev\" = true)`,\n    ),\n    // Industry tools own a sector; public tools own a domain (and usually a PDT).\n    check(\n      \"mcp_tools_owner_layer\",\n      sql`(layer = 'industry' AND owner_sector IS NOT NULL AND owner_domain IS NULL AND owner_pdt IS NULL)\n        OR (layer = 'public' AND owner_sector IS NULL AND owner_domain IS NOT NULL)`,\n    ),\n  ],\n);\n"
}

if (row.extensionId) return \"released\"\n  if (row.inDev) return \"dev\"\n  return \"none\"\n}\n\n/** Resolve the layer + group keys for a tool's `owner` path. */\nexport function ownerToParts(owner: string): {\n  layer: \"industry\" | \"public\"\n  primary: string\n  secondary?: string\n} {\n  if (owner.includes(\".\")) {\n    const [primary, secondary] = owner.split(\".\")\n    return { layer: \"public\", primary: primary!, secondary }\n  }\n  return { layer: \"industry\", primary: owner }\n}\n"

null,\n      tagline: t.blurb,\n      taglineZh: t.blurbZh,\n      description: t.blurb,\n      descriptionZh: t.blurbZh,\n      readmeMd: `# ${t.name}\\n\\nMCP server for **${t.name}** — ${t.blurb}.\\n\\n## Install\\n\\n\\`\\`\\`bash\\nagentcenter install ${t.slug}\\n\\`\\`\\`\\n`,\n      downloadsCount: 0,\n      starsAvg: \"0.0\",\n      ratingsCount: 0,\n      publishedAt: new Date(),\n    }\n  })\n  console.log(`seed: inserting ${mcpExtRows.length} mcp extension stubs`)\n  await db.insert(extensions).values(mcpExtRows)\n\n  // Landscape tool rows — extensionId points at the stub above for released\n  // ones; inDev=true for the rest of the dev-status tools.\n  const toolRows = MCP_TOOLS.map((t) => {\n    const parts = ownerToParts(t.owner)\n    return {\n      slug: t.slug,\n      name: t.name,\n      nameZh: t.nameZh ?? null,\n      layer: parts.layer,\n      ownerSector: parts.layer === \"industry\" ? parts.primary : null,\n      ownerDomain: parts.layer === \"public\" ? parts.primary : null,\n      ownerPdt:\n        parts.layer === \"public\" && parts.secondary\n          ? `${parts.primary}.${parts.secondary}`\n          : null,\n      extensionId: t.released ? `mcp-${t.slug}` : null,\n      inDev: t.inDev,\n      depsCount: t.depsCount,\n      blurb: t.blurb,\n      blurbZh: t.blurbZh,\n      tags: t.tags,\n    }\n  })\n  console.log(`seed: inserting ${toolRows.length} mcp landscape tools`)\n  await db.insert(mcpLandscapeTools).values(toolRows)\n\n  console.log(\"seed: done\")\n  await client.end()\n}"
}

import {
  listPublishedCreators,
  listPublishedPublishers,
  listTagsWithCounts,
  type CreatorFacet,
  type PublisherFacet,
  type TagFacet,
} from "~~/server/utils/queries/facets"

export default defineEventHandler(async (): Promise<{
  creators: CreatorFacet[]
  publishers: PublisherFacet[]
  tags: TagFacet[]
}> => {
  try {
    const [creators, publishers, tags] = await Promise.all([
      listPublishedCreators(),
      listPublishedPublishers(),
      listTagsWithCounts(),
    ])
    return { creators, publishers, tags }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[api/internal/facets] db error:", err)
    throw createError({
      statusCode: 500,
      statusMessage: "Failed to load filter facets",
    })
  }
})
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/utils/queries/mcp-landscape.ts",
  "content": "import { asc, eq, isNotNull } from \"drizzle-orm\"\n\nimport { useDb } from \"~~/server/utils/db\"\nimport { deriveStatus, type McpStatus } from \"~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape\"\nimport {\n  extensions,\n  mcpDomains,\n  mcpLandscapeTools,\n  mcpPdts,\n  mcpSectors,\n} from \"~~/shared/db/schema\"\n\nexport type Layer = \"industry\" | \"public\"\n\nexport interface ToolDto {\n  id: number\n  slug: string\n  name: string\n  nameZh: string | null\n  status: McpStatus\n  depsCount: number\n  blurb: string\n  blurbZh: string\n  tags: string[]\n  /** When status === \"released\", the marketplace listing slug. */\n  extensionSlug: string | null\n  ownerPrimary: string\n  ownerSecondary: string | null\n}\n\nexport interface StatusCounts {\n  released: number\n  dev: number\n  none: number\n}\n\nexport interface GroupStats {\n  total: number\n  counts: StatusCounts\n  releasedPct: number\n  activePct: number\n  lagPct: number\n}\n\ninterface SectorGroup {\n  kind: \"sector\"\n  key: string\n  label: string\n  labelZh: string\n  short: string\n  items: ToolDto[]\n  stats: GroupStats\n}\n\ninterface PdtBlock {\n  key: string\n  label: string\n  labelZh: string\n  items: ToolDto[]\n}\n\ninterface DomainGroup {\n  kind: \"domain\"\n  key: string\n  label: string\n  labelZh: string\n  short: string\n  items: ToolDto[]\n  pdts: PdtBlock[]\n  stats: GroupStats\n}\n\nexport type Group = SectorGroup | DomainGroup\n\nexport interface LayerPayload {\n  layer: Layer\n  layerStats: GroupStats\n  groups: Group[]\n}\n\nfunction blankCounts(): StatusCounts {\n  return { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0 }\n}\n\nfunction computeStats(items: { status: McpStatus }[]): GroupStats {\n  const counts = blankCounts()\n  for (const item of items) counts[item.status]++\n  const total = items.length\n  if (total === 0) {\n    return { total, counts, releasedPct: 0, activePct: 0, lagPct: 0 }\n  }\n  return {\n    total,\n    counts,\n    releasedPct: Math.round((counts.released / total) * 100),\n    activePct: Math.round(((counts.released + counts.dev) / total) * 100),\n    lagPct: Math.round((counts.none / total) * 100),\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function getLandscape(layer: Layer): Promise<LayerPayload> {\n  const db = useDb()\n  // One join across the four tables; pgsql returns ordered rows.\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      id: mcpLandscapeTools.id,\n      slug: mcpLandscapeTools.slug,\n      name: mcpLandscapeTools.name,\n      nameZh: mcpLandscapeTools.nameZh,\n      blurb: mcpLandscapeTools.blurb,\n      blurbZh: mcpLandscapeTools.blurbZh,\n      tags: mcpLandscapeTools.tags,\n      depsCount: mcpLandscapeTools.depsCount,\n      extensionId: mcpLandscapeTools.extensionId,\n      inDev: mcpLandscapeTools.inDev,\n      ownerSector: mcpLandscapeTools.ownerSector,\n      ownerDomain: mcpLandscapeTools.ownerDomain,\n      ownerPdt: mcpLandscapeTools.ownerPdt,\n      extensionSlug: extensions.slug,\n    })\n    .from(mcpLandscapeTools)\n    .leftJoin(extensions, eq(mcpLandscapeTools.extensionId, extensions.id))\n    .where(eq(mcpLandscapeTools.layer, layer))\n    .orderBy(asc(mcpLandscapeTools.id))\n\n  const tools: ToolDto[] = rows.map((r) => {\n    const status = deriveStatus({\n      extensionId: r.extensionId,\n      inDev: r.inDev,\n    })\n    const ownerPrimary = layer === \"industry\" ? r.ownerSector! : r.ownerDomain!\n    return {\n      id: r.id,\n      slug: r.slug,\n      name: r.name,\n      nameZh: r.nameZh,\n      status,\n      depsCount: r.depsCount,\n      blurb: r.blurb,\n      blurbZh: r.blurbZh,\n      tags: r.tags,\n      extensionSlug: status === \"released\" ? r.extensionSlug : null,\n      ownerPrimary,\n      ownerSecondary: r.ownerPdt,\n    }\n  })\n\n  const layerStats = computeStats(tools)\n\n  let groups: Group[]\n  if (layer === \"industry\") {\n    const sectors = await db\n      .select()\n      .from(mcpSectors)\n      .orderBy(asc(mcpSectors.sortOrder))\n\n    groups = sectors\n      .map((s): SectorGroup => {\n        const items = tools.filter((t) => t.ownerPrimary === s.key)\n        return {\n          kind: \"sector\" as const,\n          key: s.key,\n          label: s.label,\n          labelZh: s.labelZh,\n          short: s.short,\n          items,\n          stats: computeStats(items),\n        }\n      })\n      .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0)\n  } else {\n    const [domains, pdts] = await Promise.all([\n      db.select().from(mcpDomains).orderBy(asc(mcpDomains.sortOrder)),\n      db\n        .select()\n        .from(mcpPdts)\n        .where(isNotNull(mcpPdts.domainKey))\n        .orderBy(asc(mcpPdts.sortOrder)),\n    ])\n\n    groups = domains\n      .map((d): DomainGroup => {\n        const items = tools.filter((t) => t.ownerPrimary === d.key)\n        const domainPdts = pdts\n          .filter((p) => p.domainKey === d.key)\n          .map((p): PdtBlock => ({\n            key: p.key,\n            label: p.label,\n            labelZh: p.labelZh,\n            items: items.filter((t) => t.ownerSecondary === p.key),\n          }))\n          .filter((p) => p.items.length > 0)\n        return {\n          kind: \"domain\" as const,\n          key: d.key,\n          label: d.label,\n          labelZh: d.labelZh,\n          short: d.short,\n          items,\n          pdts: domainPdts,\n          stats: computeStats(items),\n        }\n      })\n      .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0)\n  }\n\n  return { layer, layerStats, groups }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/mcp-panorama.ts",
  "content": "// Types and helpers shared between the MCP Panorama API and its UI.\n\nimport type { McpStatus } from \"~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape\"\n\nexport type Layer = \"industry\" | \"public\"\n\nexport interface ToolDto {\n  id: number\n  slug: string\n  name: string\n  nameZh: string | null\n  status: McpStatus\n  depsCount: number\n  blurb: string\n  blurbZh: string\n  tags: string[]\n  /** When status === \"released\", the marketplace listing slug. */\n  extensionSlug: string | null\n  ownerPrimary: string\n  ownerSecondary: string | null\n}\n\nexport interface StatusCounts {\n  released: number\n  dev: number\n  none: number\n}\n\nexport interface GroupStats {\n  total: number\n  counts: StatusCounts\n  releasedPct: number\n  activePct: number\n  lagPct: number\n}\n\nexport interface PdtBlock {\n  key: string\n  label: string\n  labelZh: string\n  items: ToolDto[]\n}\n\nexport interface SectorGroup {\n  kind: \"sector\"\n  key: string\n  label: string\n  labelZh: string\n  short: string\n  items: ToolDto[]\n  stats: GroupStats\n}\n\nexport interface DomainGroup {\n  kind: \"domain\"\n  key: string\n  label: string\n  labelZh: string\n  short: string\n  items: ToolDto[]\n  pdts: PdtBlock[]\n  stats: GroupStats\n}\n\nexport type Group = SectorGroup | DomainGroup\n\nexport interface LayerPayload {\n  layer: Layer\n  layerStats: GroupStats\n  groups: Group[]\n}\n\nexport type RankKey = \"leading\" | \"onTrack\" | \"lagging\" | \"early\"\n\n/** Returns the rank label for a group's stats — null if too small or middling. */\nexport function rankFor(stats: GroupStats): RankKey | null {\n  if (stats.total < 3) return null\n  if (stats.releasedPct >= 75) return \"leading\"\n  if (stats.releasedPct >= 50) return \"onTrack\"\n  if (stats.lagPct >= 50) return \"lagging\"\n  if (stats.releasedPct < 25) return \"early\"\n  return null\n}\n\nexport const STATUS_ORDER: McpStatus[] = [\"none\", \"dev\", \"released\"]\n\n/** Localised display name for a tool (Chinese fallback to English when null). */\nexport function toolDisplayName(tool: ToolDto, locale: string): string {\n  if (locale === \"zh\" && tool.nameZh) return tool.nameZh\n  return tool.name\n}\n\n/** Localised display blurb. */\nexport function toolDisplayBlurb(tool: ToolDto, locale: string): string {\n  return locale === \"zh\" ? tool.blurbZh : tool.blurb\n}\n\n/** Localised group title. */\nexport function groupDisplayTitle(g: Group, locale: string): string {\n  return locale === \"zh\" ? g.labelZh : g.label\n}\n\nexport function pdtDisplayTitle(p: PdtBlock, locale: string): string {\n  return locale === \"zh\" ? p.labelZh : p.label\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolTile.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { ChevronRight } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\nimport { toolDisplayName, type ToolDto } from \"~~/shared/mcp-panorama\"\n\nconst props = withDefaults(\n  defineProps<{\n    tool: ToolDto\n    active?: boolean\n    /** Compact = used inside PdtBlock or dense panorama mode. */\n    compact?: boolean\n  }>(),\n  { active: false, compact: false },\n)\n\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ pick: [ToolDto] }>()\n\nconst { locale, t } = useI18n()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\nconst displayName = computed(() => toolDisplayName(props.tool, locale.value))\nconst isReleased = computed(() => props.tool.status === \"released\")\nconst showDeps = computed(() => props.tool.depsCount >= 10)\n\nconst tooltip = computed(() => {\n  const statusLabel = t(`mcpPanorama.status.${props.tool.status}.label`)\n  if (isReleased.value) return `${displayName.value} · ${t(\"mcpPanorama.detail.openInMarketplace\")} →`\n  return `${displayName.value} · ${statusLabel} (${t(\"mcpPanorama.detail.notAvailable\")})`\n})\n\nconst baseClass = computed(() => [\n  \"group inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md text-[12px] leading-tight font-medium tracking-tight no-underline relative shrink-0 transition-all border\",\n  // status colors\n  props.tool.status === \"released\"\n    && \"bg-(--color-status-released-bg) text-(--color-status-released) border-(--color-status-released)/20 border-l-[3px] border-l-(--color-status-released)\",\n  props.tool.status === \"dev\"\n    && \"bg-(--color-status-dev-bg) text-(--color-status-dev) border-(--color-status-dev)/20 border-l-[3px] border-l-(--color-status-dev)\",\n  props.tool.status === \"none\"\n    && \"bg-(--color-status-none-bg) text-(--color-status-none) border-(--color-status-none)/20 border-l-[3px] border-l-(--color-status-none)\",\n  // density\n  props.compact ? \"px-[7px] py-[3px] text-[11px]\" : \"px-[9px] py-[5px]\",\n  // active ring\n  props.active && isReleased.value && \"ring-2 ring-(--color-status-released)/30\",\n  // hover (only when clickable)\n  isReleased.value && \"cursor-pointer hover:-translate-y-px hover:border-(--color-status-released) hover:shadow-[0_4px_12px_-4px] hover:shadow-(--color-status-released)/40\",\n])\n\nfunction onClick(e: MouseEvent) {\n  // Allow native open-in-new-tab for cmd/ctrl/shift/middle-click.\n  if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.button === 1) return\n  emit(\"pick\", props.tool)\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <NuxtLink\n    v-if=\"isReleased && tool.extensionSlug\"\n    :to=\"localePath(`/extensions/${tool.extensionSlug}`)\"\n    :title=\"tooltip\"\n    :class=\"baseClass\"\n    @click=\"onClick\"\n  >\n    <span class=\"whitespace-nowrap shrink-0\">{{ displayName }}</span>\n    <span\n      v-if=\"showDeps\"\n      class=\"font-mono text-[9px] font-semibold rounded px-1 py-0 bg-(--color-status-released) text-(--color-card)\"\n    >{{ tool.depsCount }}</span>\n    <ChevronRight :size=\"9\" class=\"shrink-0 opacity-70\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n  </NuxtLink>\n  <span\n    v-else\n    :title=\"tooltip\"\n    aria-disabled=\"true\"\n    :class=\"[...baseClass, 'select-none cursor-default']\"\n  >\n    <span class=\"whitespace-nowrap shrink-0\">{{ displayName }}</span>\n    <span\n      v-if=\"showDeps\"\n      class=\"font-mono text-[9px] font-semibold rounded px-1 py-0 text-(--color-card)\"\n      :class=\"[\n        tool.status === 'dev' && 'bg-(--color-status-dev)',\n        tool.status === 'none' && 'bg-(--color-status-none)',\n      ]\"\n    >{{ tool.depsCount }}</span>\n  </span>\n</template>\n"
}

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { Group, GroupStats, Layer, ToolDto } from \"~~/shared/mcp-panorama\"\nimport DomainCard from \"./DomainCard.vue\"\nimport LayerSummary from \"./LayerSummary.vue\"\nimport SectorCard from \"./SectorCard.vue\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  layer: Layer\n  stats: GroupStats\n  groups: Group[]\n  activeId: number | null\n}>()\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ pick: [ToolDto] }>()\n\nconst gridCols = computed(() =>\n  props.layer === \"industry\"\n    ? \"repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))\"\n    : \"repeat(auto-fit, minmax(520px, 1fr))\",\n)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"px-7 pb-7 flex flex-col gap-5\">\n    <LayerSummary :layer=\"layer\" :stats=\"stats\" :groups=\"groups\" />\n    <div class=\"grid gap-3.5 items-start\" :style=\"{ gridTemplateColumns: gridCols }\">\n      <template v-for=\"g in groups\" :key=\"g.key\">\n        <SectorCard\n          v-if=\"g.kind === 'sector'\"\n          :group=\"g\"\n          :active-id=\"activeId\"\n          @pick=\"(t) => emit('pick', t)\"\n        />\n        <DomainCard\n          v-else\n          :group=\"g\"\n          :active-id=\"activeId\"\n          @pick=\"(t) => emit('pick', t)\"\n        />\n      </template>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}
