  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport {\n  groupDisplayTitle,\n  pdtDisplayTitle,\n  STATUS_ORDER,\n  type Group,\n  type Layer,\n  type ToolDto,\n} from \"~~/shared/mcp-panorama\"\nimport ToolCard from \"./ToolCard.vue\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  layer: Layer\n  groups: Group[]\n  activeId: number | null\n}>()\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ pick: [ToolDto] }>()\n\nconst { locale, t } = useI18n()\n\ninterface FlatGroup {\n  key: string\n  title: string\n  subtitle?: string\n  items: ToolDto[]\n}\n\nconst flatGroups = computed<FlatGroup[]>(() => {\n  if (props.layer === \"industry\") {\n    return props.groups.map((g) => ({\n      key: g.key,\n      title: groupDisplayTitle(g, locale.value),\n      items: g.items,\n    }))\n  }\n  // Public: split each domain by PDT into separate sections.\n  const out: FlatGroup[] = []\n  for (const g of props.groups) {\n    if (g.kind !== \"domain\") continue\n    for (const p of g.pdts) {\n      out.push({\n        key: `${g.key}.${p.key}`,\n        title: groupDisplayTitle(g, locale.value),\n        subtitle: pdtDisplayTitle(p, locale.value),\n        items: p.items,\n      })\n    }\n  }\n  return out\n})\n\nfunction itemsByStatus(items: ToolDto[], status: typeof STATUS_ORDER[number]) {\n  return items.filter((t) => t.status === status)\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"px-7 pb-7 flex flex-col gap-5\">\n    <section v-for=\"g in flatGroups\" :key=\"g.key\">\n      <div class=\"flex items-baseline justify-between px-1 pb-2.5\">\n        <div class=\"flex items-baseline gap-2.5\">\n          <h3 class=\"font-serif text-[20px] font-medium tracking-tight m-0 text-(--color-ink)\">\n            {{ g.title }}\n          </h3>\n          <span v-if=\"g.subtitle\" class=\"font-mono text-[13px] text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n            / {{ g.subtitle }}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2.5\">\n          <div class=\"flex h-1.5 w-[120px] rounded overflow-hidden bg-(--color-border)\">\n            <template v-for=\"s in STATUS_ORDER\" :key=\"s\">\n              <div\n                v-if=\"itemsByStatus(g.items, s).length > 0\"\n                :class=\"[\n                  s === 'released' && 'bg-(--color-status-released)',\n                  s === 'dev' && 'bg-(--color-status-dev)',\n                  s === 'none' && 'bg-(--color-status-none)',\n                ]\"\n                :style=\"{ width: `${(itemsByStatus(g.items, s).length / g.items.length) * 100}%` }\"\n              />\n            </template>\n          </div>\n          <span class=\"font-mono text-[11px] text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n            {{ t(\"mcpPanorama.detail.depsCount\", { count: g.items.length }) }}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div class=\"grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 border border-(--color-border) rounded-xl p-3 bg-(--color-card)\">\n        <div v-for=\"s in STATUS_ORDER\" :key=\"s\" class=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          <div class=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 pb-2 border-b border-dashed border-(--color-border)\">\n            <span\n              class=\"size-[6px] rounded-full\"\n              :class=\"[\n                s === 'released' && 'bg-(--color-status-released)',\n                s === 'dev' && 'bg-(--color-status-dev)',\n                s === 'none' && 'bg-(--color-status-none)',\n              ]\"\n            />\n            <span class=\"font-mono text-[11px] text-(--color-ink-muted) tracking-wide\">\n              {{ t(`mcpPanorama.status.${s}.short`) }} · {{ itemsByStatus(g.items, s).length }}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          <span\n            v-if=\"itemsByStatus(g.items, s).length === 0\"\n            class=\"font-mono text-[11px] text-(--color-ink-muted) italic pt-1 opacity-60\"\n          >—</span>\n          <ToolCard\n            v-for=\"tool in itemsByStatus(g.items, s)\"\n            :key=\"tool.id\"\n            :tool=\"tool\"\n            :selected=\"activeId === tool.id\"\n            @pick=\"(t) => emit('pick', t)\"\n          />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/mcp-landscape/StatusChip.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { McpStatus } from \"~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  /** Status key, or \"all\". */\n  value: McpStatus | \"all\"\n  label: string\n  count: number\n  active: boolean\n}>()\n\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ click: [] }>()\n\nconst dotClass = computed(() => {\n  switch (props.value) {\n    case \"released\": return \"bg-(--color-status-released)\"\n    case \"dev\": return \"bg-(--color-status-dev)\"\n    case \"none\": return \"bg-(--color-status-none)\"\n    default: return \"\"\n  }\n})\n\nconst activeClass = computed(() => {\n  if (!props.active) return \"border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) text-(--color-ink)\"\n  switch (props.value) {\n    case \"released\": return \"border-(--color-status-released) bg-(--color-status-released-bg) text-(--color-status-released)\"\n    case \"dev\": return \"border-(--color-status-dev) bg-(--color-status-dev-bg) text-(--color-status-dev)\"\n    case \"none\": return \"border-(--color-status-none) bg-(--color-status-none-bg) text-(--color-status-none)\"\n    default: return \"border-(--color-accent) bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent)\"\n  }\n})\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <button\n    type=\"button\"\n    class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 border rounded-full text-[12px] cursor-pointer transition\"\n    :class=\"[activeClass, active ? 'font-semibold' : 'font-medium']\"\n    @click=\"emit('click')\"\n  >\n    <span v-if=\"value !== 'all'\" class=\"size-[6px] rounded-full\" :class=\"dotClass\" />\n    <span>{{ label }}</span>\n    <span\n      class=\"font-mono text-[10px] px-1.5 py-[1px] rounded-full\"\n      :class=\"active ? 'bg-transparent' : 'bg-(--color-bg) text-(--color-ink-muted)'\"\n    >\n      {{ count }}\n    </span>\n  </button>\n</template>\n"
}

\"./StatusChip.vue\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  layer: Layer\n  activePrimary: string | null\n  activeSecondary: string | null\n  visibleCounts: { total: number; released: number; dev: number; pdt.labelZh : pdt.label\n      return `${primaryLabel} · ${pdtLabel}`\n    }\n  }\n  return primaryLabel\n})\n\nconst ownerLayer = computed<\"industry\" | \"public\">(() => {\n  if (!props.tool) return \"public\"\n  return props.tool.ownerSecondary ? \"public\" : props.groups.find((g) => g.key === props.tool?.ownerPrimary)?.kind === \"domain\" ? \"public\" : \"industry\"\n})\n\nconst endpoint = computed(() =>\n  props.tool && props.tool.status === \"released\"\n    ? === 'industry'\" :size=\"10\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n              <Globe2 v-else :size=\"10\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n              {{ t(`mcpPanorama.layer.${ownerLayer}Short`) }}\n            </span>\n            <h2 class=\"font-serif text-[28px] font-medium text-(--color-ink) tracking-tight leading-[1.1] m-0\">\n              {{ displayName }}\n            </h2>\n            <div class=\"text-[13px] text-(--color-ink) leading-snug\">\n          {{ t(`mcpPanorama.status.${tool.status}.desc`) }}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Meta grid -->\n      <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-b border-(--color-border) grid grid-cols-2 gap-y-3.5 gap-x-4\">\n        <div class=\"flex flex-col gap-1 min-w-0\">\n          <span class=\"font-mono text-[10px] tracking-wide uppercase text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n            {{ t(\"mcpPanorama.detail.dependents\") }}\n          </span>\n          <span class=\"text-[13px] text-(--color-ink) truncate\">\n            {{   "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { McpStatus } from \"~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape\"\nimport type { Group, Layer, LayerPayload, ToolDto } from \"~~/shared/mcp-panorama\"\n\ndefinePageMeta({ layout: \"mcp-panorama\" })\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\nconst head = useLocaleHead()\nuseHead(() => ({\n  title: t(\"mcpPanorama.page.title\"),\n  htmlAttrs: head.value.htmlAttrs ?? {},\n}))\n\nconst layer = ref<Layer>(\"public\")\nconst activePrimary = ref<string | null>(null)\nconst activeSecondary = ref<string | null>(null)\nconst statusFilter = ref<\"all\" | McpStatus>(\"all\")\nconst search = ref(\"\")\nconst viewMode = ref<\"panorama\" | \"list\">(\"panorama\")\nconst activeTool = ref<ToolDto | null>(null)\n\n// Re-fetch when layer changes; everything else filters client-side.\nconst { data, pending, error, refresh } = await useFetch<LayerPayload>(\n  \"/api/internal/mcp-landscape\",\n  {\n    query: { layer },\n    key: \"mcp-landscape\",\n  },\n)\n\nwatch(layer, () => {\n  activePrimary.value = null\n  activeSecondary.value = null\n  activeTool.value = null\n})\n\n// All tools for the current layer (used for derived counts).\nconst allTools = computed<ToolDto[]>(() =>\n  data.value ? data.value.groups.flatMap((g) => g.items) : [],\n)\n\n// Filter individual tools (drill-down + status + search).\nfunction filterTool(tool: ToolDto): boolean {\n  if (activePrimary.value && tool.ownerPrimary !== activePrimary.value) return false\n  if (activeSecondary.value && tool.ownerSecondary !== activeSecondary.value) return false\n  if (statusFilter.value !== \"all\" && tool.status !== statusFilter.value) return false\n  if (search.value.trim()) {\n    const q = search.value.trim().toLowerCase()\n    const hay =\n      tool.name.toLowerCase()\n      + \" \"\n      + (tool.nameZh ?? \"\").toLowerCase()\n      + \" \"\n      + tool.blurb.toLowerCase()\n      + \" \"\n      + tool.blurbZh.toLowerCase()\n      + \" \"\n      + tool.tags.join(\" \")\n    if (!hay.includes(q)) return false\n  }\n  return true\n}\n\n// Re-shape groups with filtered items, dropping empty groups/PDTs.\nconst filteredGroups = computed<Group[]>(() => {\n  if (!data.value) return []\n  return data.value.groups\n    .map((g): Group => {\n      const items = g.items.filter(filterTool)\n      if (g.kind === \"sector\") {\n        return { ...g, items, stats: computeStats(items) }\n      }\n      const pdts = g.pdts\n        .map((p) => ({ ...p, items: p.items.filter(filterTool) }))\n        .filter((p) => p.items.length > 0)\n      return { ...g, items, pdts, stats: computeStats(items) }\n    })\n    .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0)\n})\n\nfunction computeStats(items: ToolDto[]) {\n  const counts = { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0 }\n  for (const it of items) counts[it.status]++\n  const total = items.length\n  if (total === 0) {\n    return { total, counts, releasedPct: 0, activePct: 0, lagPct: 0 }\n  }\n  return {\n    total,\n    counts,\n    releasedPct: Math.round((counts.released / total) * 100),\n    activePct: Math.round(((counts.released + counts.dev) / total) * 100),\n    lagPct: Math.round((counts.none / total) * 100),\n  }\n}\n\n// Visible counts for the section header subtitle and filter chips. These\n// reflect search + drill-down but ignore the status filter — chips show how\n// many tools each status would surface if selected.\nconst visibleCounts = computed(() => {\n  const counts = { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0, total: 0 }\n  if (!data.value) return counts\n  for (const tool of allTools.value) {\n    if (activePrimary.value && tool.ownerPrimary !== activePrimary.value) continue\n    if (activeSecondary.value && tool.ownerSecondary !== activeSecondary.value) continue\n    if (search.value.trim()) {\n      const q = search.value.trim().toLowerCase()\n      const hay =\n        tool.name.toLowerCase() + \" \" + tool.blurb.toLowerCase() + \" \" + tool.blurbZh.toLowerCase()\n      if (!hay.includes(q)) continue\n    }\n    counts[tool.status]++\n    counts.total++\n  }\n  return counts\n})\n\nconst totals = computed(() => ({ total: data.value?.layerStats.total ?? 0 }))\n\nfunction setActive(primary: string | null, secondary: string | null) {\n  activePrimary.value = primary\n  activeSecondary.value = secondary\n  activeTool.value = null\n}\n\nfunction clearDrill() {\n  activePrimary.value = null\n  activeSecondary.value = null\n}\n\nfunction pickTool(tool: ToolDto) {\n  activeTool.value = tool\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <!-- Sidebar -->\n  <ClientOnly>\n    <McpLandscapeLayerSidebar\n      v-if=\"data\"\n      :layer=\"layer\"\n      :groups=\"data.groups\"\n      :total-count=\"totals.total\"\n      :active-primary=\"activePrimary\"\n      :active-secondary=\"activeSecondary\"\n      @update:layer=\"(l) => (layer = l)\"\n      @set-active=\"setActive\"\n    />\n    <template #fallback>\n      <div class=\"w-[268px] shrink-0 border-r border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-sidebar)\" />\n    </template>\n  </ClientOnly>\n\n  <!-- Main + side panel -->\n  <div class=\"flex-1 overflow-auto bg-(--color-bg) relative\">\n    <McpLandscapeSectionHeader\n      v-if=\"data\"\n      :layer=\"layer\"\n      :active-primary=\"activePrimary\"\n      :active-secondary=\"activeSecondary\"\n      :visible-counts=\"visibleCounts\"\n      :totals=\"totals\"\n      :status-filter=\"statusFilter\"\n      :view-mode=\"viewMode\"\n      :search=\"search\"\n      :groups=\"data.groups\"\n      @update:status-filter=\"(v) => (statusFilter = v)\"\n      @update:view-mode=\"(v) => (viewMode = v)\"\n      @update:search=\"(v) => (search = v)\"\n      @clear-drill=\"clearDrill\"\n    />\n\n    <div v-if=\"pending && !data\" class=\"px-7 pb-7 pt-2 text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n      <div class=\"h-6 w-40 rounded bg-(--color-border) animate-pulse\" />\n    </div>\n\n    <div v-if=\"error\" class=\"px-7 pb-7 pt-2\">\n      <div class=\"text-(--color-ink-muted) text-[13px] mb-2\">{{ t(\"mcpPanorama.page.errorLoad\") }}</div>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        class=\"px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg) text-[12px] font-medium cursor-pointer\"\n        @click=\"refresh()\"\n      >\n        {{ t(\"mcpPanorama.page.retry\") }}\n      </button>\n    </div>\n\n    <McpLandscapePanoramaView\n      v-if=\"data && viewMode === 'panorama'\"\n      :layer=\"layer\"\n      :stats=\"filteredGroups.length === data.groups.length && statusFilter === 'all' && !activePrimary && !search.trim()\n        ? data.layerStats\n        : computeStats(filteredGroups.flatMap((g) => g.items))\"\n      :groups=\"filteredGroups\"\n      :active-id=\"activeTool?.id ?? null\"\n      @pick=\"pickTool\"\n    />\n    <McpLandscapeGroupedListView\n      v-else-if=\"data && viewMode === 'list'\"\n      :layer=\"layer\"\n      :groups=\"filteredGroups\"\n      :active-id=\"activeTool?.id ?? null\"\n      @pick=\"pickTool\"\n    />\n  </div>\n\n  <McpLandscapeToolDetailPanel\n    :tool=\"activeTool\"\n    :groups=\"data?.groups ?? []\"\n    @close=\"activeTool = null\"\n  />\n</template>\n"
}

// @vitest-environment nuxt
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest"
import { mountSuspended } from "@nuxt/test-utils/runtime"
import { defineComponent } from "vue"
import Sidebar from "./Sidebar.vue"

const NuxtLinkStub = defineComponent({
  name: "NuxtLink",
  props: { to: { type: String, required: true } },
  template: "<a :href=\"to\"><slot /></a>",
})

const mountOpts = {
  props: { collapsed: false },
  global: { stubs: { NuxtLink: NuxtLinkStub } },
}

describe("Sidebar", () => {
  it("renders Browse + Categories + Collections section headers", async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)
    const html = wrapper.html().toLowerCase()
    expect(html).toContain("browse")
    expect(html).toContain("categor")
    expect(html).toContain("collection")
  })

  it("renders at least 5 browse links targeting /extensions", async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)
    const links = wrapper.findAll("a")
    expect(links.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5)
    for (const link of links) {
      const href = link.attributes("href") ?? ""
      expect(href).toContain("/extensions")
    }
  })

  it("collapsed prop hides the inner content", async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, {
      ...mountOpts,
      props: { collapsed: true },
    })
    expect(wrapper.findAll("a").length).toBe(0)
  })

  it("renders inline color swatches for each func category", async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)
    // happy-dom strips/canonicalizes oklch() in style attributes — check via raw outerHTML
    const styled = wrapper.findAll("span").filter((s) => {
      const styleAttr = s.attributes("style")
      return typeof styleAttr === "string" && styleAttr.length > 0
```

expect(wrapper.find(\"a\").exists()).toBe(false)\n    const span = wrapper.find(\"span[aria-disabled=\\\"true\\\"]\")\n    expect(span.exists()).toBe(true)\n    expect(span.text()).toContain(\"DT\")\n expect(wrapper.find(\"span[aria-disabled=\\\"true\\\"]\").exists()).toBe(true)\n  })\n\n  it(\"shows the deps count badge only when depsCount >= 10\", async () => {\n    const low = makeTool({  props: { tool: low },\n      global: { stubs: { NuxtLink: NuxtLinkStub } },\n    })\n    const highWrap = await mountSuspended(ToolTile, {\n      props: { tool: high },\n      global: { stubs: { })\n})\n"

  "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\"\nimport { rankFor, toolDisplayBlurb, toolDisplayName, type GroupStats, type ToolDto } from \"./mcp-panorama\"\n\nfunction stats(released: number, dev: number, none: number): GroupStats {\n  const total = released + dev + none\n  return {\n    total,\n    counts: { released, dev, none },\n    releasedPct: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((released / total) * 100),\n    activePct: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round(((released + dev) / total) * 100),\n    lagPct: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((none / total) * 100),\n  }\n}\n\ndescribe(\"rankFor\", () => {\n  it(\"returns null for groups with fewer than 3 tools\", () => {\n    expect(rankFor(stats(2, 0, 0))).toBeNull()\n    expect(rankFor(stats(0, 0, 2))).toBeNull()\n  })\n\n  it(\"returns leading at >= 75% released\", () => {\n    expect(rankFor(stats(8, 1, 1))).toBe(\"leading\")\n    expect(rankFor(stats(3, 1, 0))).toBe(\"leading\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"returns onTrack at 50–74% released\", () => {\n    expect(rankFor(stats(5, 5, 0))).toBe(\"onTrack\")\n    expect(rankFor(stats(6, 4, 0))).toBe(\"onTrack\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"returns lagging when >= 50% are no-mcp-needed\", () => {\n    expect(rankFor(stats(0, 1, 4))).toBe(\"lagging\")\n    expect(rankFor(stats(1, 1, 5))).toBe(\"lagging\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"returns early when very low coverage and not lagging\", () => {\n    // 4 tools, 0% released, 1 dev, 3 none → not lagging (3/4=75% none>=50% triggers lagging)\n    // build a case: 4 tools, 0 released, 4 dev → lagPct=0, releasedPct=0 → early\n    expect(rankFor(stats(0, 4, 0))).toBe(\"early\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"returns null in the middling band\", () => {\n    // 4 tools, 1 released, 2 dev, 1 none → 25% released, 25% lag → no rank\n    expect(rankFor(stats(1, 2, 1))).toBeNull()\n  })\n})\n\ndescribe(\"toolDisplayName\", () => {\n  const tool = (over: Partial<ToolDto> = {}): ToolDto => ({\n    id: 1, slug: \"x\", name: \"EN\", nameZh: null, status: \"none\",\n    depsCount: 0, blurb: \"\", blurbZh: \"\", tags: [],\n    extensionSlug: null, ownerPrimary: \"x\", ownerSecondary: null,\n    ...over,\n  })\n\n  it(\"falls back to English when nameZh is null even in zh locale\", () => {\n    expect(toolDisplayName(tool({ name: \"IDE\" }), \"zh\")).toBe(\"IDE\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"uses nameZh in zh locale when set\", () => {\n    expect(toolDisplayName(tool({ name: \"5G-Sim\", nameZh: \"5G 仿真\" }), \"zh\")).toBe(\"5G 仿真\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"uses English in en locale even when nameZh is set\", () => {\n    expect(toolDisplayName(tool({ name: \"5G-Sim\", nameZh: \"5G 仿真\" }), \"en\")).toBe(\"5G-Sim\")\n  })\n})\n\ndescribe(\"toolDisplayBlurb\", () => {\n  const tool: ToolDto = {\n    id: 1, slug: \"x\", name: \"X\", nameZh: null, status: \"none\",\n    depsCount: 0, blurb: \"english\", blurbZh: \"中文\", tags: [],\n    extensionSlug: null, ownerPrimary: \"x\", ownerSecondary: null,\n  }\n\n  it(\"returns english blurb in en locale\", () => {\n    expect(toolDisplayBlurb(tool, \"en\")).toBe(\"english\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"returns chinese blurb in zh locale\", () => {\n    expect(toolDisplayBlurb(tool, \"zh\")).toBe(\"中文\")\n  })\n})\n"
}

All driven by CSS variables defined in\n  the previous commit so dark theme works without per-component branches.\n- Released-status tiles render as <NuxtLink> to /extensions/{slug};\n  dev / no-mcp tiles render as static <span> with aria-disabled. The\n  green tile only links when extensionSlug is non-null (defensive even\n  though seed always provides one).\n- AgentCenter integration: prominent MCP Panorama callout at the top of\n  the Sidebar and a MCP Panorama link in the TopBar nav between Explore\n  and Publish.\n- Shared types + helpers in shared/mcp-panorama.ts (Group, ToolDto,\n  rankFor, locale-aware display helpers) used by both the server query\n  and the client components.\n- Tests: ToolTile (released vs dev/none rendering, deps badge cutoff,\n  null extensionSlug fallback), shared rank/display helpers,\n  deriveStatus / ownerToParts in shared/data. > AGENT

Five clean commits on `feat/mcp-panorama`, validated end-to-end. Summary:

**Schema + data**
- `mcp_sectors`, `mcp_domains`, `mcp_pdts`, `mcp_landscape_tools` + `mcp_layer` enum with CHECK constraints (`status_xor`, `owner_layer`). Migration `0004_loose_elektra.sql`.
- 12 industry sectors, 5 public domains × 29 PDTs, 110 tools, bilingual labels/blurbs in `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts`. Seed adds 54 thin marketplace MCP extension stubs so every green tile lands on a real `/extensions/{slug}`.
- Status is derived (`extensionId` set → released, else `inDev` → dev, else none).

**API**
- `GET /api/internal/mcp-landscape?layer=industry|public` returns layer stats + grouped sectors / domains+PDTs in one round-trip via `server/utils/queries/mcp-landscape.ts`.

**UI**
- `app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue` on a dedicated `mcp-panorama` layout (TopBar + slot, no global Sidebar).
- 14 components under `app/components/mcp-landscape/` (LayerSidebar, SectionHeader, LayerSummary, PanoramaView, SectorCard, DomainCard, CardHeader, PdtBlock, ToolTile, GroupedListView, ToolCard, StatusPill, StatusChip, ToolDetailPanel).
- Released → `<NuxtLink>`; dev / none → static `<span aria-disabled>`. Deps badge shown when `≥ 10`.

**Theme + i18n**
- Status + layer colour tokens in `tailwind.css` with light + dark variants.
- Full `mcpPanorama.*` tree in both `en.json` and `zh.json` (396 keys parity), plus `nav.mcpPanorama` and `sidebar.mcpPanorama.{title,subtitle}`.

**Integration**
- Prominent MCP Panorama callout at the top of AgentCenter's `Sidebar.vue`, MCP Panorama link in `TopBar.vue`.

**Verified**
- `bun run validate` ✓ (lint + typecheck + 233/233 tests pass)
- Migration applied; seed inserted 12 sectors, 5 domains, 29 PDTs, 54 stubs, 110 tools.
- Public layer API: 68 tools, 53% released, 87% MCP-active.
- Industry layer API: 42 tools, 43% released, 76% MCP-active.
- Page renders 36 released-tile `<a>` links + 33 dev/none static spans in SSR; IDE/CodeCheck specifically link to `/en/extensions/{slug}`.
- Chinese page renders sector/domain/PDT/tool labels in Chinese (e.g. 系统设计, 架构框图编辑, MCP 全景).
- AgentCenter `/extensions` page shows the MCP Panorama callout and topbar link.

Ready for PR review when you want it. Per CLAUDE.md I'll wait for explicit ask before `git push` / `gh pr create`.

45	- **No `--amend` once pushed, no force-push, no `--no-verify`.** Corrective commits over history rewrites.
46	- **Breaking changes to `/api/v1`** require `!` after the scope and a `BREAKING CHANGE:` footer.
47	- Always work on a feature branch and open a PR — never commit to `main` directly.
48	- When a binding decision changes, update `docs/plan.md` in the same commit as the code change.
49	
50	## Project structure
51	
52	See `docs/plan.md` §1 for the full tree.
53	
54	- `app/pages/...` — file-based routes (locale-prefixed via `@nuxtjs/i18n`)
55	- `app/components/{ui,layout,extension,filters,publish,profile}` — Vue components
56	- `app/composables/`, `app/middleware/` — client state + route guards
57	- `server/api/v1/...` — public registry API consumed by the CLI (frozen contract)
58	- `server/api/internal/...` — form-backing endpoints (Nuxt's analogue of Next.js server actions)
```

    feat(p22-mcp-panorama): page, components, AgentCenter sidebar entry
    
    - New /[locale]/mcp-panorama page on a dedicated layout (TopBar + slot,
      no default Sidebar) so the page can render its own LayerSidebar with
      state-driven sector/domain/PDT tree.
    - 14 components under app/components/mcp-landscape/: LayerSidebar,
      SectionHeader, LayerSummary, PanoramaView, SectorCard, DomainCard,
      CardHeader, PdtBlock, ToolTile, GroupedListView, ToolCard, StatusPill,
      StatusChip, ToolDetailPanel. All driven by CSS variables defined in
      the previous commit so dark theme works without per-component branches.
    - Released-status tiles render as <NuxtLink> to /extensions/{slug};
      dev / no-mcp tiles render as static <span> with aria-disabled. The
      green tile only links when extensionSlug is non-null (defensive even
      though seed always provides one).
    - AgentCenter integration: prominent MCP Panorama callout at the top of
      the Sidebar and a MCP Panorama link in the TopBar nav between Explore
      and Publish.
    - Shared types + helpers in shared/mcp-panorama.ts (Group, ToolDto,
      rankFor, locale-aware display helpers) used by both the server query
      and the client components.
    - Tests: ToolTile (released vs dev/none rendering, deps badge cutoff,
      null extensionSlug fallback), shared rank/display helpers,
      deriveStatus / ownerToParts in shared/data. Updated Sidebar test to
      allow the new MCP Panorama callout link. 233 / 233 tests pass.

> AGENT

That error is unrelated to my MCP Panorama work — it's the existing `/api/internal/extensions` endpoint, and the failure is at the connection layer:

```
database "neo%20%20ndb" does not exist
```

The DB name in your `DATABASE_URL` literally contains `%20%20` (two URL-encoded spaces between `neo` and `ndb`). Most likely it should be `neondb` and got mangled with stray spaces during a copy/edit. My seed earlier succeeded because the env at that point was clean — something changed.

Quick fix: open `.env` and check the path component of `DATABASE_URL`. The end should be `/neondb` (or whatever your actual db name is), not `/neo%20%20ndb` or `/neo  ndb`. Then restart `bun dev`.

Want me to take a look at `.env` to confirm, or would you rather inspect it yourself? (Happy either way — if it has secrets you'd rather not surface, just paste the database name back to me.)

{"body":"## Summary\n\n- Reshapes the test pyramid into four tiers (pure unit / integration / orchestrator unit / contract / e2e) and updates `docs/plan.md` §11–12 accordingly.\n- Extracts every Drizzle call touching extensions, versions, files, installs, collections into `server/repositories/**`, with `Transactable` (driver-agnostic `PgDatabase<…>`) as the universal first arg. PGlite (Postgres-in-WASM, in-process) backs the integration suite via `tests/integration/helpers/db.ts` — same SQL dialect as production, no Docker.\n- Lifts the personal-vs-org / scan-decision branching out of `server/utils/extensions-state.ts` into pure `shared/extensions/state.ts`; same for install version-selection in `shared/installs/record.ts`. Orchestrators are now thin compositions over repos.\n\n## Why\n\n`docs/plan.md` §11 used to say \"no DB mocking, push to shared/ or Playwright.\" That rule conflated mocking Drizzle's chained builder (rightly banned) with mocking your own repository functions (legitimate). The result: `server/utils/extensions-state.ts` and `installs.ts` had zero unit tests, the publish state machine could only be covered end-to-end via Playwright, and `tests/contract/` was promised but never written. This PR reseats the architecture so each layer has a coherent place to test, with PGlite as the integration substrate.\n\n## What's covered now\n\n| Tier | Tests | Files |\n|---|---|---|\n| Pure unit (`shared/**/*.test.ts`, `app/**.nuxt.test.ts`) | 210 (was 114) | validators, search query, pure decisions, composables (useFilters, usePublishWizard, useTheme), profile validator |\n| Integration (`tests/integration/repositories/*.test.ts` against PGlite) | 44 | extensions, versions, files, installs, collections + harness smoke |\n| Orchestrator unit (`server/utils/*.test.ts` with mocked repos) | 19 | extensions-state, installs |\n| Contract (`tests/contract/*.test.ts` against `docs/api.md`) | 23 | all 6 `/api/v1` endpoints |\n| E2E (existing) | unchanged | Playwright |\n\nCoverage on the gated surface today: **96.37% stmts / 89.77% br / 95.83% fn / 97.29% lines**.\n\n## Commits\n\n13 sequential commits — `docs → harness → repos → orchestrator refactors → pure decisions → unit tests → contract tests → composable tests → threshold ratchet`. Each commit compiles, typechecks, and stands on its own; PR head passes the full validate pipeline plus `bun run test:integration`.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] `bun install --frozen-lockfile`\n- [ ] `bun run validate` — prepare + lint + typecheck + test\n- [ ] `bun run test:integration` — PGlite repo suite (~25s)\n- [ ] `bun run test:coverage` — confirms per-area thresholds pass\n- [ ] Manually smoke the publish flow against a dev DB to verify the orchestrator refactor preserved behavior (personal-scope auto-publish, org/enterprise wait for admin, install row + collection item + downloads increment)\n\n## Out of scope (deliberate)\n\n- Migrate `server/utils/{publish, queries/{facets,profile}, jobs/*}.ts` to repos, then enable the ESLint `drizzle-orm` import restriction.\n- Playwright specs for the refactored publish/install flows.\n- Interactive component coverage (UploadWizard, FilterBar, InstallButton, DeptPicker, TagDrawer).\n- Merge integration-suite lcov into the main coverage report so `server/repositories/**` gets a numeric gate.\n- Tighten `shared/search/**` branches 87 → 90 (filter-chip paths still unexercised).\n\nEach is tracked in the closing section of `docs/plan.md` and ready to land as separate PRs.\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n## Release Notes\n\n* **Tests**\n  * Added integration test framework with PGlite database support\n  * Added API contract tests validating endpoint response shapes\n  * Expanded test coverage for composables and utilities\n\n* **Documentation**\n  * Updated architecture documentation reflecting finalized testing strategy\n\n<!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->"}

Green tiles navigate to the real `/extensions/{slug}` listing; yellow/grey tiles are static (`aria-disabled`).\n- Bilingual end-to-end — every sector / domain / PDT / blurb seeded in both EN and ZH, full `mcpPanorama.*` i18n tree in lockstep.\n\n## Why\n\nUser asked for a panorama view of MCP adoption across internal tools, hand-mocked the design in Claude Design, and asked us to ship it. The design exists at the user's Claude Design URL (also unpacked into the repo's `/tmp/design-bundle/agentcenter/project/` during planning). The user explicitly tied **release status to marketplace listing** so the data model can never drift: when a publisher ships an MCP extension for a tool, that tool flips to released automatically — no second source of truth.\n\n## What's included\n\n| Layer | Files |\n|---|---|\n| Schema + migration | `shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts`, `drizzle/0004_loose_elektra.sql` |\n| Static data + helpers | `app/assets/css/tailwind.css` — status + layer tokens (light + dark) |\n| i18n | `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json` — full `mcpPanorama.*` tree (key parity verified) |\n| AgentCenter integration | `Sidebar.vue` callout, `TopBar.vue` nav link |\n| Tests | `ToolTile.nuxt.test.ts`, `shared/mcp-panorama.test.ts`, `shared/data/mcp-landscape.test.ts` |\n| Docs | `CLAUDE.md` — also adds the new rule forbidding agent-attribution trailers / PR footers |\n\n## Commits\n\nSix sequential commits, each compiles & validates on its own:\n\n1. `feat(p22-mcp-panorama): add mcp landscape schema + migration`\n2. `feat(p22-mcp-panorama): seed taxonomy, tools, and marketplace stubs`\n3. `feat(p22-mcp-panorama): API endpoint, status/layer tokens, and i18n keys`\n4. `feat(p22-mcp-panorama): page, labels\n- [ ] AI R&D → Development Services → confirm `IDE`, `CodeCheck`, `DT` tiles render with correct status colours\n- [ ] Click a green tile (e.g. `IDE`) → lands on `/en/extensions/ide` (the seeded marketplace stub)\n- [ ] Click a yellow / grey tile → no navigation, side panel opens with `Track progress` / `Request MCP build` action\n- [ ] Status filter chips reduce the visible tile Panorama** callout in the AgentCenter sidebar; topbar shows the nav link\n\n## Out of scope (deliberate)\n\n- **Kanban + Heatmap views** — the design also defines these; only Panorama + Grouped List ship in this PR (per the user's scoping decision during planning).\n- **Real adoption %** — the design had a fake `adoptionPct` we dropped; reintroduce as a derived metric over the `installs` table once that has signal.\n- **Publish-flow auto-flip** — the schema supports it (the FK is in place); wiring the publish handler to set `extensionId` on a matching landscape tool is a follow-up.\n- **Tweaks panel** (sort by coverage/lagging, dense/normal density, layer-tinted accent toggle) — the panorama uses a static accent and default sort for v1.\n- **Richer marketplace stubs** — the 54 <!-- This is an auto-generated comment: summarize by coderabbit.ai -->
<!-- walkthrough_start -->

<details>
<summary>📝 Walkthrough</summary>

## Walkthrough

This PR introduces a comprehensive "MCP Panorama" feature enabling browsing and exploration of MCP (Model Context Protocol) tools organized by industry and public layers. It includes a new database schema, server API, seed data, and a full Vue UI with hierarchical navigation, filtering, search, and tool details panels.

## Changes

**MCP Panorama**

|Layer / File(s)|Summary|
|---|---|
|**Shared data model and localization helpers** <br> `shared/mcp-panorama.ts`, `shared/mcp-panorama.test.ts`|Defines core types (Layer, ToolDto, GroupStats, PdtBlock, SectorGroup, DomainGroup, RankKey), rank classification logic rankFor(), and locale-aware helpers for tool/group/PDT display names with Chinese fallback support; includes comprehensive test coverage.|
|**Seed data model and utilities** <br> `shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts`, `shared/data/mcp-landscape.test.ts`|Exports seed-data types (McpStatus, seed interfaces), static datasets (INDUSTRY_SECTORS, PUBLIC_DOMAINS, MCP_TOOLS), and helper functions deriveStatus() and ownerToParts() for status classification and ownership parsing; includes tests for both helpers.|
|**Database schema and migration** <br> `shared/db/schema/mcp-landscape.ts`, `shared/db/schema/index.ts`, `drizzle/0004_loose_elektra.sql`, `drizzle/meta/0004_snapshot.json`, `drizzle/meta/_journal.json`|Defines Drizzle ORM schema (mcpLayerEnum, mcpSectors, mcpDomains, mcpPdts, mcpLandscapeTools) with multilingual support, ownership relationships, XOR/layer-consistency constraints, and performance indexes; creates SQL migration and updates snapshot/journal metadata.|
|**Server API and query logic** <br> `server/api/internal/mcp-landscape.get.ts`, `server/utils/queries/mcp-landscape.ts`|Implements API handler that normalizes layer parameter and calls getLandscape(); implements getLandscape() that queries tools, derives status, computes GroupStats, and builds industry (sector) or public (domain+PDT) hierarchical structures with filtering and aggregation.|
|**Data seeding** <br> `scripts/seed.ts`|Imports MCP seed data and utilities; inserts sector/domain/PDT lookup records; creates extension stubs for released tools; inserts mcpLandscapeTools with ownership decomposition and status fields (extensionId for released, inDev for dev tools).|
|**Utility UI components** <br> `app/components/mcp-landscape/StatusPill.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/StatusChip.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolTile.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolTile.nuxt.test.ts`|Implements StatusPill (status badge with size), StatusChip (clickable filter chip), and ToolTile (tool tile with conditional link/span based on release status, deps badge); includes ToolTile unit test covering released/dev/none rendering and extensionSlug handling.|
|**Container components for cards and blocks** <br> `app/components/mcp-landscape/CardHeader.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectorCard.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/DomainCard.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/PdtBlock.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolCard.vue`|Implements CardHeader (group title, rank badge, status bar, legend), SectorCard (header + tiles), DomainCard (header + PDT blocks), PdtBlock (PDT title + tiles), and ToolCard (tool button with deps and owner); components compose into hierarchical card structures with pick event propagation.|
|**View and layout components** <br> `app/components/mcp-landscape/GroupedListView.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/LayerSidebar.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/LayerSummary.vue`, `app/components/mcp-landscape/PanoramaView.vue`|Implements GroupedListView (flattened list view with status distribution), SectionHeader (breadcrumb/title, view mode toggle, filter chips, search), LayerSidebar (layer selector, hierarchical group tree), LayerSummary (layer stats with top/bottom groups), and PanoramaView (responsive grid of cards); handle drill-down and selection state.|
|**Tool detail panel** <br> `app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolDetailPanel.vue`|Implements slide-out aside panel showing selected tool: header with layer badge, status via StatusPill, metadata grid (dependents, owner, endpoint), downstream tools list (5 max, deterministic), and footer actions (marketplace link for released, track/request buttons, docs).|
|**Main page and layout orchestration** <br> `app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue`, `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue`|Implements mcp-panorama page fetching layer data, managing UI state (layer, drill-down, filter, search, viewMode, tool selection), computing filteredGroups and visibleCounts with applied filters, rendering sidebar/header/content conditionally; layout provides collapsible sidebar and sticky TopBar integration.|
|**Navigation integration** <br> `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue`, `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue`, `app/components/layout/Sidebar.nuxt.test.ts`|Updates Sidebar to add LayoutGrid-icon MCP Panorama callout link; updates TopBar to add nav link with active-class; updates Sidebar test to accept /mcp-panorama route alongside /extensions routes.|
|**Styling and internationalization** <br> `app/assets/css/tailwind.css`, `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json`|Adds Tailwind CSS custom properties for MCP Panorama status (released/dev/none) and layer (industry/public) colors in light/dark modes with background variants; adds comprehensive English and Chinese i18n covering navigation, sidebar, page content, UI strings, and relative-time labels.|
|**Workflow guidelines** <br> `CLAUDE.md`|Adds workflow rule prohibiting agent-attribution trailers and agent-generated footers in commits/PR descriptions, relying on branch authorship instead.|

## Estimated code review effort

🎯 4 (Complex) | ⏱️ ~60 minutes

  "old_string": "// Filter individual tools (drill-down + status + search).\nfunction filterTool(tool: ToolDto): boolean {\n  if (activePrimary.value && tool.ownerPrimary !== activePrimary.value) return false\n  if (activeSecondary.value && tool.ownerSecondary !== activeSecondary.value) return false\n  if (statusFilter.value !== \"all\" && tool.status !== statusFilter.value) return false\n  if (search.value.trim()) {\n    const q = search.value.trim().toLowerCase()\n    const hay =\n      tool.name.toLowerCase()\n      + \" \"\n      + (tool.nameZh ?? \"\").toLowerCase()\n      + \" \"\n      + tool.blurb.toLowerCase()\n      + \" \"\n      + tool.blurbZh.toLowerCase()\n      + \" \"\n      + tool.tags.join(\" \")\n    if (!hay.includes(q)) return false\n  }\n  return true\n}",
  "new_string": "// Search match — single source of truth, used by both filterTool (which\n// drives what's rendered) and visibleCounts (which drives the chip badges).\nfunction matchesSearch(tool: ToolDto): boolean {\n  const q = search.value.trim().toLowerCase()\n  if (!q) return true\n  return (\n    tool.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)\n    || (tool.nameZh ?? \"\").toLowerCase().includes(q)\n    || tool.blurb.toLowerCase().includes(q)\n    || tool.blurbZh.toLowerCase().includes(q)\n    || tool.tags.some((tg) => tg.toLowerCase().includes(q))\n  )\n}\n\n// Filter individual tools (drill-down + status + search).\nfunction filterTool(tool: ToolDto): boolean {\n  if (activePrimary.value && tool.ownerPrimary !== activePrimary.value) return false\n  if (activeSecondary.value && tool.ownerSecondary !== activeSecondary.value) return false\n  if (statusFilter.value !== \"all\" && tool.status !== statusFilter.value) return false\n  return matchesSearch(tool)\n}"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolDetailPanel.vue",
  "old_string": "  if (!props.tool) return []\n  // Deterministic pick from across the groups, capped at min(deps, 5) — purely\n  // illustrative dependency list for the side panel.\n  const all = props.groups.flatMap((g) => g.items).filter((x) => x.id !== props.tool!.id)\n  if (all.length === 0) return []\n  const seed = props.tool.id * 7\n  const n = Math.min(props.tool.depsCount, 5)\n  const out: ToolDto[] = []\n  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n    out.push(all[(seed + i * 13) % all.length]!)\n  }\n  return out\n})",
  "new_string": "  if (!props.tool) return []\n  // Deterministic pick from across the groups, capped at min(deps, 5) — purely\n  // illustrative dependency list for the side panel. Walks the pool linearly\n  // until n distinct tools are collected, so adjacent picks never collide.\n  const all = props.groups.flatMap((g) => g.items).filter((x) => x.id !== props.tool!.id)\n  if (all.length === 0) return []\n  const target = Math.min(props.tool.depsCount, 5, all.length)\n  const out: ToolDto[] = []\n  const seen = new Set<number>()\n  let i = props.tool.id * 7\n  while (out.length < target) {\n    const candidate = all[i % all.length]!\n    if (!seen.has(candidate.id)) {\n      seen.add(candidate.id)\n      out.push(candidate)\n    }\n    i += 13\n  }\n  return out\n})"
}
```

1	# MCP Panorama
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	The user has been working with Claude Design and produced a **panorama / landscape map** (renamed from "MCP Adoption Panorama" → just **"MCP Panorama"** per user direction) of MCP adoption across internal tool services. The bundled design lives at `/tmp/design-bundle/agentcenter/project/MCP Adoption Kanban.html` (+ four `.jsx` partials). Key facts from the design + chat transcripts:
6	
7	- **Goal**: An internal-facing dashboard (lives inside AgentCenter) so leadership and engineers can see, at a glance, which traditional internal tools have shipped an MCP version, which are in development, and which don't need one — and spot leading vs. lagging organisations.
8	- **Two layers**:
9	  - **Industry Services** → 12 sectors (Wireless, Datacom, Cloud, Terminals, Optical, Carrier BG, Enterprise BG, Consumer BG, Digital Energy, Intelligent Auto, Smart City, Industrial). No PDT sub-layer.
10	  - **Public Services** → 5 domains (AI R&D, Product & Software, Hardware, Product Digitization, Infrastructure), each with several PDTs. AI R&D has 6 named PDTs incl. Development Services with `IDE`, `CodeCheck`, `DT`. Product & Software has 8 PDTs.
11	- **Three statuses** colour-coded: `released` (green) · `dev` (amber) · `none` (grey).
12	- **Default view**: **Panorama** (every tool = one tile, grouped into sector / domain-PDT cards, each card has a coverage bar + leader/laggard tag). User explicitly **rejected** the original Kanban-as-default in favour of this landscape.
13	- **User decisions for this iteration**: ship **Panorama + Grouped List** (skip Kanban + Heatmap), back the data with a **Drizzle table + seed** (not a static fixture), wire a sidebar entry from AgentCenter, and make **only `released` (green) tiles clickable** — they navigate to the marketplace MCP detail page.
14	
15	The visual language is a 1:1 match with AgentCenter's existing Editorial Ivory theme (warm cream bg, Fraunces serif headers, Inter body, JetBrains Mono micro-text, accent `oklch(40% 0.09 35)`) — every CSS token in `app/assets/css/tailwind.css` already lines up with the design tokens in `mcp-kanban-ui.jsx`. The new screen needs **two extra tokens** for the layer-tinted accent: industry blue `oklch(48% 0.09 240)` and softened variants.
```

Perfect. Now I have all the information I need. Let me provide a concise report:

## Report: MCP Panorama Link Relocation Analysis

### 1. **Extensions Index Page Structure** (`/extensions/index.vue`)
The page has a clean two-tier layout:
- **Header region** (lines 30-37): Serif title "Browse Extensions" + count summary
- **FilterBar** (line 39-43): Horizontal filter controls (scope pills, dept picker, creator/publisher pickers, tags, chips, sort)
- **ExtGrid** (line 45-49): Card grid that renders either empty state or auto-fill grid of ExtCard components

**Natural insertion point**: Between FilterBar and ExtGrid—a contextual banner could appear here when `category === 'mcp'`, above the grid but below active filters. This follows the visual hierarchy and doesn't disrupt the header.

### 2. **Filter / Category State Detection**
Route parameter handling:
- `useFilters()` composable (lines 10-25) parses `route.query` into a `Filters` object
- **Direct category detection**: Access via `route.query.category` (as done in Sidebar.vue line 39)
- **Sidebar reference** (Sidebar.vue lines 38-44): `activeCategory` computed property validates category against allowed values (`['skills', 'mcp', 'slash', 'plugins']`)
- **In index.vue**: Add `const category = computed(() => route.query.category)` and check `category.value === 'mcp'`

### 3. **ExtCard / ExtGrid & Empty State**
- ExtGrid (lines 16-44): Renders empty state (SearchX icon, contextual message, "Clear Filters" link) when `items.length === 0`
- ExtCard: Generic card renderer—no category-specific variants
- **No intro components**: The page has no hero banner or category-intro block; FilterBar is the only region above grid content

### 4. **Existing Precedent for Category Conditionals**
**Zero precedent found**: The codebase has ONE location that checks category state—the Sidebar's `activeCategory` computed (lines 38-44). No pages or components use category-conditional rendering (no `v-if="category === 'mcp'"` patterns).

**Design implication**: You'll be establishing a new pattern. The sidebar callout (lines 94-110) is purely location-based, not reactive to category state. Moving the link to the extensions page means creating the first category-conditional CTA.

### 5. **Sidebar BROWSE_ITEMS Row Structure**
Each row renders as:
```vue
<NuxtLink>
  <component :is="item.Icon" :size="14" class="shrink-0" />
  <span class="flex-1 truncate">{{ t(item.labelKey) }}</span>
</NuxtLink>
```

**Class chain**: `flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition`
- **Active state**: `bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent) font-semibold`
- **Hover**: `hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)`

**Sub-link feasibility**: Adding an indented row beneath "MCP Servers" is structurally possible—the list is a flex column with `gap-px`. You could insert a nested link with `ml-2` (2 units left margin) and smaller text (`text-[11px]`), matching the Categories section's l1 nesting pattern (lines 185-223). This wouldn't break rhythm; it mirrors the existing taxonomy nesting UX.

---

**Summary**: The extensions index page is insertion-friendly between FilterBar and ExtGrid. The sidebar row can accommodate a sub-link without design strain. No category-conditional banners exist yet, so you'll establish a new pattern—consider whether to keep the topbar link alongside a new index-page CTA, or consolidate.
```

> AGENT

I have enough context to think through it. My take on your concern, then a question before I plan.

**Your instinct is right.** Three problems with the current callout:

1. **Always-on, only sometimes relevant.** A user browsing Slash Commands or Plugins sees a heavy MCP Panorama callout that has nothing to do with their context.
2. **Visual weight mismatch.** Bordered + accent-tinted at the top of the sidebar implies "this is a peer of *all browsing*" — but conceptually the panorama is a *related view of MCP-category content*.
3. **Topbar already has it.** The global `MCP Panorama` link in the TopBar is the right place for ambient discovery. The sidebar callout duplicates that, just louder.

Moving it closer to the MCP context is the right move. Four reasonable shapes — let me check which you prefer.

## Approved Plan:
# Relocate the MCP Panorama entry-point

## Context

PR #17 shipped the MCP Panorama page with two entry points:
1. **TopBar nav link** (`MCP Panorama` between `Explore` and `Publish`) — global, ambient, correct.
2. **Prominent callout at the top of the global left Sidebar** — accent-tinted block with `LayoutGrid` icon and a "Service landscape →" subtitle.

The sidebar callout is the wrong shape:
- It is *always on* — visible to a user browsing Slash Commands or Plugins, where it is irrelevant.
- Its visual weight implies *peer-of-all-browsing*, but conceptually the panorama is a **related view of MCP-category content**, not a top-level navigation.
- It duplicates the TopBar entry without adding contextual value.

Per the user's choice, replace the sidebar callout with a **contextual banner** on the `/extensions` browse page that renders **only when `?category=mcp` is active**. Discovery flow becomes:

> User clicks "MCP Servers" in the sidebar → page filters to MCP extensions → a banner above the grid offers "Looking for the bigger picture? View the MCP Panorama →".

The TopBar link stays as the always-visible entry.

## Approach

### 1 · Remove the sidebar callout
- `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue`: delete the `MCP Panorama callout` block (the `<NuxtLink>` at lines 94-110) and drop the now-unused `LayoutGrid` import.
- No other Sidebar logic is affected.

### 2 · New banner component
- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.vue` — small self-contained component (matches `app/components/extension/` co-location). Renders only when invoked.
- Visual: same accent-tinted treatment the old callout used (`bg-(--color-accent)/8`, `border-(--color-accent)/25`, `LayoutGrid` icon, accent-coloured title), but full-width above the grid with a clear CTA chevron.
- Pure presentational — no internal `category` check; the page decides whether to render it.

### 3 · Wire the banner into the extensions page
- `app/pages/extensions/index.vue`: insert `<McpPanoramaBanner v-if="isMcpCategory" />` between the existing `<FilterBar>` (line 39-43) and `<ExtGrid>` (line 45-49) — the natural insertion point identified during exploration.
- Add a `const isMcpCategory = computed(() => route.query.category === 'mcp')` in `<script setup>`. `route` is already in scope (line 3).

### 4 · i18n: relocate the strings
- Keep `nav.mcpPanorama` (TopBar link) — unchanged.
- **Remove** `sidebar.mcpPanorama.{title,subtitle}` from both `en.json` and `zh.json` — those keys belonged to the deleted callout.
- **Add** `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.{title,subtitle,cta}` in lock-step:
  - `title` — "MCP Panorama" / "MCP 全景图"
  - `subtitle` — short descriptor ("See the full MCP adoption landscape across our internal tools." / "查看内部工具 MCP 化的全景图。")
  - `cta` — "View Panorama →" / "查看全景图 →"

Both files updated in the same commit; key parity verified via the same `node -e` walk used in the original PR.

### 5 · Tests
- **`app/components/layout/Sidebar.nuxt.test.ts`**: tighten the "renders at least 5 browse links targeting /extensions" assertion — revert the `/mcp-panorama` exception added in commit `dd44146` since the link is gone. The check goes back to `expect(href).toContain("/extensions")` for all anchors.
- **New `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.nuxt.test.ts`**: minimal — verify it renders, the link points to `localePath('/mcp-panorama')`, and the title/cta i18n keys resolve. Mirrors the existing `ToolTile.nuxt.test.ts` pattern (NuxtLink stub, `mountSuspended`).

### 6 · Verify
- `bun run validate` (lint + typecheck + 233 unit tests) stays green; the Sidebar test count stays the same (4 tests), banner test adds 3.
- `bun dev`, then:
  - Visit `/en/extensions` (no category) → **no banner**, no sidebar callout.
  - Visit `/en/extensions?category=mcp` (or click "MCP Servers" in the sidebar) → **banner appears** above the grid; clicking it navigates to `/en/mcp-panorama`.
  - Visit `/en/extensions?category=skills` → no banner.
  - Locale switch to `/zh/extensions?category=mcp` → banner renders in Chinese.
  - TopBar `MCP Panorama` link works as before from any page.

## Critical files

**Modified**
- `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` — delete callout block + `LayoutGrid` import.
- `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — add `isMcpCategory` computed + `<McpPanoramaBanner v-if="isMcpCategory" />`.
- `app/components/layout/Sidebar.nuxt.test.ts` — tighten link assertion.
- `i18n/locales/en.json` + `i18n/locales/zh.json` — drop `sidebar.mcpPanorama.*`, add `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.{title,subtitle,cta}`.

**New**
- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.vue` — banner UI.
- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.nuxt.test.ts` — render + href + i18n smoke test.

## Out of scope

- Any change to the TopBar `MCP Panorama` nav link (stays exactly where it is).
- Any change to the panorama page itself or its data flow.
- Establishing a generic "category-conditional banner" pattern. This is a one-off CTA today; if a Skills landscape ever appears we can generalise then.

## Commit shape

One PR, one commit body — small enough that splitting adds no clarity:
`feat(p23-mcp-entry): contextual MCP Panorama banner on /extensions, drop sidebar callout`

  "content": "// @vitest-environment nuxt\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\"\nimport { mountSuspended } from \"@nuxt/test-utils/runtime\"\nimport { defineComponent } from \"vue\"\nimport McpPanoramaBanner from \"./McpPanoramaBanner.vue\"\n\nconst NuxtLinkStub = defineComponent({\n  name: \"NuxtLink\",\n  props: { to: { type: String, required: true } },\n  template: \"<a :href=\\\"to\\\"><slot /></a>\",\n})\n\nconst mountOpts = { global: { stubs: { NuxtLink: NuxtLinkStub } } }\n\ndescribe(\"McpPanoramaBanner\", () => {\n  it(\"renders an <a> linking to the locale-prefixed panorama route\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(McpPanoramaBanner, mountOpts)\n    const link = wrapper.find(\"a\")\n    expect(link.exists()).toBe(true)\n    expect(link.attributes(\"href\")).toContain(\"/mcp-panorama\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"renders the title, subtitle, and CTA strings\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(McpPanoramaBanner, mountOpts)\n    const text = wrapper.text()\n    // i18n keys are loaded from i18n/locales/en.json — assert the EN strings appear.\n    expect(text).toContain(\"MCP Panorama\")\n    expect(text.toLowerCase()).toContain(\"mcp\")\n    expect(text).toContain(\"Panorama\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"renders the LayoutGrid icon and chevron\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(McpPanoramaBanner, mountOpts)\n    expect(wrapper.find(\"svg.lucide-layout-grid\").exists()).toBe(true)\n    expect(wrapper.find(\"svg.lucide-chevron-right\").exists()).toBe(true)\n  })\n})\n"
}

{
  "command": "gh pr create --base main --head feat/mcp-panorama-entry --title \"feat(p23-mcp-entry): contextual MCP Panorama banner on /extensions\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\n- Drop the prominent **MCP Panorama callout** from the global left sidebar — it was always-on, visually heavy, and contextually irrelevant for users browsing Skills / Slash Commands / Plugins.\n- Add a **contextual banner** on `/extensions` that renders **only when `?category=mcp` is active**. Discovery flow becomes: click \"MCP Servers\" in the sidebar → page filters to MCP extensions → banner above the grid offers \"View Panorama →\".\n- TopBar `MCP Panorama` link is unchanged — it remains the always-visible entry.\n\n## Why\n\nPR #17 shipped two entry points for the panorama. The TopBar nav was the right one. The sidebar callout duplicated it with more visual weight, while implying the panorama was a peer of all browsing rather than what it actually is — a related view of MCP-category content. User flagged it as \"weird\"; we're moving the secondary entry next to the MCP nav it actually relates to.\n\n## Files\n\n| Change | File |\n|---|---|\n| Drop callout block + `LayoutGrid` import | `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` |\n| New banner component | `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.vue` |\n| Wire banner into the page (guarded by category) | `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` |\n| Drop `sidebar.mcpPanorama.*`, add `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.{title,subtitle,cta}` (en + zh, parity 397/397) | `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json` |\n| Tighten Sidebar link assertion (revert PR #17 relaxation) | `app/components/layout/Sidebar.nuxt.test.ts` |\n| New banner render / href / icon tests | `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.nuxt.test.ts` |\n\nTest count: **236 → 236** (Sidebar still 4, banner adds 3, sidebar callout test removed = net +0).\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] `bun install --frozen-lockfile`\n- [ ] `bun run validate` — prepare + lint + typecheck + 236 tests\n- [ ] `bun dev`, then:\n  - `/en/extensions` (no category) → no banner; sidebar has no callout\n  - Click \"MCP Servers\" in the sidebar (or visit `/en/extensions?category=mcp`) → banner appears above the grid\n  - Click the banner → lands on `/en/mcp-panorama`\n  - `/en/extensions?category=skills` → no banner\n  - `/zh/extensions?category=mcp` → banner renders in Chinese\n  - TopBar `MCP Panorama` link works as before from any page\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Any change to the panorama page itself or its data flow.\n- Generalising the category-conditional banner pattern — this is a one-off; if a Skills / Plugins landscape is ever added, generalise then.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Open PR"
}

# Deployment

AgentCenter (Nuxt) ships as a standalone Node server, with Postgres + an object store + Inngest behind it. Two deploy targets are supported:

- **Node** (default) — `nitro: { preset: "node-server" }`. Runs anywhere Node 22+ runs: bare metal, container, PaaS.
- **Cloudflare** (stretch) — `nitro: { preset: "cloudflare_module" }`. Workers + R2 binding. Not maintained alongside primary; expect bundle-size + driver tweaks.

This guide covers the Node path. The Cloudflare path is a config flip plus a storage driver swap.

## Prerequisites

| Service | Purpose | Required |
|---|---|---|
| **PostgreSQL 15+** | Application database | yes |
| **Object storage** — Supabase Storage **or** Cloudflare R2 | Extension bundles | yes |
| **Inngest** account (cloud) or local dev server | Background jobs (scan, index) | yes (cloud for prod) |
| **Node 22+** | Runtime | yes |
| **Bun 1.3.12** | Build (production runs Node, not Bun) | yes during build |
--
```bash
bun install
DATABASE_URL="postgresql://..." bun run db:migrate
DATABASE_URL="postgresql://..." bun run db:apply-fts
DATABASE_URL="postgresql://..." bun run db:seed   # optional: 16 sample extensions
```

The `db:apply-fts` step requires `psql` on PATH. It runs `drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql` against your DB (FTS column + trgm indexes).

## 2. Provision object storage

### Supabase Storage (default)

1. Supabase dashboard → Storage → New bucket (e.g. `agentcenter-bundles`)
2. Generate a service-role key (Settings → API → service_role secret)
3. Set env: `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `SUPABASE_BUCKET`

### Cloudflare R2 (alternative)

1. Cloudflare dashboard → R2 → Create bucket
--

1. Create an Inngest app (cloud) and copy `INNGEST_EVENT_KEY` + `INNGEST_SIGNING_KEY`
2. After deploying, register the webhook URL in the Inngest dashboard:
   ```text
   https://yourdomain.com/api/inngest
   ```
3. Inngest will verify by calling the endpoint; `scan-bundle` and `reindex-search` will appear as registered functions

For local dev, run the Inngest dev server instead:
```bash
bunx inngest-cli@1.19.2 dev
```
It auto-discovers `/api/inngest` and routes events locally. No event/signing keys needed locally.

## 4. Build & run

```bash
bun install --frozen-lockfile
--
3. Hot-swap the Node process behind the reverse proxy (PM2, systemd, blue/green)
```

> AGENT

The preview returns 401 (Vercel password-protection, can't probe from outside). But here's the answer:

**PR #18 itself adds no new schema** — it's UI/i18n only. Two `.vue` files, two test files, i18n key shuffle. No migration needed for *this* PR to deploy.

**However — PR #17 (already merged) added 4 new tables** (`mcp_sectors`, `mcp_domains`, `mcp_pdts`, `mcp_landscape_tools`) plus 5 new ext rows. If the Vercel-hosted DB hasn't been migrated since PR #17 merged, then:

- `/extensions?category=mcp` will render fine (the new banner needs no DB).
- Clicking the banner → `/mcp-panorama` will **500** (server query joins against tables that don't exist).
- `bun run validate` doesn't catch this — it's tests-against-PGlite, not against prod.

The deploy story per `docs/deploy.md` is **manual migration** — `vercel.json` doesn't exist, no auto-migrate hook, no `postdeploy` script. So someone needs to run, against the Vercel DATABASE_URL:

```bash
DATABASE_URL="postgresql://…vercel-prod-or-preview-db…" bun run db:migrate
DATABASE_URL="postgresql://…" bun run scripts/seed.ts   # only if you want the 110 demo tools visible
```

Quick way to confirm whether the Vercel DB is already current: open the preview URL in a logged-in browser and visit `/en/mcp-panorama`. If it loads → already migrated. If it 500s → needs migration.

Want me to do anything? Two reasonable next steps:

1. **Add an auto-migrate step to the Vercel build** — a small `package.json` change so `vercel build` runs `drizzle-kit migrate` first. Removes the "did anyone migrate?" footgun from every future PR. Needs `DATABASE_URL` available at build time on Vercel.
2. **Just run the migration manually now** — you give me the prod/preview `DATABASE_URL` (or do it yourself), and we're done. Lower risk, but the same problem will resurface next time we add a table.

#1 is the right long-term fix; #2 unblocks the panorama in this preview today.

1	import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs"
2	import { resolve } from "node:path"
3	
4	import { defineConfig } from "drizzle-kit"
5	
6	const envPath = resolve(".env")
7	if (existsSync(envPath)) {
8	  const content = readFileSync(envPath, "utf-8")
9	  for (const line of content.split("\n")) {
10	    const match = line.match(/^([A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*)=(.*)$/)
11	    if (match && !process.env[match[1]]) {
12	      process.env[match[1]] = match[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "")
13	    }
14	  }
15	}
16	
17	const databaseUrl = process.env.DATABASE_URL
18	if (!databaseUrl) {
19	  throw new Error(
20	    "Missing DATABASE_URL env var required for Drizzle Kit. " +
21	      "Set it in .env or your shell before running db:* scripts.",
22	  )
23	}

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --base main --head chore/vercel-auto-migrate --title \"chore(deploy): auto-run drizzle migrations on Vercel build\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nVercel was deploying code without running pending Drizzle migrations — so any PR that adds a table (PR #17 added 4) leaves the Vercel-hosted DB stale until someone runs \\`db:migrate\\` against the production DATABASE_URL by hand.\n\nThis adds a \\`vercel-build\\` script (Vercel's standard build-override hook) that chains \\`drizzle-kit migrate && nuxt build\\`. Every Vercel deploy now migrates the DB before producing build output. Local \\`bun run build\\` is unchanged.\n\n## Why now\n\nPR #17 (MCP Panorama) shipped 4 new tables. The Vercel preview for PR #18 was likely 500ing on \\`/mcp-panorama\\` because the preview's DB hadn't been migrated. Manual \\`bun run db:migrate\\` against the production URL each time isn't a reasonable footgun to keep.\n\n## Why this approach\n\n- **\\`vercel-build\\`** is recognized by Vercel automatically when defined; no \\`vercel.json\\` needed.\n- **\\`drizzle-kit migrate\\`** is idempotent — already-applied migrations are skipped via the \\`__drizzle_migrations\\` table, so re-running on every deploy is safe.\n- **\\`&&\\` chain** — if the migration fails, the build aborts. No code ships against a stale schema.\n- **Devs unaffected** — local \\`bun run build\\` still routes through \\`build\\`, not \\`vercel-build\\`.\n\n## Operator requirements\n\n- \\`DATABASE_URL\\` must be available at **Build** time (and Runtime) in the Vercel project. Vercel exposes both by default unless the env var is marked Plain Text → Runtime-only. Verify in Project Settings → Environment Variables before merging.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] \\`bun run validate\\` ✅ (236 tests pass; no code changes that could affect tests)\n- [ ] After merge, watch the next Vercel deploy log → confirm the \\`drizzle-kit migrate\\` step runs (look for \\`migrations applied successfully!\\` or \\`No migrations needed\\` in the build output)\n- [ ] After merge, hit \\`/en/mcp-panorama\\` on the production URL → confirms the panorama tables exist on the Vercel-hosted DB\n\n## Caveats\n\n- **Destructive migrations** (DROP COLUMN, etc.) will run before the previous deployment's still-running instances stop. Standard auto-migrate caveat — prefer additive migrations or split destructive changes into multi-step sequences (deploy 1 stops writing to col, deploy 2 drops it). Not a problem for any migration we have today.\n- **\\`db:apply-fts\\`** (the manual \\`psql\\` step for the FTS column) is intentionally NOT in the auto-build — it requires \\`psql\\` on PATH and is a one-shot setup, not a per-deploy step. Documented separately in \\`docs/deploy.md\\`.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -3",
  "description": "Open PR"
}
```

> DEVELOPER

the vercel is ok now, but the data of panorama is empty, can we seeds on vercel build? or it need to do it manual?

> AGENT

Good question. Honest answer: **yes we can, but not by adding the current `db:seed` to the build** — that script does `TRUNCATE TABLE organizations, tags RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE`, which would nuke every signed-up user, extension, install, and collection on every deploy. Running it on a populated prod DB once would be a disaster.

The right shape is a **separate, idempotent MCP-landscape seed**:

- Only touches `mcp_sectors`, `mcp_domains`, `mcp_pdts`, `mcp_landscape_tools`, plus the ~50 `extensions` stubs the panorama links to.
- Uses `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` (Drizzle's `onConflictDoUpdate`) instead of TRUNCATE — safe to re-run.
- Adds a minimal `system` org row (FK target for the stubs) via the same upsert pattern.
- Chains into `vercel-build` after `drizzle-kit migrate`, before `nuxt build`.

That makes the static landscape the source of truth (consistent with `docs/plan.md` §11 "landscape is seed-managed in v1"). The destructive `scripts/seed.ts` stays as the one-time demo-data setup.

**Trade-offs to flag:**
- Each Vercel build runs ~150 upserts before the Nuxt build. Adds ~1-2s. Idempotent so safe across parallel deploys.
- If you ever add an admin UI that lets you *edit* a landscape tool, the next deploy would clobber that edit (the seed re-asserts the static value). Today that's fine — plan explicitly defers admin edit; revisit then.

Want me to build it as PR #20? Or would you rather run the existing destructive seed against the Vercel DB once by hand (faster, but you'd need to do it again if the Vercel DB ever gets reset)?

> DEVELOPER

I think we can merge PR #18 first, what do you think?

> AGENT

Agreed — PR #18 is functionally independent of the seed:
- It's UI/i18n only; doesn't touch the DB at all.
- The banner just appears on `/extensions?category=mcp` and links to `/mcp-panorama`. Whether the target page has data is orthogonal — the link works either way; users just see an empty landscape until #20 lands.
- GitHub's merge commit will incorporate PR #19's `vercel-build` script automatically, so post-merge main is fully consistent.

No reason to gate it on the seed work. Merging now.

335	  await db.insert(mcpLandscapeTools).values(toolRows)
336	
337	  console.log("seed: done")
338	  await client.end()
339	}
340	
341	main()
342	  .then(() => process.exit(0))
343	  .catch((err) => {
344	    console.error("seed: failed")
345	    console.error(err)
346	    process.exit(1)

Re-runnable; never destructive.\n */\nexport async function seedMcpLandscape(db: Db): Promise<void> {\n  // Owner org for the marketplace extension stubs. We upsert by id so the\n  // demo seed's `default` org (when present) is preserved verbatim, and a\n  // fresh deploy can stand the row up on its own.\n  await db\n    .insert(organizations)\n    .values({\n      id: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n      slug: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n      name: \"Default Organization\",\n      nameZh: \"默认组织\",\n    })\n    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: organizations.id })\n\n  // upserting ${sectorRows.length} sectors`)\n  await db\n    .insert(mcpSectors)\n    .values(sectorRows)\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: mcpSectors.key,\n      set: {\n        label: sql`excluded.label_zh`,\n        short: sql`excluded.short`,\n        sortOrder: sql`excluded.sort_order`,\n      },\n    })\n\n  // PDTs.\n  const pdtRows = PUBLIC_DOMAINS.flatMap((d) =>\n    d.pdts.map((p, i) => ({\n      key: `${d.key}.${p.key}`,\n      domainKey: d.key,\n      label: p.label,\n      labelZh: p.labelZh,\n      sortOrder: i,\n    })),\n  )\n  console.log(`seed-mcp: upserting ${pdtRows.length} PDTs`)\n  await db\n    .insert(mcpPdts)\n    .values(pdtRows)\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: mcpPdts.key,\n      set: {\n        domainKey: extension stubs — one per released tool. `publisherUserId`\n  // is left null on auto-seed so we don't require the demo creator rows; the\n  // demo seed can override these afterwards if it runs.\n  const releasedTools = MCP_TOOLS.filter((t) => t.released)\n  const mcpExtRows = releasedTools.map((t) => ({\n    id: `mcp-${t.slug}`,\n    slug: t.slug,\n    category: \"mcp\" as const,\n    badge: null,\n    scope: \"enterprise\" as const,\n    funcCat: null,\n    subCat: null,\n    publisherUserId: null,\n    ownerOrgId: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n    deptId: null,\n    iconEmoji: null,\n    iconColor: null,\n    visibility: \"published\" as const,\n    name: t.name,\n    nameZh: t.nameZh ?? null,\n    tagline: t.blurb,\n    taglineZh: t.blurbZh,\n    description: t.blurb,\n    descriptionZh: Date(),\n  }))\n  console.log(`seed-mcp: upserting ${mcpExtRows.length} marketplace stubs`)\n  await db\n    .insert(extensions)\n    .values(mcpExtRows)\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: extensions.id,\n      set: {\n        slug: sql`excluded.slug`,\n        category: sql`excluded.category`,\n        scope: sql`excluded.scope`,\n        ownerOrgId: sql`excluded.owner_org_id`,\n        visibility: sql`excluded.visibility`,\n        name: sql`excluded.name`,\n        nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,\n        tagline: sql`excluded.tagline`,\n        taglineZh: sql`excluded.tagline_zh`,\n        description: sql`excluded.description`,\n        descriptionZh: sql`excluded.description_zh`,\n        readmeMd: sql`excluded.readme_md`,\n        updatedAt:  .values(toolRows)\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: mcpLandscapeTools.slug,\n      set: {\n        name: sql`excluded.name`,\n        nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,\n        layer: sql`excluded.layer`,\n        ownerSector: sql`excluded.owner_sector`,\n        ownerDomain: sql`excluded.owner_domain`,\n        ownerPdt: sql`excluded.owner_pdt`,\n        extensionId: sql`excluded.extension_id`,\n        inDev: sql`excluded.in_dev`,\n        depsCount: sql`excluded.deps_count`,\n        blurb: sql`excluded.blurb`,\n        blurbZh: sql`excluded.blurb_zh`,\n        tags: sql`excluded.tags`,\n        updatedAt: sql`now()`,\n      },\n    })\n}\n\n// ─── Standalone entry-point: `bun scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts` ──────────────\nimport { sql } from \"drizzle-orm\"\n\nasync function main() {\n  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL\n  if (!url) {\n    console.error(\"seed-mcp: DATABASE_URL is not set\")\n    process.exit(1)\n  }\n  const client = postgres(url)\n  const db = drizzle(client, { schema, casing: \"snake_case\" })\n\n  console.log(\"seed-mcp: starting\")\n  await seedMcpLandscape(db)\n  console.log(\"seed-mcp: done\")\n  await client.end()\n}\n\n// Bun-friendly check for \"is this the entry script?\" — true when invoked\n// directly via `bun scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts`, false when imported.\n// `Bun.main` and `import.meta.url` both work; pick the standard one.\nconst isEntry = import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`\nif (isEntry) {\n  main()\n    .then(() => process.exit(0))\n    .catch((err) => {

The `vercel-build` script in `package.json` chains `drizzle-kit migrate && nuxt build`, so every deploy applies pending migrations against `DATABASE_URL` before building. Idempotent against an already-migrated DB; aborts the deploy on migration failure (prevents code shipping against a stale schema).",
  "new_string": "- **Vercel** (auto-detected) — Nitro picks `preset: \"vercel\"` when `process.env.VERCEL` is set. The `vercel-build` script in `package.json` chains `drizzle-kit migrate && bun scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts && nuxt build`, so every deploy: (1) applies pending migrations against `DATABASE_URL`, then (2) upserts the static MCP panorama landscape (taxonomy + tool rows + marketplace stubs) idempotently, then (3) builds. Aborts the deploy if either step fails (prevents code shipping against a stale or empty schema). The destructive `db:seed` script (demo users + sample extensions) is **not** in the build path — that stays a one-shot manual operation."
}

Aborts on any failure; no half-deployed states.\n- **`scripts/seed.ts` (demo) refactored** to import + delegate to `seedMcpLandscape` instead of duplicating the upsert logic. ~100 lines removed. The demo seed still TRUNCATEs and reseeds demo data; it's just unchanged in spirit and now reuses one source of truth for the landscape.\n- **`db:seed:mcp` npm-script** added as the operator shortcut for manual runs.\n\n## Verified locally\n\n```\n=== run 1 ===                            === run 2 ===\nseed-mcp: upserting 12 sectors           seed-mcp: upserting 12 sectors\nseed-mcp: upserting 5 domains            seed-mcp: upserting 5 domains\nseed-mcp: upserting 29 PDTs              seed-mcp: upserting 29 PDTs\nseed-mcp: upserting 54 marketplace stubs seed-mcp: upserting 54 marketplace stubs\nseed-mcp: upserting 110 landscape tools  seed-mcp: upserting 110 landscape tools\nseed-mcp: done                           seed-mcp: done\n```\n\nRow counts stable across runs (12 / 5 / 29 / 110 / 54). [{"body":"## Summary\n\nVercel was deploying code without running pending Drizzle migrations — so any PR that adds a table (PR #17 added 4) leaves the Vercel-hosted DB stale until someone runs \\`db:migrate\\` against the production DATABASE_URL by hand.\n\nThis adds a \\`vercel-build\\` script (Vercel's standard build-override hook) that chains \\`drizzle-kit migrate && nuxt build\\`. Every Vercel deploy now migrates the DB before producing build output. Local \\`bun run build\\` is unchanged.\n\n## Why now\n\nPR #17 (MCP Panorama) shipped 4 new tables. The Vercel preview for PR #18 was likely 500ing on \\`/mcp-panorama\\` because the preview's DB hadn't been migrated. Manual \\`bun run db:migrate\\` against the production URL each time isn't a reasonable footgun to keep.\n\n## Why this approach\n\n- **\\`vercel-build\\`** is recognized by Vercel automatically when defined; no \\`vercel.json\\` needed.\n- **\\`drizzle-kit migrate\\`** is idempotent — already-applied migrations are skipped via the \\`__drizzle_migrations\\` table, so re-running on every deploy is safe.\n- **\\`&&\\` chain** — if the migration fails, the build aborts. No code ships against a stale schema.\n- **Devs unaffected** — local \\`bun run build\\` still routes through \\`build\\`, not \\`vercel-build\\`.\n\n## Operator requirements\n\n- \\`DATABASE_URL\\` must be available at **Build** time (and Runtime) in the Vercel project. Vercel exposes both by default unless the env var is marked Plain Text → Runtime-only. Verify in Project Settings → Environment Variables before merging.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] \\`bun run validate\\` ✅ (236 tests pass; no code changes that could affect tests)\n- [ ] After merge, watch the next Vercel deploy log → confirm the \\`drizzle-kit migrate\\` step runs (look for \\`migrations applied successfully!\\` or \\`No migrations needed\\` in the build output)\n- [ ] After merge, hit \\`/en/mcp-panorama\\` on the production URL → confirms the panorama tables exist on the Vercel-hosted DB\n\n## Caveats\n\n- **Destructive migrations** (DROP COLUMN, etc.) will run before the previous deployment's still-running instances stop. Standard auto-migrate caveat — prefer additive migrations or split destructive changes into multi-step sequences (deploy 1 stops writing to col, deploy 2 drops it). Not a problem for any migration we have today.\n- **\\`db:apply-fts\\`** (the manual \\`psql\\` step for the FTS column) is intentionally NOT in the auto-build — it requires \\`psql\\` on PATH and is a one-shot setup, not a per-deploy step.
